Explanatory Remarks:

1. Structural elements are enclosed in <>.

Text:

<1>
|| bhārgavatantram ||
|| prathamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
< bhārgavatantrasya prayojanapratipādanādhyāyaḥ >
< maṅgalācaraṇam >
jñānānandamayaṃ devaṃ nirmalasphaṭikākṛtim|
ādhāraṃ sarvavidyānāṃ hayagrīvamupāsmahe || 1.1 ||
< prāstāvikam >
agastyaṃ muniśārdūlaṃ mahendraśikhare sthitam|
praṇasya prayatāḥ bhūtvā papracchurmunipuṃgavāḥ || 1.2 ||
munayaḥ
bhagavan muniśārdūla sarvaśāstraviśārada|
kathamadya vayaṃ sarve tīrṇāssama bhavasāgarāt || 1.3 ||
adhītānyapi śāstrāṇi samadhītā trayī punaḥ|
tathāpi nirmalaṃ naiva mano'smākaṃ munīśvara || 1.4 ||
kathaṃ vayaṃ samuttīrṇā bhaviṣyāmo bhavāmbudheḥ|
upāyamadya no brūhi yena yāmo'kṣayaṃ padam || 1.5 ||
etacchrutvā mahātejāḥ svayaṃ svāyaṃbhuvo muniḥ|
ṛṣibhyo vaktumārebhe svadhītaṃ ca svanuṣṭhitam || 1.6 ||
agastyaḥ
purā khalu mayā rakṣo jīrṇitaṃ śrāddhabhojane|
vātāpirilvalaḥ paścāt kupitaḥ so'pi māritaḥ || 1.7 ||
aho rakṣodvayaṃ naṣṭaṃ mayā khalu tapasyatā|
tābhyāṃ naṣṭaṃ tapo'smākaṃ kiṃ kurma iti cintayan || 1.8 ||
himavantamupāsādya devagandharvasevitam|
visarpigāṅgasalilaṃ siddhavidyādharārcitam || 1.9 ||
śaṅkarajyotiṣāśliṣṭaṃ pārvatītoṣakāriṇam|
nārāyaṇamahāyogicaraṇāmbujamudritam || 1.10 ||
puṇyagandhavahopetaṃ puruhūtādivanditam|
puratrayopasaṃhāre cāparūpadharaṃ param || 1.11 ||
phalapuṣpabharānamravṛkṣaṣaṇḍasamākulam|
anekāśramasaṃkīrṇamamalaṃ harimandiram || 1.12 ||
<himālaye agastyatapaścaryā ākāśavāṇīśravaṇaṃ ca>
atyugraṃ tapa atiṣṭhaṃ nirāhāro jitendriyaḥ|
vardhamāne tadā ghore tapasyaghahare'dbhute || 1.13 ||
ākāśavāṇīmaśrauṣamatigambhīravādinīm|
svalpākṣarāṃ ca bahvarthāṃ pariṇāmasukhāvahām || 1.14 ||
<hareḥ ārādhanamahattvaṃ, muneḥ mahendragirigamanāya nirdeśaśca>
ārādhito yadi haristapasā kiṃ prayojanam|
nārādhito yadi haristapasā kiṃ prayojanam || 1.15 ||
antarbahiryadi haristapasā kiṃ prayojanam|
nāntarbahiryadi haristapasā kiṃ prayojanam || 1.16 ||
tapaso virama brahman marhendraṃ vraja parvatam|
tatra rāmaṃ samāsādya tasmāt sukhamavāpnuhi || 1.17 ||
<agastyasya mahendragirigamanam>
etacchrutvāmbarādvāṇīṃ manaḥśrutisukhapradām|
viramya tapaso bhūyo dakṣiṇāṃ diśamabhyagām || 1.18 ||
tato mahendramāsādya puṇyagandharvasevitam|
rāmaṃ kamalapatrākṣamadrākṣamṛṣibhiḥ saha || 1.19 ||
<paraśurāmasya varṇanam>
taptacāmīkaranibhaṃ piṅgaśmaśrujaṭādharam|
valkalājinasaṃvītaṃ jvalatparaśudhāriṇam || 1.20 ||
tapantamiva cādityaṃ śītayantaṃ mṛgāṅkavat|
bhāsayantaṃ jagatsarvaṃ vāsudevaprabhaughavat || 1.21 ||
bhāntaṃ brahma śriyātīva śriyā nārāyaṇo yathā|
bhāratyā parameṣṭhīva śarvo girijayā yathā || 1.22 ||
<agastyakṛtā paraśurāmastutiḥ>
jāmadagnyaṃ tamālokya rāmaṃ sarvāṅgasundaram|
mudā paramayopetaḥ śirasā samavandiṣam || 1.23 ||
kṛtto vanaspatiriva pātito dharaṇītale|
vigraho me muniśreṣṭhāḥ rāmasya padapadmayoḥ || 1.24 ||
anujñātaḥ samutthāya toṣayāmāsa taṃ prabhum|
uccaiḥ svaraṃ samāsādya puṇḍarīkākṣavidyayā || 1.25 ||
jitante puṇḍarīkākṣa namaste viśvabhāvana|
namaste'stu hṛṣīkeśa mahāpuruṣapūrvaja || 1.26 ||
namaste vāsudevāya śāntānandacidātmane|
ajitāya namastubhyaṃ ṣāḍguṇyanidhaye namaḥ || 1.27 ||
mahāvibhūtisaṃsthāya namaste puruṣottama|
sahasraśirase tubhyaṃ sahasracaraṇāya te || 1.28 ||
sahasrabāhave tubhyaṃ sahasranayanāya te|
amūrtāya namastubhyamekamūrtāya te namaḥ || 1.29 ||
anekamūrtaye tubhyamakṣarāya ca te namaḥ|
vyāpine vedavedyāya namaste paramātmane || 1.30 ||
cinmātrarūpiṇe tubhyaṃ namastraiyantamūrtaye|
aṇiṣṭhāya sthaviṣṭhāya mahiṣṭhāya ca te namaḥ || 1.31 ||
nediṣṭhāya daviṣṭhāya kṣepiṣṭhāya ca te namaḥ|
variṣṭhāya vasiṣṭhāya kaniṣṭhāya ca te namaḥ || 1.32 ||
pañcātmane namastubhyaṃ sarvāntaryāmiṇe namaḥ|
kalpanākroḍarūpāya sṛṣṭisthityantahetave || 1.33 ||
namaste guṇarūpāya guṇarūpānuvartine|
vyastāya ca samastāya samastavyastarūpiṇe || 1.34 ||
ādimadhyāntaśūnyāya tadvate ca namo namaḥ|
praṇavapratipādyāya namaḥ praṇavarūpiṇe || 1.35 ||
lokayātrāprasiddhyarthaṃ sraṣṭubrahmādirūpiṇe|
namastubhyaṃ nṛsiṃhādimūrtibhedāya viṣṇave || 1.36 ||
vipākaiḥ karmaṇāṃ kleśairaspṛṣṭavapuṣe namaḥ|
namo brahmaṇyadevāya tejasāṃ nidhaye namaḥ || 1.37 ||
nityāsādhāraṇānekalokarakṣāparicchade|
saccidānandarūpāya vareṇyāya namo namaḥ || 1.38 ||
yajamānāya yajñāya yaṣṭavyāya namo namaḥ|
ijyāphalātmane tubhyaṃ namaḥ svādhyāyaśāline || 1.39 ||
namaḥ paramahaṃsāya namaḥ sarvaguṇāya te|
sthitāya parame vyomni bhūyo bhūyo namo namaḥ || 1.40 ||
<agastyasyāgamanahetukaḥ praśnaḥ abhayapradānaṃ ca>
evaṃ stuto jagannātho rāmaḥ kamalalocanaḥ|
avekṣya kṛpayā devo māmuvāca jagatprabhuḥ || 1.41 ||
rāmaḥ
kimarthaṃ kuṃbhatanaya mahendraṃ samupāgataḥ|
bhītivadbhāsi yogīndra kutaste bhayamāgatam || 1.42 ||
indrādvā varuṇādvāpi brahmaṇo vā trilocanāt|
sākṣānnārāyaṇādvāpi na te bhūyādito bhayam || 1.43 ||
vaivasvato vā rakṣāṃsi dānavā vā sapannagāḥ|
vipriyaṃ yadi kuryuste gatisteṣāṃ paraśvadhaḥ || 1.44 ||
tasmādabhītavatsthitvā brūhi kāryaṃ sasaṃbhramam|
sādhayāmyahamadyaiva darśanaṃ me śubhodayam || 1.45 ||
<agastyena svāgamanahetunivedanam>
ityukto jāmadagnyena bhītavat kuṃbhasaṃbhavaḥ|
yathāvadvaktumārebhe rāmāyāmitatejase || 1.46 ||
na bhītiḥ brahmaṇo deva na viṣṇorna ca śaṅkarāt|
nāpi jiṣṇorjana ca yamāt varuṇādvāpi rākṣasāt || 1.47 ||
rākṣasebhyo'pi na bhayaṃ na devebhyo na cāsurāt|
nānyebhyaśca bhayaṃ me'dya bhītaḥ pāpādihāgataḥ || 1.48 ||
vātāpirilvalaścobhau rākṣasau ṛṣikaṇṭakau|
tau hantumarthito devaiḥ ṛṣibhiśca mahātmabhiḥ || 1.49 ||
dattaṃ mayābhayaṃ tebhyo vadhe rākṣasayostayoḥ|
niścitaṃ me manaḥ paścādrakṣasāpi nimantritaḥ || 1.50 ||
rākṣasasya pituḥ śrāddhe sampannamapi pṛṣṭavān|
uktaṃ mayāstu sampannaṃ jaṭhare piṇḍatāṃ gataḥ || 1.51 ||
jīrṇo'bhūnmama vātāpistataḥ kupita ilvalaḥ|
muṣṭinā hantumārebhe māṃ mayā huṃkṛtiḥ kṛtā || 1.52 ||
tena dagdho'bhavadrakṣaḥ sarvaṃ tena hṛtaṃ tapaḥ|
tena khinno'gamaṃ śailaṃ tapase himabhūṣitam || 1.53 ||
atyugraṃ tapa ātiṣṭhaṃ vāṇī kāpyaśarīriṇī|
tapasānena kiṃ te syāt rāmeṇa bhava nirbharaḥ || 1.54 ||
tata utthāya tapasaḥ tvāmadya śaraṇaṃ gataḥ|
anāgasaṃ māṃ bhagavan kṛtvā samavalokaya || 1.55 ||
<bhagavadārādhanatantraśravaṇasya pāpanāśakatvam>
etacchrutvā vaco devo bhagavān bhārgavo muniḥ|
pratyuvāca tataḥ prīto maitrāvaruṇaye prabhuḥ || 1.56 ||
rāmaḥ
ṛṣīṇāmagnikalpānāṃ devānāṃ prītaye tvayā|
vātāpirilvalaścobhau nāśitau te tapo balāt || 1.57 ||
niśśalyā ṛṣayaścāpi devāścāsan tvayānagha|
tadarthaṃ ca tapastaptaṃ bhavatā himavattaṭe || 1.58 ||
asmādālokanenaiva pūto'bhūrmunipuṅgava|
tapodhanakṣayabhayaṃ na te bhavati dāruṇam|| 59 ||
yā cāsīddhṛdaye śaṅkā rakṣo nāśanimittajā|
tāṃ ca haryarcanātantraṃ śrutvā nāśaya kumbhaja || 1.60 ||
<haryarcanātantraviṣaye agastyotthāpitajijñāsā>
agastyaḥ
kiṃ taddharyarcanātantraṃ tantraṃ tatkathamāgatam|
sarvaṃ niravaseṣeṇa upadeṣṭuṃ tvamarhasi || 1.61 ||
|| iti śrībhārgavatantre prathamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<2>
|| dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<tantravivaraṇapūrvakavāsudevārādhanaphalādinirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<tantravivaraṇam>
śrīrāmaḥ
akṣayyasaṃkhyaṃ tantraṃ tat parasmin brahmaṇi sthitam|
sārdhakoṭipramāṇena jagrāha bhagavān vidhiḥ || 2.1 ||
nārado'pi ca jagrāha koṭyardhaṃ brahmaṇo muniḥ|
kapilo'pi muniḥ pañca lakṣamātramupāharat || 2.2 ||
vainateyo'pi jagrāha pādalakṣaṃ janārdanāt|
viṃśatsahasraṃ jagrāha pūjātantraṃ hareḥ phaṇī || 2.3 ||
catvāriṃśatsahasrāṇi vikhanā muniragrahīta|
lakṣmīḥ ṣaṣṭisahasrāṇi lakṣamekaṃ vasundarā || 2.4 ||
śukaḥ pañca sahasrāṇi sahasraṃ saptaterharaḥ|
caturdaśa sahasrāṇi bhagavān sa parāśaraḥ || 2.5 ||
viṣvakseno'pi jagrāha granthaṃ lakṣadvayaṃ hareḥ|
anye ca bahavaḥ kāmaṃ jagṛhurmunipuṅgavāḥ || 2.6 ||
<śāstravidhinā hareḥ ārādhanaphalam>
asaṃkhyātamidaṃ śāstraṃ harerjaṭharapūritam|
saṃgraheṇādya te vacmi tena pūto bhaviṣyasi || 2.7 ||
tena śāstravidhānena pūjayasva ramāpatim|
tena sarvaṃ layaṃ yāti kaluṣaṃ kalaśodbhava || 2.8 ||
smaraṇaṃ kīrtanaṃ viṣṇoḥ pūjanaṃ pādavandanam|
pradakṣiṇaṃ darśanaṃ ca sarvāghaughavināśanam || 2.9 ||
ekaikamapi paryāptaṃ sarvapāpāpanodane|
kiṃ punarbahavo yasya saḥ pumān harireva hi || 2.10 ||
yadyadicchan dhiyā viṣṇuṃ sevate bhaktisaṃyutaḥ|
tasya tattatkaratale nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 2.11 ||
<devālayādikalpanasya tattadārādhanādeścaphalakīrtanam>
yastu viṣṇugṛhaṃ kuryāt vaikuṇṭhaṃ tasya mandiram|
yastu bimbaṃ hareḥ kuryāt sa tu viṣṇau layaṃ vrajet || 2.12 ||
yena pīṭhāni kalpyante sa yāne carate divi|
yacchatraṃ cāmaraṃ pīṭhaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ tathāmbaram || 2.13 ||
anyānyupacārāṇi karoti kamalāpateḥ|
sa tu taistaistathākāśe nirmale'mmarasevite || 2.14 ||
devaiḥ paryupacaryante sa nāghena vilipyate|
kāsāravāpikākūpataṭākādi karoti yaḥ || 2.15 ||
sa bhūyāt satataṃ śā taḥ sa na tāpena lipyate|
kavacāyudhabhūṣāsrak kirīṭādi karoti yaḥ || 2.16 ||
udyānāni ca ramyāṇi dharaṇīṃ sasyamālinīm|
sabhāṃ ca maṇḍapaṃ ramyaṃ sarvaṃ tasya pare'mbare || 2.17 ||
naivedyaṃ vividhaṃ bhakṣyaṃ bhojyaṃ lehyaṃ ca pānakam|
utpādayati paścāt sa sudhāśī bhavati dhruvam || 2.18 ||
anicchannakhilaṃ bhogaṃ yaḥ prītyā kurute kriyāḥ|
sa bhavedamale dhāmni parasminneva līyate || 2.19 ||
yaḥ sadā devasadane dhyāyannāste mudā harim|
punarna māturjaṭhare nivasetkalasodbhava || 2.20 ||
pānīyaṃ devadevāya yo dadāti gataspṛhaḥ|
mātuḥ stanyaṃ na pibati punaḥ kalasasaṃbhava || 2.21 ||
tālavṛntena deveśaṃ yo vījayati bhaktimān|
sa na tāpatrayairlipyeteti śāstravinirṇayaḥ || 2.22 ||
gandhaṃ dadāti yo bhaktyā narake sa na majjati|
āmodayati yaḥ sadma sa svarge matuhīyate || 2.23 ||
dadyācca yo ambaraṃ viṣṇoḥ nagnatvaṃ tasya nocyate|
ālokya hṛṣṭo viṣṇuṃ yaḥ sa sālokyaṃ samaśnute || 2.24 ||
dīpān pradīpayedyastu taddhyānaviṣayo hariḥ|
mārjanālepanairyānti pāpāni vilayaṃ dhruvam || 2.25 ||
toraṇairaṅgavalyādyaiḥ maṇḍayan devamandiram|
sarvadā maṇḍito bhūyādvaikuṇṭhe davamandire || 2.26 ||
vedairaṅgaistathā stotraistoṣayedyadi śārṅgiṇam|
sarvadā toṣitaḥ sarvaiḥ bhavedvaikuṇṭhasadmani || 2.27 ||
gītaṃ nṛtyaṃ tathā vādyaṃ kārayedyo hareḥ priyam|
sa tena tena bhavati parituṣṭaḥ pare pade || 2.28 ||
anantaśayane deve paramānandavigrahe|
acalā paramā bhaktiryasya sa brahmabhājanam || 2.29 ||
bhagavadbhaktiyukteṣu bhaktimān yo bhavennaraḥ|
sa bhavedbhagavatprāṇastasya sthānaṃ bhaveddhariḥ || 2.30 ||
<vāsudevadhyānasya siddhipradātṝtvam>
tatastvaṃ śārṅgadhanvānaṃ vāsudevaṃ sanātanam|
dhyāyasva satataṃ citte tena te siddhiravyayā || 2.31 ||
|| iti bhārgavatantre dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<3>
|| tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<devālayakalpanaprayojanasthānādinirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<bhagavadālayakalpanaviṣayakajijñāsā>
agastyaḥ
bhagavan bhārgava śrīman sarvakalyāṇavigraha|
harimandiranirmāṇaṃ kathaṃ kuryājjagatprabho || 3.1 ||
<bhagavadālayanirmāṇasya prayojanadvayam>
śrīrāmaḥ
kṛtvā prabhūtaṃ salilamārāmān viniveśya ca|
devatāyatanaṃ kuryāt yaśodharmābhivṛddhaye || 3.2 ||
iṣṭāpūrtena labhyante ye lokāstān bibhūṣatā|
devānāmālayaḥ kāryo dvayamapyatra dṛśyate || 3.3 ||
<bhagavadālayakalpanayogyasthānanirdeśaḥ>
salilodyānayukteṣu kṛteṣvakṛtakeṣu ca|
sthāneṣveteṣu sānnidhyamupagacchanti devatāḥ || 3.4 ||
sarassu nalinīcchatranirastaraviraśmiṣu|
haṃsāṃsākṣiptakalhāravīcīvimalavāriṣu || 3.5 ||
haṃsakāraṇḍavaktrauñcacakravākavirāviṣu|
paryantaniculacchāyāviśrāntajalacāriṣu || 3.6 ||
krauñcakrauñcīkalāpāśca kalahaṃsakalasvanāḥ|
phullanīradrumottaṃsāssaṅgamaśreṇimaṅgalāḥ || 3.7 ||
pulinābhyunnatorasthā haṃsahāsāśca nimnagāḥ|
vanopāntanadīśailanirjharopāntabhūmiṣu || 3.8 ||
ramante devatāḥ nityaṃ pureṣūdyānavatsu ca|
parvateṣu praśasteṣu tīreṣu ca payonidheḥ || 3.9 ||
āśrameṣu munīnāṃ ca brāhmaṇāḥ yatra nityaśaḥ|
tatra haryādayo devāḥ vastumicchanti sarvadā || 3.10 ||
yatra bhūmiḥ praśastā syāt bhūsurāṇāṃ manoharā|
sā bhaveddevatānāṃ ca priyā kalaśasaṃbhava || 3.11 ||
<devālayakalpanakrame dvārāditattadbhāgānāṃ pramāṇādiḥ>
catuṣṣaṣṭipadaṃ kāryaṃ devatāyatanaṃ sadā|
dvāraṃ ca madhyamaṃ tatra samadiksthaṃ praśasyate || 3.12 ||
yo vistāro bhavedyasya dviguṇā tatsamunnatiḥ|
ucchrāyādyastṛtīyoṃ'śastena tulyā kaṭirbhavet || 3.13 ||
vistārārdhaṃ bhavedgarbho bhittayo'nyāssamantataḥ|
garbhapādena vistīrṇaṃ dvāraṃ dviguṇamucchritam || 3.14 ||
ucchrāyātpādavistīrṇā śākhā tadvadudumbaraḥ|
vistārapādapratimaṃ bāhulyaṃ śākhayoḥ smṛtam || 3.15 ||
tripañcasaptanavabhiḥ śākhābhistatpraśasyate|
adhaśśākhācaturbhāge pratihārau niveśayet|| 3.16 ||
śeṣaṃ maṅgalyavihagaiḥ śrīvṛkṣasvastikairghaṭaiḥ|
mithunaiḥ patravallībhiḥ pratimābhiśca śobhayet || 3.17 ||
dvāramānāṣṭabhāgonā pratimā syāt sapiṇḍikā|
dvau bhāgau pratimā tatra tṛtīyāṃśaśca paṇḍikā || 3.18 ||
<viṃśatisaṃkhyākānām ālayānāṃ parigaṇanam>
merumandarakailāsavimānacchandanandanāḥ|
samudrapadmagaruḍanadīvardhanakuñjarāḥ || 3.19 ||
guharājo vṛṣo siṃhassarvatobhadrako ghaṭaḥ|
haṃso vṛttaścatuṣkoṇaḥ ṣoḍaśāṣṭāśrakastathā || 3.20 ||
ityete viṃśatiḥ proktāḥ prāsādāḥ śārṅgadhanvanaḥ|
<devālayasya sāmānyaṃ lakṣaṇam>
yathoktena krameṇaiva teṣāṃ lakṣaṇamucyate || 3.21 ||
pūrvoktāyāmavistārāvardhīkṛtya yathāvasu|
caturbhāgena vā kuryāt sarvaṃ vittānusārataḥ || 3.22 ||
<ālayakalpanakrame ardhamaṇḍapādyāyāmādiḥ>
garbhagehasamāyāmam ardhamaṇṭapamucyate|
sapādaṃ sārdhayuktaṃ vā tripādādhikasaṃyutā || 3.23 ||
ardhamaṇḍapamānena sārdhaṃ nṛttārdhamaṇḍapam|
vaipulyasadṛśaṃ sārdhaṃ dviguṇaṃ vā yathecchayā || 3.24 ||
tayormaṇḍapayorevamāyāmo munipuṅgava|
antarmaṇḍalanāmānaṃ prākāraṃ prathamaṃ viduḥ || 3.25 ||
vimānaṃ veṣṭayedetat yadvā syādardhamaṇḍapam|
antarhārāhvayaṃ vapraṃ kārayettadanantaram || 3.26 ||
maryādākhyaṃ tṛtīyaṃ tatprākāraṃ vidhibhāṣitam|
<mūlabimbādisthāpanasthānanirdeśaḥ>
mūlabimbaṃ pratiṣṭhāpyaṃ garbhagehe vidheḥ pade || 3.27 ||
yadvā daive mānuṣe vā dhruvasthāpanamācaret|
<dvārapālasthānanirdeśaḥ>
caṇḍapracaṇḍau saṃsthāpya garbhadvāre yathākramam || 3.28 ||
ardhamaṇḍapakadvāre śaṅkhacakrottamāṅginau|
antarmaṇḍalasaṃjñasya sthalasya dvārapārśvayoḥ || 3.29 ||
jayaśca vijayaścobhau kartavyau munipuṅgava|
antarhārāhvaye dhāmni dvāri padmagadādharau || 3.30 ||
durjayaprabalau sthāpyau maryādādvārapārśvayoḥ|
<parivāradevasthānanirdeśaḥ>
ekāvaraṇake dhāmni parivārānnimān śṛṇu || 3.31 ||
nṛttārdhamaṇḍapasyāgre kalpanīyo vihaṅgarāṭ|
gopurasyottare sāle mandirābhimukhe raviḥ || 3.32 ||
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe sāle candramāḥ parikalpyate|
vahnau kāmo yame brahmā nairṛte syādgajānanaḥ || 3.33 ||
vāruṇe ṣaṇmukho durgā vāyau saumye dhanādhipaḥ|
īśāne śaṃkaraṃ tatra kṣetrapālaṃ prakalpayet|| 3.34 ||
someśayorantarāle viṣvaksenaṃ prakalpayet|
aṅgaṇe dakṣiṇe saptamātaraḥ śrīstu nairṛte || 3.35 ||
indrādayo lokapālāḥ pūjyāḥ svāśāsu cāṅgaṇe|
gopurasya bhavedantarbahirvā balipīṭhikā || 3.36 ||
antarharāhvayopete viśeṣo dhāmni kathyate|
sūryācandramasoḥ sthāne viśeṣau puruṣācyutau || 3.37 ||
sūryācandramasau kalpyau dvītīyāvaraṇe tathā|
hayagrīvaḥ smarasthāne sthāpyaḥ saṅkarṣaṇo vidheḥ || 3.38 ||
varāho vighnarāṭ sthāne pradyumnaḥ ṣaṇmukhasya ca|
kātyāyinyāḥ anantaḥ syāt aniruddho narāsane || 3.39 ||
nṛkesarī śaṃkarasya cāṅgaṇe pīṭhikāsu cet|
indrādayaḥ samabhyarcyāḥ teṣāṃ sthāne yathākramam || 3.40 ||
cakrī ca musalī śaṃkhī khaḍgī caiva gadādharaḥ|
śarṅgī padmī tathā vajrī pūjyā divyāstradhāriṇaḥ || 3.41 ||
prathamāvaraṇe yeṣāṃ sthānā ye samudīritāḥ|
<ālaye dvitīyāvaraṇasthaparivāradevānāṃ sthānāni>
jñeyāḥ dvitīye te sarve parivārāḥ yathākramam || 3.42 ||
maryādāvaraṇe dhāmni gopurobhayapārśvayoḥ|
śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ tathā sthāpya devadevāyudhottamau || 3.43 ||
upendraṃ gopuraśirobhāge sthāpya tataḥ param|
āgneyādikrameṇaiva prākṛtādīn prakalpayet|| 3.44 ||
upendraḥ prākṛtaḥ puṇyaḥ puṣkaro viśvabhāvanaḥ|
asuraghnaḥ kṛtāntaśca bhūtanātho'ṣṭamaḥ smṛtaḥ || 3.45 ||
aṅgaṇe pīṭhikāsthāne kumudādīn prakalpayet|
kumudaḥ kumudākṣaśca puṇḍarīkaśca vāmanaḥ || 3.46 ||
śaṅkukarṇaḥ sarpanetraḥ sumukhassupratiṣṭhitaḥ|
viṣvaksenaṃ tadāgneyyāṃ kṣetrapālaṃ khageśvaram || 3.47 ||
pradhānavedyabhimukhaṃ kalpayetkumbhasaṃbhava|
mahāpīṭhaṃ ca tatraiva dhvajayaṣṭiṃ ca śārṅgiṇaḥ || 3.48 ||
<ālaye mahānasādisthānanirdeśaḥ>
prathamāvaraṇe vāpi dvitīyāvaraṇe'pi vā|
tṛtīyāvaraṇe vāpi pākasthānaṃ prakalpayet || 3.49 ||
vāruṇe vā'thavā saumye kūpaṃ śārve'pi vā bhavet|
yatra vā'pāṃ rasāssanti tatra kūpaṃ prakalpayet || 3.50 ||
<trividhaṃ vaiṣṇavavimānam>
nāgaraṃ drāviḍaṃ ceti vesaraṃ ca tridhā matam|
vimānaṃ devadevasya vimalaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ smṛtam || 3.51 ||
|| iti bhārgavatantre tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<4>
|| caturtho'dhyāyaḥ ||
<pratimāṅgamānādinirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<pratimānirmāṇaviṣaye sāmānyajijñāsā>
agasyaḥ
kathaṃ vā vāsudevasya nirmeyā pratimā hareḥ|
kati bhedāni bimbānāṃ brūhi me kṛpayā prabho || 4.1 ||
<reṇvādimānaviśeṣāṇāṃ lakṣaṇam>
rāmaḥ
vātāyanapathaṃ prāpya ye bhānti raviraśmayaḥ|
teṣu sūkṣmā visarpanti reṇavaḥ paramāṇavaḥ || 4.2 ||
paramāṇurajaḥ keśalīkṣāyūkayavāṣṭakaiḥ|
labdhaṃ mānāṅgulaṃ śālitribhirmānāntarāṅgulam || 4.3 ||
ācāryadakṣiṇakare madhyamāṅgulamadhyame|
parvaṇorantaraṃ dīrghaṃ mātrāṅgulamudāhṛtam || 4.4 ||
vināṅguṣṭhena śeṣābhiḥ muṣṭimaṅgulibhiḥ kṛtam|
caturdhā vibhajedeko bhāgo muṣṭyaṅguliḥ smṛtāḥ || 4.5 ||
yatkiñci pratimāmānaṃ vibhajya daśadhā punaḥ|
ekaṃ dvādaśadhā labdhaṃ dehalabdhāṅgulaṃ bhavet || 4.6 ||
<upādānadravyabhedānusāraṃ saptavidhāni bimbāni>
ratnajaṃ śailajaṃ bimbaṃ dārujaṃ lohajaṃ tathā|
mṛttraivastukayorbimbaṃ citrajaṃ ceti saptadhā || 4.7 ||
<bimbotsedhaviṣaye uttamaḥ pakṣaḥ>
bimbāni vāsudevasya kathyante kalaśodbhava|
tatra pratikṛtestāvattadaṅgānāṃ ca kathyate || 4.8 ||
utsedhamānaṃ mūrdhādipādāntamanupūrvaśaḥ|
labdhānāṃ dehatastāvat aṅgulīnāṃ śatena ca || 4.9 ||
caturviṃśati mānena pratimāṃ parikalpayet|
<pratimāyāḥ tattadaṅgamānanirdeśaḥ>
uṣṇīṣamaṅgulamitaṃ keśāntaṃ tryaṅgulaṃ matam || 4.10 ||
dṛksūtrāntaṃ tadārabhya ṣaṭtriṃśat yavasammitam|
tadvannāsāpuṭāntaṃ syāt tadvacca cibukāvadhi || 4.11 ||
tena keśāntamārabhya mukhamānaṃ munīśvara|
aṣṭottarayavaśataṃ cibukāvayavāvadhi || 4.12 ||
galamardhāṅgulaṃ sārdhaṃ tryaṅgulaṃ kaṇṭhamiṣyate|
hikkāsūtraṃ samārabhya hṛdayāntaṃ mukheritam || 4.13 ||
tataḥ prabhṛti nābhyantaṃ sārdhaṃ satridaśāṅgulam|
tataḥ prabhṛti meḍhrāntaṃ tadvadutsedhamiṣyate || 4.14 ||
tanmūlājjānuparyantaṃ trinyūnaṃ tridaśāṅgulam|
caturaṅgulamutsedhaṃ jānumaṇḍalamiṣyate || 4.15 ||
jānoradhastādakṣāntamūrumānamudīritam|
tatastālāntamutsedhaṃ caturaṅgulasammitam || 4.16 ||
<bimbotsedhaviṣaye madhyamapakṣaḥ>
dehalabdhāni yāvanti daśatāle'bhipūjite|
vibhajya tāvaduktāni madhyame pratimāvidhau || 4.17 ||
trayodaśāṅgulaṃ vaktraṃ tristhāne caturaṅgulam|
ṣaḍviṃśatyaṅgulotsedhamūrumānamudāhṛtam || 4.18 ||
tadvajjaṅdhāsamutsedhamuttareṇetaratsamam|
<bimbotsedhaviṣaye adhamaḥ pakṣaḥ tattadaṅgavistārādikaṃ ca>
adhame daśatāle tu mukhaṃ śatayavaṃ matam || 4.19 ||
tristhāne tāvadeva syāt ūruryavaśatadvayam|
jaṅghā ca tatsamotsedhā mukhyenānyatsamaṃ smṛtam || 4.20 ||
viṃśadyavaśataṃ pādaṃ drāghimā kamalāpateḥ|
aṅkuṣṭhaṃ vedamānaṃ syāt tadāyāmā ca tarjanī || 4.21 ||
ekatriṃśadyavāyāmā madhyamāṅguliriṣyate|
triṃśadyavānāmikā syātkaniṣṭhā tryaṅgulātmikā || 4.22 ||
yavaiśca pañcabhiryuktā pārṣṇo ca caturaṅgulā|
hikkāntayoradho bāhuraṅgulissaptarviśatiḥ || 4.23 ||
tataśca maṇibandhāntaṃ ṣaḍviṃśatyaṅgulāyatam|
yavadvayena ca yutaṃ maṇibandhāttalaṃ punaḥ || 4.24 ||
madhyamāṅguliparyantam aṅgulaṃ sapta kathyate|
pañcāṅgulamathāṅguṣṭhaṃ tarjanī tadvadāyatā || 4.25 ||
ṣaḍyavaiścādhikāyāmā madhyamā tu ṣaḍaṅgulā|
āyatā ṣaḍyavaiścāpi tarjanyānāmikā samā || 4.26 ||
kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhatulitā māṃsalagnaṃ nakhaṃ smṛtam|
ekāṅgulā nakhāyāmā śiṣṭā ṣaḍyavasammitā || 4.27 ||
bāhuvaccopabāhuḥ syāccaturaṅgulamāyatam|
caturyavaṃ cordhvakaṇṭhaṃ nālaṃ pañcāṅgulāyatam || 4.28 ||
liṅgaṃ pañcāṅgulāyāmaṃ muṣkaṃ tu caturaṅgulam|
sārdhāṣṭāṅgulavistāraṃ lalāṭaṃ tadanantaram || 4.29 ||
dvādaśāṅgulavistāraṃ bālacandrasamākṛtiḥ|
tatastu karṇayormadhyaṃ sārdhaṃ satridaśāṅgulam || 4.30 ||
daśāṅgulamadhastasya vistāraṃ cibukasya tu|
sapādaṃ dvyaṅgulaṃ pūrṇaṃ cāpatulyākṛtirbhavet || 4.31 ||
mūlamadhyāgravistāro grīvāyāḥ kramaśo bhavet|
navāṅgulaṃ tathaikonaṃ sārdhasaptāṅgulaṃ tathā || 4.32 ||
hikkāntatoradho bāhurmūlayorantaraṃ bhavet|
yavaiścaturbhirvistīrṇaṃ sa catvāriṃśadaṅgulam || 4.33 ||
kakṣayormadhyavistārastryaṅgalaḥ sārdhaviṃśatiḥ|
aṣṭādaśāṅgulaṃ mānaṃ stanayorapi cāntaram || 4.34 ||
ṣoḍaśāṅgulavistāraṃ tundamadhyamudāhṛtam|
aṣṭādaśāṅgulā śroṇiradharodaramiṣyate || 4.35 ||
kaṭipradeśavistārastryaṅgulaḥ sārdhaviṃśatiḥ|
ūrumūlasya vistārassārdhaṃ satridaśāṃṅgulam || 4.36 ||
agramekāṅgulaṃ sārdhaṃ dvyaṅgulaṃ madhyamiṣyate|
dvyaṅgulo mūlavistāro liṅgasya samudāhṛtam || 4.37 ||
vistīrṇaṃ tryaṅgulaṃ mūlaṃ madhyamardhādhikaṃ punaḥ|
vistīrṇamagramuṣkasya dvātriṃśadyavasammitam || 4.38 ||
vistīrṇamekādaśabhiraṅgulairūrumadhyamam|
agraṃ daśabhireva syāt vistīrṇaṃ kalaśodbhava || 4.39 ||
navāṅgulaṃ jānucakraṃ sārdhamardhendusannibham|
mūlaṃ navāṣṭau madhye syājjaṅghāgraṃ sārdhasaptakam || 4.40 ||
vistīrṇo nālikābhāgaḥ sārdhaiśca caturaṅgulaiḥ|
aṅghrimūlasya vistārastryaṅgulassacaturyavaḥ || 4.41 ||
sārdhapañcāṅgulaṃ madhyamagraṃ sārdhaṣaḍaṅgulam|
sārdhamaṣṭāṅgulaṃ mūlaṃ bāhū saptāṅgulau punaḥ || 4.42 ||
madhye'gre tulyavistāre prakoṣṭhāvayavaṃ punaḥ|
sārdhena saptabhirmūle vistīrṇaṃ sārdhapañcabhiḥ || 4.43 ||
madhye caturbhiragre syānmaṇibandhasya tūcyate|
pañcāṅgulārdhasahitastadvat karatalaṃ smṛtam || 4.44 ||
aṅguṣṭhe mūlavistāro dvyaṅgulo madhyamāṅgulam|
saṣaḍyavamamuṣyāgramaṅgulenaiva sammatam || 4.45 ||
tarjanyā mūlavistāro navabhiḥ sammito yavaiḥ|
madhyamekāṅgulaṃ sapta yavamagramudāhṛtam || 4.46 ||
mūlaṃ daśayavaṃ madhyaṃ madhyamāyāḥ yavā nava|
agramekāṅgulaṃ proktamitarā tarjanī samā || 4.47 ||
mūlamaṣṭau kaniṣṭhāyāḥ yavāḥ saptaiva madhyame|
ṣaḍyavo'gre'ṅgulāgrebhyaḥ pādonā nakhavistṛtiḥ || 4.48 ||
madhye karatale rekhā madhyamāmūlamāsthitā|
kaniṣṭhāmūlamārabhya rekhā madhyamagā bhavet || 4.49 ||
madhyāṅgulitarjanyornimātavyā vicakṣaṇaiḥ|
aṅguṣṭhamūlamārabhya tarjanyantaśca madhyamā || 4.50 ||
tayośca rekhayormadhye tarjanī mūlagāminī|
rekhā kalpyā yathāśāstraṃ śāstrābhijñena śilpinā || 4.51 ||
lalāṭasya ca vistārassapādastryaṅgulo bhavet|
tasyādhastādbhruvau kalpyau catvāriṃśadyavāyatau || 4.52 ||
madhye yavena vistīrṇe yūkāgre cāpasannibhe|
tayormadhyaṃ nava yavam aṣṭādaśayavāyatam || 4.53 ||
cakṣuḥkanīnikāyāmo vistāraśca yavaḥ smṛtaḥ|
aṅgulaṃ śvetabimbasya vistāraṃ kṛṣṇamaṇḍalam || 4.54 ||
yavonamaṅgulaṃ jyotistanmadhye yavasammitam|
yūkamānaṃ tu tanmadhye dṛṣṭiḥ pakṣmāvalī punaḥ || 4.55 ||
aśīti pakṣmaromāṇi vaktrāgrāṇyasitāni ca|
dvyaṅgulaṃ cakṣuṣormadhyaṃ madhyaṃ ca śrutyapāṅgayoḥ || 4.56 ||
catvāriṃśadyavaṃ karṇavistāro dvyaṅgulo bhavet|
ūrdhvakarṇasya cāyāmassahārdhacaturaṅgulaḥ || 4.57 ||
nālaṃ pañcāṅgulamitaṃ vistāraḥ ṣaḍyavaṃ punaḥ|
paścāccaturyavā pālī nālatulyaṃ prakalpayet || 4.58 ||
śravaṇasya ca pippalyā madhyagartaṃ caturyavam|
trivakrakarṇapiplyā gallapātramiva sthitam || 4.59 ||
bandhanaṃ tryaṅgulaṃ randhraṃ netrasūtrasamunnatam|
nāsikāmūlamadhyāgravistāraḥ kathyate kramāt || 4.60 ||
aṅgulaṃ cāṅgulaṃ sārdhaṃ dvyaṅgulaṃ sacaturyavam|
aṅgulenonnataṃ mūlaṃ tena sārdhena madhyamam || 4.61 ||
dvābhyāṃ yavābhyā mūlena dvyaṅgulenāgramuttamam|
ṣaḍyavaṃ suṣiraṃ tasya puṭamadhyaṃ caturyavam || 4.62 ||
puṭapārśvasya vistāro dviyavaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|
utsedho'rdhāṅgulastasya nāsikā tilapuṣpavat || 4.63 ||
uttaroṣṭhāyataṃ sārdhaṃ caturaṅgulamiṣyate|
vistāro'rdhāṅgulastasya para sārdhāṅgulāyatam || 4.64 ||
vistīrṇamaṅgulaṃ tasya pālī caikayavāyatā|
cibuko'ṣṭādaśayavaḥ vistārāyāmasammitaḥ || 4.65 ||
gulphaṃ pādāṅgulotsedhaṃ darpaṇodarasannibham|
vistīrṇāvayavau tulyau rājadantau caturyavau || 4.66 ||
dvātriṃśaddaśanāḥ kalpyāḥ yathāśāstraṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ|
jihvā ṣaḍaṅgulāyāmā vistīrṇā tryaṅgulairbhavet || 4.67 ||
rekhātrayāṅkitā grīvā hikkāsūtrāt stanasthitiḥ|
ekādaśāṅgulaṃ vṛttaṃ dvyaṅgulaṃ stanamaṇḍalam || 4.68 ||
caturyavaṃ cūcukaṃ syāt stanamaṇḍalamadhyagam|
caturyavaṃ nābhigartaṃ dakṣiṇāvartamiṣyate || 4.69 ||
adhyardhamaṅgulaṃ vṛttamakṣamardhāṅgulonnatam|
uṣṇīṣātpṛṣṭhakeśāntamekādhikadaśāṅgulam || 4.70 ||
uṣṇīṣātpārśvakeśāntaṃ dvādaśāṅgulamānakam|
karṇayorantaraṃ vidyāt sārdhaṃ satridaśāṅgulam || 4.71 ||
mūlamadhyāgrabhāgeṣu pṛṣṭhagrīvā yathākramam|
vistīrṇā navabhiḥ sārdhairaṅgulairnavabhistathā || 4.72 ||
aṣṭabhiśca bhavettasyā adhastāt kakudasthitiḥ|
āyāmotsedhavistāraiḥ kakudantaṃ caturyavam || 4.73 ||
ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgulaṃ bāhvormadhyaṃ vistāra pṛṣṭhataḥ|
ṣaḍviṃśatiścāṅgulānāṃ kakṣayorantaraṃ bhavet || 4.74 ||
pṛṣṭhamadhyasya vistāro brahman saptadaśāṅgulaḥ|
vistīrṇau pañcaviṃśatyā śroṇīpārśvau tathāṅgulaiḥ || 4.75 ||
kaṭipradeśavistāraṣṣaṅviśatyaṅgulo bhavet|
ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgulaṃ madhye kakudasya kaṭestathā || 4.76 ||
dvyaṅgulonnatiretasya sphītau tanmadhyamāṅgulam|
aṣṭāṅgulaṃ stane mūlaṃ madhyaṃ saptāṅgulaṃ bhavet || 4.77 ||
agryaṃ tryaṅgulavistāram ūrvormūlādiṣu triṣu|
bhavetkrameṇa vistāraḥ sārdhaḥ satridaśāṅgulaḥ || 4.78 ||
dvādaśāṅgulayuktaḥ syāt tathaiva dvādaśāṅgulaḥ|
jānvaṣṭāṅgulavistāraṃ sārdhamaṣṭāṅgulaṃ bhavet|| 4.79 ||
jaṅghāmadhyasya vistāro nalakā caturaṅgulā|
caturyavādhikā caiva gulphaśca caturaṅgulaḥ || 4.80 ||
vistāramānaṃ triguṇaṃ sarveṣāṃ nahanaṃ matam|
abhītido vā varado mukhyo vāmetaraḥ karaḥ || 4.81 ||
stanasūtrasamaṃ kāryaṃ aṅgulāgraṃ karasya tu|
tayorbhavedantarālaṃ dvādaśāṅgulamānakam || 4.82 ||
<pratimāyāḥ ābhūṣaṇānām vivaraṇam>
aṅguṣṭhatarjanīmadhye sanālaṃ kamalaṃ bhavet|
varade kamalākāraṃ likhet karatalodare || 4.83 ||
adhomukhe vāmahaste mukhye kuryādgadāṃ śubhām|
jaghanyayostu karayoḥ śaṅkhaṃ cakraṃ niveśayet || 4.84 ||
bāhuṣvaṣṭasu devasya tāni tānyāyudhānyapi|
niveśayedyathākāmaṃ śilpaśāstravicakṣaṇaḥ || 4.85 ||
kirīṭaṃ kuṇḍale hārān yajñasūtrāṅguliyake|
kaustubhaṃ vanamālāṃ ca śrīvatsaṃ dakṣiṇorasi || 4.86 ||
aṅgade kaṭake ramye kāñcīdāma ca nūpure|
dukūlamantarīyaṃ ca sottarīyaṃ niveśayet || 4.87 ||
deśakālopalālyāni bhūṣaṇāni niveśayet|
<bhagavataḥ pratimāyāḥ āsīnādisvarūpavivekaḥ>
āsīnaṃ vā śayānaṃ vā tiṣṭhantaṃ vā yathāruci || 4.88 ||
āruḍhaṃ pakṣirāje vā kalpayedviṣṇuvigraham|
<pratimāyāḥ sthāpanasthānavivekaḥ>
evaṃ garbhagṛhe devaṃ brahmasthāne niveśayet || 4.89 ||
yadvā divye mānuṣe vā dvārasūtre niveśayet|
evaṃ saṃsthāpayeddevaṃ pratiṣṭhāsamaye sudhīḥ || 4.90 ||
<devīpratimāsthāpanasthānanirdeśaḥ>
aṣṭau devīḥ śriyādyāśca pārśvayorviniveśayet|
śrīrdakṣiṇe dharā vāme devyau devasya kalpayet || 4.91 ||
ratissarasvatī tuṣṭirdevyo devasya dakṣiṇe|
kīrtiḥ prītiśca śāntiśca vāme devasya saṃsthitāḥ || 4.92 ||
<devyāḥ saṃkhyāvicāraḥ>
aṣṭabhirvā catasṛbhirdvābhyāṃ yadvaikayāpi vā|
vinā vā devadeveśaṃ saśrīvatsaṃ niveśayet || 4.93 ||
<devīnāṃ mūrtikalpane viśeṣaḥ>
devīnāntu śriyādīnāṃ viśeṣaścaiṣa kalpane|
rekhātrayāṅkitā grīvā hikkāsūtrātstanasthitiḥ || 4.94 ||
ekādaśāṅgulaṃ vṛttaṃ dvyaṅgulaṃ stanamaṇḍalam|
caturyavaṃ cūcukaṃ syāt stanamaṇḍalamadhyagam || 4.95 ||
aṣṭādaśāṅgulamurasstanayorapi maṇḍalam|
aṅgulaiḥ pañcadaśabhiḥ paricchinnaṃ yathātatham || 4.96 ||
navāṅgulaśca vistāraḥ utsedhaścāpi tādṛśaḥ|
dvādaśāṅgulavistāraḥ stanayormadhya iṣyate || 4.97 ||
kṛṣṇamaṇḍalavistāro dvyaṅgulaṃ parikīrtitam|
śroṇī ṣaḍaṅgulā yāmā yonissārdhāṅgulatrayam || 4.98 ||
<devīpratimāyāḥ ābhūṣaṇādinirdeśaḥ>
abalānuguṇairanyairambarairapi bhūṣaṇaiḥ|
bhūṣayenmakuṭaṃ yadvā dhammilaṃ vā prakalpayet || 4.99 ||
asvatantrāḥ yadā devyaḥ dvibhūjāḥ pārśvabhūmiṣu|
caturbhujāstāḥ svātantrye dvibhujā vā yathāmati || 4.100 ||
dakṣiṇetarabhāgasthā dakṣiṇe puṣpadhāriṇī|
vāmetarasthitā devī vāme kamaladhāriṇī || 4.101 ||
anyena coruviśrāntāḥ kalpayecchilpatantravit|
<utsavādipratimotsedhādivivaraṇam>
mūlabimbatṛtīyāṃśasamamutsavakautukam || 4.102 ||
snapanapratimā cāpi tadutsedhāḥ prakīrtitāḥ|
balibimbapratikṛtiḥ śayanotthānakautukam || 4.103 ||
karmabimbaṃ ca nirmeyaṃ mūlatālapramāṇataḥ|
<aṅgabimbakalpanavicāraḥ>
brāhme bhāge dhruve nyaste naivāṅgaparikalpanā || 4.104 ||
laukikenaiva bimbena kuryānnaimittikaṃ budhaḥ|
dhruvaberārdhamānena kautukaṃ lohanirmitam || 4.105 ||
laukikaṃ tena kurvīta sarvāṃ devakriyāṃ sudhīḥ|
cakreṇa balidānaṃ syāt tathā tīrthāvagāhanam || 4.106 ||
kūrcena śayanaṃ kāryaṃ brāhme mūlārcanāvidhau|
daiva vā mānuṣye sthāpye brāhme karmāṇi vinyaset || 4.107 ||
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ sarvaṃ tenaiva pūryate|
sthānayānāsanavidhau dhruvabimbaṃ yathākramam || 4.108 ||
karmārcādīni tadvat syāddhruve tu śayite punaḥ|
tiṣṭhedvāsīta vā kāmaṃ karmādyarcā yathāmati || 4.109 ||
nityotsavapratikṛtiḥ sarvatraiva sthitā bhavet|
karmādibimbaṣaṭkaṃ ca śrībhūmisahitaṃ bhavet || 4.110 ||
yadvā śrībhūmisahitamekaṃ vā makhakautukam|
<pīṭhakalpanaprakāraḥ>
arcāyāmaṃ tridhā kṛtvā pīṭhamekena kalpayet || 4.111 ||
upānahādipañcāṅgayuktaṃ vṛttaṃ sukhāvaham|
athavā caturaśraṃ vā vistāratulitāyati || 4.112 ||
pīṭhotsedhaṃ tridhā kṛtvā padmamekena kalpayet|
dalairdvādaśabhiryuktamaṣṭābhirvā yathāruci || 4.113 ||
pādamānaṃ dalāyāmaṃ kevalaṃ vā saroruham|
pīṭhaṃ vā kevalaṃ vāpi kalpayetkalaśodbhava || 4.114 ||
āsīne'śrāyataṃ yadvā kuryādvṛttāyataṃ tu vā|
pratimārdhena vā pīṭhaṃ tṛtīyāṃśena vā tathā || 4.115 ||
padmaṃ bahudalairyuktaṃ karṇikākesarānvitam|
lambamānapadādhāraṃ kamalaṃ tatra kalpayet || 4.116 ||
saṅkocitaprasavyāṅghreritaraṃ lambayedadhaḥ|
mūlaberatripādena pādenārdhena vā bhavet || 4.117 ||
ucchrāyaḥ parivārāṇām evaṃ vaikuṇṭhabhāṣitam|
aṃsatulyā harerdevī brahmadyā parivāravat || 4.118 ||
balipīṭhāni sarvāṇi piṇḍikāmānamānataḥ |
<vimāne kīlakalpanādyavasaraḥ>
evaṃ prakalpya bimbāni vimāne kīlakalpanam || 4.119 ||
tatastu kalaśanyāsaḥ pratiṣṭhā tadanantaram|
|| iti bhārgavatantre caturtho'dhyāyaḥ ||
<5>
|| pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<pratiṣṭhāṅgasaṃbhāravivaraṇādhyāyaḥ>
<pratiṣṭhākrame maṇḍapādīnāṃ kalpanādiviṣaye jijñāsā>
agastyaḥ
kathaṃ tu bhagavan viṣṇoḥ pratiṣṭhāṃ kārayetsudhīḥ|
saṃbhārāḥ ke ca sambhāryāstatra yogyāḥ bravītu me || 5.1 ||
<maṇḍapakuṇḍasvarūpādinirdeśaḥ>
śrīrāmaḥ
prasādasyāgrataḥ kuryādadhivāsanaṇḍapam|
prathamāvaraṇe vāpi dvitīyāvaraṇe'pi vā || 5.2 ||
tṛtīyāvaraṇe yadvā deśe yogyāvakāśake|
bāhudaṇḍaiśca daśabhiścaturaśraṃ manoharam || 5.3 ||
caturdvārasamāyuktaṃ kalpayecchilpavittamaḥ|
tanmadhye pañcabhirhastaiḥ caturaśrā manoharā || 5.4 ||
hastocchrāyāthavārdhena caturaśreṣṭakācitā|
tālatrayāt bahistasyāḥ kuṇḍāni parikalpayet || 5.5 ||
caturaśraṃ bhavetprācyāṃ yāmye cāpasamākṛtiḥ|
vṛttakuṇḍaṃ tu vāruṇyāṃ saumye koṇatrayānvitam || 5.6 ||
caturviṃśāṅgulaṃ sūtraṃ vistārāyāmasammitam|
prāgbhāge kalpitaṃ kuṇḍaṃ caturaśraṃ vidhīyate || 5.7 ||
ṣaṣṭhyaṅgulaṃ bhaveccāpaṃ jyā tu ṣaṭtriṃśadaṃgulā|
cāpakuṇḍaṃ bhavedetaddakṣiṇe vedikā bhuvaḥ || 5.8 ||
dvātriṃśadaṅgulaṃ sūtraṃ triguṇena prakalpayet|
uttare vedikābhūmeḥ trikoṇaṃ vidyate sphuṭam || 5.9 ||
pakṣasaṃkhyāṅgulaṃ sūtraṃ madhyato bhrāmayedyadi|
vṛttakuṇḍaṃ bhavedetadvāruṇe vedikā bhuvaḥ || 5.10 ||
sūtrapātaṃ purā kṛtvā viṃśatyā caturaṅgulaiḥ|
agādhamavanīṃ khātvā dvyaṅgulaṃ tadbahiḥ pade || 5.11 ||
<kuṇḍamekhalākalpanaprakāraḥ>
mekhalāyāstrayaṃ kuryāt dṛḍhayeṣṭakayā sudhīḥ|
athavā prakṛterbāhye caturaṅgulamānataḥ || 5.12 ||
mekhalāyāstrayaṃ kuryāt supakveṣṭakayā dṛḍham|
prathamā tālamānā syāt madhyamāṣṭāṅgulonnatā || 5.13 ||
caturaṅgulamānaiva caramā mekhalā bhavet|
<kuṇḍe yonikalpanakramaḥ>
paścime vāthavā yāmye bhāge yoniṃ prakalpayet || 5.14 ||
pakṣasaṃkhyāṅgulāyāmāṃ kāhalākṛtisannibhām|
mūlasūkṣmordhvavistīrṇāṃ madhye nimnāṃ śiro bhuvi || 5.15 ||
aśvatthadalavaddṛśyāṃ pratikuṇḍaṃ prakalpayet|
mumukṣornaiva yoniḥ syāt kuṇḍaṃ vā saikamekhalam || 5.16 ||
<prapādikalpanakramanirdeśaḥ>
prapāṃ vā parikurvīta caturdvārasamanvitām|
samadhyavedikuṇḍāḍhyāmaiśānyāṃ snānamaṇḍapam || 5.17 ||
aṅkurārpaṇabhūbhāgaṃ kalpayena surakṣitam|
dṛḍhāstiraskariṇyaśca bandhanīyā yathāvidhi || 5.18 ||
dvihastā vā trihastā vā vedikā snānamaṇḍape|
<sruksru voḥ kalpanaprakāraḥ>
sruksruvau kalpanīyau ca rambhāpuṣpadalānanau || 5.19 ||
sūcyagre sūkaramukhaṃ ghṛtakulyā niveśanam|
sarpiḥ kuṇḍababhṛdvaktraṃ na sūkaramukhaṃ sruve || 5.20 ||
mukhādadhastāt kaṇṭhaṃ ca bāhudaṇḍāyatāvubhau|
kandaṃ vā kamalaṃ vāpi tayormūle prakalpayet || 5.21 ||
<toraṇasnānapīṭhādikalpanakramaḥ>
toraṇāni ca catvāri śūlatrayayutāni ca|
adhaḥ śākhāvihīnāni kalpayetkalaśodbhava || 5.22 ||
dvāviṃśatyaṅgulāyāmaṃ tadardhena ca vistṛtam|
ghanaṃ cāpi tadardhena kalpayedaṣṭamaṅgulam || 5.23 ||
bimbapramāṇānuguṇaṃ snānapīṭhaṃ prakalpayet|
ṛtvijāṃ pūjakānāṃ ca deśikasyāsanāni ca || 5.24 ||
<pādukādisaṃbhārāṇāṃ vivaraṇam>
pāduke pādasadṛśau sarvaṃ syādyajñavṛkṣajam|
śrīvatsaṃ pūrṇakuṃbhaṃ ca bherīṃ darpamaṇḍalam || 5.25 ||
matsyayugmaṃ tathā śaṃkhaṃ cakraṃ kāśyapanandana|
pratyekamaṣṭāvetāni vilikhidaṣṭamaṅgale || 5.26 ||
kūrmākāraṃ likhettattadāsaneṣvadhikāriṇām|
śvetau raktau tathā pītau nīlāvaṣṭāvime dhvajāḥ || 5.27 ||
mahākuṃbhopakuṃbhāśca karakaṃ maṇikaṃ tathā|
kalaśāścāpi sambhāryāḥ dīpastambhāstathā ghṛtam || 5.28 ||
tailaṃ ca dīpapātrāṇi vartinī suguṇānyapi|
candanāni sugandhāni puṣpāṇi surabhīṇi ca || 5.29 ||
vastrāṇi ca dukūlāni kambalānyāvikāni ca|
vyāghrakṛṣṇājinādīni śobhanānyakṣatāni || 5.30 ||
samidhaśca kuśāḥ kāśāḥ dhānyāni śayanāsanam|
śiropadhānaṃ gaṇḍūṣaṃ tūlikāratnakambalam || 5.31 ||
vividhāni ca dhānyāni ratnāni vividhāni ca|
svarṇādisaptalohāni gāḥ savatsāḥ manoharāḥ || 5.32 ||
dhūpadravyāṇi karpūraṃ ghanasāraṃ ca guggulam|
tāmbūlaṃ kramukaṃ mālā hārāśca kusumaiḥ kṛtāḥ || 5.33 ||
phalāni makarandaṃ ca payodadhi ca śarkarā|
guḍaṃ ca netra sāmagrī śalāke dve ca hemaje || 5.34 ||
kuṃbhapratikṛtiḥ pañca bhūṣaṇāni ca kaṅkaṇam|
kaṭāhaṃ ca jaladroṇī sauvarṇakamalāni ca || 5.35 ||
tantavaścāpi kuṃbhānāṃ pālikāśca ghaṭādayaḥ|
śarāvāścāpi sāmānyā mṛdbhāṇḍāni śubhānyapi || 5.36 ||
kanyakārucirāśaacāpi rūpavatyaḥ svalaṅkṛtāḥ|
dhūpapātraṃ dīpapātraṃ ghaṇṭākarpūrapaṅkajam || 5.37 ||
pādyācamanayoḥ pātramardhyamāvāhanāhvayam|
naivedyapātraṃ pānīyabhājanaṃ ca pratigraham || 5.38 ||
pādukādarpaṇaṃ svarṇakaṅkataṃ ghaṭadīpakam|
nīrājanasyāñjanasya balidānasya bhājanam || 5.39 ||
vyajanaṃ cātapatraṃ ca cāmaraṃ puṣpabhājanam|
piṭakāni khanitraṃ ca karīṣaṃ kāṣṭhasaṃcayaḥ || 5.40 ||
hivaḥ pātraṃ mekṣaṇaṃ ca dhamanyo vyajanāni ca|
ājyapātraṃ carusthālī praṇītā prokṣaṇī tathā || 5.41 ||
aṣṭabandhanavastūni lāṅgalaṃ hemanirmitam|
dakṣiṇārthaṃ dhanaṃ pūrṇaṃ vādyāni vividhānyapi || 5.42 ||
rūpavatyaśca gaṇikāḥ nṛttagītaviśāradāḥ|
vedavedāṅganipuṇāḥ brāhmaṇāḥ bhagavanmayāḥ || 5.43 ||
yātrāpīṭhaṃ ca devasya yānāni vividhānyapi|
bāhupradīpāḥ vividhāḥ śubhado mattavāraṇaḥ || 5.44 ||
sūtrāṇyapi śalākāśca sambhāryā yajñakarmaṇi|
|| iti bhārgavatantre pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<6>
|| ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ ||
<pratiṣṭhāviṣayavivecanādhyāyaḥ>
<pratiṣṭhāviṣayakajijñāsopasthāpanam>
pratiṣṭhā kīdṛśī brahmān pratiṣṭhatā tu kīdṛśaḥ|
ko vā kālaḥ pratiṣṭhāyāḥ śubhadaḥ sarvadehinām || 6.1 ||
kīdṛśo'pyadhikārī vā kathaṃ vā tatsamāpyate|
jāmadagnyaparaṃ dhāman kṛpayā brūhi me prabho || 6.2 ||
<pratiṣṭhākrame yajamānalakṣaṇam>
śrīrāmaḥ
śraddhayā parayā yuktaḥ kalyāṇahṛdayaḥ svayam|
āstiko devadeveśe nyastacittaḥ svayaṃ prabhuḥ || 6.3 ||
aprameyaṃ dhanaṃ dhānyam utsāhasca ime trayaḥ|
vasantyavirataṃ yatra sa pratiṣṭhāpako bhavet || 6.4 ||
pariśuddhaśca dakṣaśca kṛtajño gurupaṅktiṣu|
alobhayati kāryāṇi yaḥ sa evārabhet kriyām || 6.5 ||
cāturvarṇyo'nulomo vā jñātibhiḥ sakalairyutaḥ|
ācāryaṃ purataḥ kṛtvā pratiṣṭhāṃ tena sādhayet || 6.6 ||
<pratiṣṭhākrame ācāryasya lakṣaṇam>
pāñcarātravidaṃ śāntaṃ sarvasiddhāntakovidam|
niḥspṛhaṃ nirjitadvandvaṃ sarvadoṣavivarjitam || 6.7 ||
ṛjubuddhiṃ sadā brahmadhyānaikanirataṃ śubham|
ādyavarṇaṃ bhāgavataṃ nārāyaṇaparāyaṇam || 6.8 ||
pratiṣṭhotsavakāryeṣu prāyaścittādikarmasu|
nipuṇaṃ varayetpūrvamācāryatve subuddhimān || 6.9 ||
<pratiṣṭhāsampādanārthamṛtvigādivicāraḥ>
tatastenaiva guruṇā ṛtvijaḥ ṣoḍaśāthavā|
aṣṭau vā caturo yadvā trīn dvau vā varayet sudhīḥ || 6.10 ||
alābhe ṛtvijāṃ yajñaṃ gururekaḥ samāpayet|
ekādhvaravidhānena yathāvatkalaśodbhava || 6.11 ||
<ācāryavaraṇakrame niṣiddhāḥ>
vānaprastho yatiścāpi brāhmaṇāditare trayaḥ|
naivādhikāriṇaḥ sarve pañcasaṃskāravarjitāḥ || 6.12 ||
na kuṇḍagolakau naiva bhaveyuranulomajāḥ|
karmādhikāriṇo naiva jātyā brāhmaṇayonayaḥ || 6.13 ||
<ācāryavaraṇakramaḥ>
vidhuro'pyadhikārī syāt gurutve jñānagauravāt|
evaṃ vicārya sakalamācāryaṃ varayetpurā || 6.14 ||
samaye yajamānastu gāṃ bhuvaṃ kāñcanaṃ tathā|
nārikelaṃ ca karmādāvācāryāya pradāpayet || 6.15 ||
<pratiṣṭhākrame grāhyāgrāhyakālavivekaḥ>
pratiṣṭhākālamadhunā vakṣe śrṛṇu manīśvara|
mukhyaṃ pūrvāyanaṃ proktaṃ jaghanyaṃ dakṣiṇāyanam || 6.16 ||
dhruvaberapratiṣṭhāyāṃ mukhyamevāyanaṃ varam|
jaghanyaṃ karmabimbānāṃ doṣāya na kadācana || 6.17 ||
pratiṣṭhākaraṇe tyājyaṃ pitṛmāsacatuṣṭayam|
māseṣvanyeṣu sarveṣu kārayedavicārayan || 6.18 ||
bālyaṃ ca somaputrasya mauḍhyaṃ ca guruśukrayoḥ|
uparāgayutaṃ māsaṃ sarvathā parivarjayet || 6.19 ||
yadvā pakṣaṃ purastācca parastācca parityajet|
durdinaṃ ravisaṅkrāntimatimāsaṃ malāhvayam || 6.20 ||
gaṇḍātigaṇḍayogāni sarvathā parivarjayet|
arkāravārau mandasya vāraṃ viṣṭaṃ ca varjayet || 6.21 ||
amāriktāṣṭamītithyaḥ varjanīyāḥ prayatnataḥ|
kṛttikā bharaṇī jeṣṭhā sarvaraudradvidaivatam || 6.22 ||
trīṇi pūrvāṇi pitṛbhaṃ ninditāni parityajet|
āsuraṃ mārutaṃ citrā śraviṣṭhā śatatārakam || 6.23 ||
nātyuccaṃ nātinīcaṃ ca samaṃ grāhyaṃ manīṣibhiḥ|
pāpadṛgyogasahitamaṣṭame grahasaṃyutam || 6.24 ||
daśame pāpasaṃyuktaṃ tallagnaṃ parivarjayet|
evaṃ vilokya nitarāṃ saha mauhūrtikairguruḥ || 6.25 ||
vilokya guṇasaṃvādhaṃ guṇagrāmaṃ ca śāstrataḥ|
yajamānagrāmadhāmnāmanukūle samācaret || 6.26 ||
īdṛśaṃ maṅgaladinaṃ viniścitya dvijottamaiḥ|
ārabheta pratiṣṭhātuṃ deśikaḥ śāstrasāravit || 6.27 ||
<aṅkuramṛtsaṃgrahaṇāt pūrvaṃ sampādanīyāni kṛtyāni>
tadarthaṃ yajamānaśca ṛtvijaśca gurūttamaḥ|
anukūle śubhe kāle snāyāsuḥ kṣurakarmaṇā || 6.28 ||
snātāśśuddhāmbaradharāssarvālaṅkārabhūṣitāḥ|
āhūya rathakāraṃ ca kulālamapi śilpinaḥ || 6.29 ||
ācāryastoṣayitvā tānvisṛjyānu ca śilpinā|
netrāṇyunmīlya vidhivat karmabinbeṣu śilpinaḥ || 6.30 ||
toṣayitvā dhanairvastraiḥ manaḥ prahlādanairapi|
nirgamayyātha puṇyāhaṃ vācayetsarvaśuddhaye || 6.31 ||
gomayālepite bhūmau raṅgabalyā pariṣkṛte|
dhānyapīṭhe nyaset kuṃbhaṃ sākṣataṃ sūtraveṣṭitam || 6.32 ||
śarāvakūrcapatrāḍhyaṃ tatrāvāhya śriyaḥ patim|
gāyatryā caiva vaiṣṇavyā samabhyarcyākṣatādibhiḥ || 6.33 ||
abliṅgaiḥ pavanairmantraiḥ pavamānaiḥ pavitrakaiḥ|
śāntimantraiśca ṛtvigbhiḥ japtvā vyāhṛtividyayā || 6.34 ||
pīṭhaṃ saṃkṣālitaṃ bimbamāsāditaśubhodakaiḥ|
prokṣayedaṅkurāyātha mṛtsaṅgrahamācaret || 6.35 ||
<aṃkurārthaṃ mṛtsaṅgrahakramaḥ>
ājānupādau prakṣālya hastau cāmaṇibandhanāt|
trirācamya mṛdā puṇḍrairalaṅkṛtya pavitravān || 6.36 ||
piṭakaṃ ca khanitraṃ ca śodhayitvā śubhodakaiḥ|
vastrairgandhaiśca mālyaiśca khanitraṃ bhūṣitaṃ purā || 6.37 ||
śirasā dhārayedṛtvigathavā paricārakaḥ|
brāhmaṇaiḥ śākunaṃ sūktaṃ paṭhadbhiḥ vādyamaṅgalaiḥ || 6.38 ||
bāhupradīpairvimalaiḥ pūjopakaraṇaiḥ saha|
diśaṃ prācīmudīcāṃ vā śubhāṃ bhūmiṃ samāśrayan || 6.39 ||
āsīnaḥ prāṅmukho bhūtvā prāṇānāyamya deśikaḥ|
bhūtagrāmaṃ viśodhyātha puṇyāhamapi vācayet || 6.40 ||
`vyāhṛtyā' prokṣayedbhūmiṃ khanitraṃ piṭakaṃ tathā|
`bhūmisūktena' dharaṇīm ṛtvigbhirabhimantrayet || 6.41 ||
samudravasanāṃ devīṃ parvatastanamaṇḍitām|
ratnagarbhāṃ gururdhyāyet vasudhāṃ sasyasālinīm || 6.42 ||
āvāhya bhūmimantreṇa bhūmiṃ bhūmau samarcayet|
ardhyapādyādibhirgandhaiḥ puṣpairnānāvidhairapi || 6.43 ||
dhūpairdīpaiśca naivedyaiḥ vividhai rasanāpriyaiḥ|
stotreṇābhiṣṭuyāddevīṃ vasudhāṃ ratnamaṇḍitām || 6.44 ||
<mṛtsaṅgrahakrame bhūstutiḥ>
sarvabhūtadhare kānte sarvalokanamaskṛte|
viṣṇupatni jagaddhātri vasudhe sasyamālini || 6.45 ||
puṇyakānanakeśāḍhye parvatastanamaṇḍite|
samudravasane devi nadīviṣayavāsini || 6.46 ||
valmīkaśravaṇe vedabhāṣiṇi śrutisevite|
phullāmbujekṣaṇe devi prasīda harivallabhe || 6.47 ||
namaste bhuvaneśāne namaḥ sarvasurārcite|
namo bhārgavi me mātaḥ praṇato'smi vasundhare || 6.48 ||
viṣṇuśaktisamudbhūte śaṅkhavarṇe maheśvari|
anekaratnasaṅkīrṇe bhūmi tumyaṃ namo namaḥ || 6.49 ||
iti stutvā ca natvā ca dhyātvā vimalacetasā|
<mṛtsaṅgrahamantranirdeśaḥ>
`aśvakrānte'ti mantreṇa khanitreṇa gurūttamaḥ || 6.50 ||
mṛdaṃ samagrāṃ gṛhṇīyāt prāṅmukhaḥ kroḍavidyayā|
<mṛdānayanaṃ gurvādisammānanaṃ ca>
tāṃ mṛdaṃ mūlamantreṇa piṭake vetranirmite || 6.51 ||
ācchādya nava vastreṇa mantreṇā`stre'ṇa deśikaḥ|
svayaṃ gṛhītvā śirasā brāhmaṇaiḥ svastipāṭhakaiḥ || 6.52 ||
vividhairmaṅgalairanyaiḥ praviśenmandiraṃ guruḥ|
dhāma pradakṣiṇīkṛtya aṅkurārpaṇamaṇḍape || 6.53 ||
vinyasettāṃ mṛdaṃ paścāt yajamāno guruṃ tathā|
ṛtvijo vastragandhādyaiḥ puṣpaiḥ pañcāṅgabhūṣaṇaiḥ || 6.54 ||
hāropavītakaṭakakeyūrāṅgulibhūṣaṇam|
pañcāṅgabhūṣaṇamidaṃ kathitaṃ kamalodbhava || 6.55 ||
<pālikāsthāpanamaṅkuradhānyasaṃskāraḥ tattaddevatārādhanañca>
atha te'laṅkṛtāḥ samyak aṅkurārpaṇabhūtale|
puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvātha prokṣayet pālikādikam || 6.56 ||
pālikā dvādaśāhṛtya tāsāṃ kaṇṭhaṃ tṛṇādibhiḥ|
baddhvā'pūrya mṛdā paścādaṅkurārpaṇabhūtale || 6.57 ||
gomayena samāliptāṃ raṅgavalyā pariṣkṛtām|
āsādya dhānyaiḥ sampūrṇaiḥ pīṭhaṃ tatra prakalpayet || 6.58 ||
prāgāyatāni pañcaiva tathā tiryañci pātayet|
sūtrāṇi ṣoḍaśapade madhye padacatuṣṭayam || 6.59 ||
somakuṃbhāya sammārjya paritaḥ pālikāspadam|
`pauruṣe'ṇaiva mantreṇa pālikāsthāpanaṃ caret || 6.60 ||
somakuṃbhamalaṅkṛtya gandhaiḥ puṣpaiśca sākṣataiḥ|
sūtrairvastraiśśarāveṇa kūrcena ca yathākramam || 6.61 ||
vinyasyāṅkuradhānyāni godhūmabrīhisāḍhakī|
mugdaṃ sacaṇakaṃ rājamāṣaṃ ca satilaṃ tathā || 6.62 ||
māṣaṃ kalutthametāni dhānyāni nava cāharet|
saṃkṣālya `viṣṇugāyatryā' cāplutya payasā gavām || 6.63 ||
`ṛtaṃ satye'ti mantreṇa brahmāvāhya samarcayet|
gandhodakena sampūrya somakumbhaṃ saratnakam || 6.64 ||
`sante payāṃsi' mantreṇa somamāvāhya pūjayet|
pālikāsu dvādaśasu viṣṇorāvāhanaṃ caret || 6.65 ||
`tadasyoru' `pratadviṣṇu'ryasyoru'ca pare ubhe|
`vicakrame' `dhruvāso'sya' `trirdevaśca' `praviṣṇakama' || 6.66 ||
`ato devā' `idaṃ viṣṇa'riti mantrairyathākramama|
pālikāsu sakūrcāsu prāgādīśāvasānakam || 6.67 ||
āvāhya gandhapuṣpādyairarcayitvā tataḥ parama|
<homaprakāraḥ>
prāgbhāge pālikāvedyāḥ sthaṇḍilaṃ parikatpayet || 6.68 ||
aginaṃ saṃsādhya vidhivat sarpiṣāṣṭottaraṃ śatam|
`sante payāṃsi'mantreṇa hutvā sampātamāharet || 6.69 ||
`idaṃ viṣṇuri'ti śrutyā hutvā pūrṇāhutiṃ guruḥ|
<bījāvāparakṣaṇādiprakāraḥ>
sampātājyena saṃsiñcedbījapātraṃ ca pālikāḥ || 6.70 ||
somakuṃbhe punaḥ somamabhyarcya ca nivedya ca|
`dvādaśākṣara'mantreṇa bījāvāpaṃ samācaret || 6.71 ||
ācāryo vāpayedbījaṃ tūṣṇīmanye ca ṛtvijaḥ|
`mūrdhānami'ti mantreṇa mṛdbhirācchādayettataḥ || 6.72 ||
`imaṃ me varuṇo'tyeva payasā secayedapi|
anirvāṇaṃ tato dīpaṃ rakṣennaktaṃ divaṃ mudā || 6.73 ||
somakumbhārcanaṃ nityaṃ yāvatkarma samāpyate|
pālikārādhanaṃ kuryāt sāyaṃ prātarnivedayet || 6.74 ||
|| iti bhārgavatantre ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ ||
<7>
|| saptamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<vāstupūjābimbajalādhivāsavivaraṇādhyāyaḥ>
<vāstupūjāyāḥ prayojananirdeśaḥ>
rāmaḥ
aṅkurāṇyarpayitvaivaṃ vāstupūjāṃ samācaret|
vimānamaṇḍapādīnāṃ prākārāṇāñca śuddhaye || 7.1 ||
śāntaye sarvadoṣāṇāṃ kriyamāṇasya karmaṇaḥ|
nirvighnena samāptyarthaṃ vāstupūjā praśasyate || 7.2 ||
<vāstupūjāprakāraḥ>
kuśaiḥ kāśaistṛṇaiścāpi vāstunāthasya vigraham|
kṛtvā sarvāṅgasampūrṇaṃ vāsasā bhūṣaṇena ca || 7.3 ||
kusumairapyalaṅkṛtya maṇḍape dakṣiṇe bhuvi|
saptadarbhakṛtaṃ kūrcaṃ kṛtvorasi niyojayet || 7.4 ||
puṇyāhavāribhiḥ prokṣya pūjayedvāstunāyakam|
vāstvīśāṣṭākṣareṇaiva kūrte āvāhya deśikaḥ || 7.5 ||
`asunīte'ti mantreṇa kuryātprāṇaṃ pratiṣṭhitam|
ardhyaiḥ pādyaistathācāmairgandhaiḥ puṣpaistathākṣataiḥ || 7.6 ||
dhūpairdīpaiśca naivedyaistāmbūlairvāsanāyutaiḥ|
arcayitvā yathāśāstraṃ toṣayitvā yathāvasu || 7.7 ||
<vāstupūjāṅgahomaḥ vāstuśeṣaparityāgādikañca>
paścime vāstunāthasya juhuyājjātavedasi|
`pañcopaniṣadai'rmantraiḥ sarpiṣā juhuyācchatam || 7.8 ||
śamyapāmārgakhadirasamidbhiśca yathākramam|
`vāstvīśa'mūlamantreṇa hyaṣṭāvaṣṭau pṛthak pṛthak || 7.9 ||
`vāstospate pratītyādi' ṛgbhiścāpi catasṛbhiḥ|
caruṇā juhuyādagnau tato `vāstvīśa'mūlataḥ || 7.10 ||
aṣṭottaraśataṃ vārān sarpiṣā juhuyātpunaḥ|
`idaṃ viṣṇu'riti śrutyā hutvā pūrṇāhutiṃ tataḥ || 7.11 ||
dagdhvā vāstupratikṛtiṃ `parivāje'ti vai ṛcā|
paryagnikaraṇaṃ kṛtvā dhāma tanmaṇḍapādikam || 7.12 ||
īśānabhāge grāmasya vāstuśeṣaṃ parityajet|
tataḥ snātvā vidhānena puṇyāhaprokṣaṇaṃ caret || 7.13 ||
<mānādiprayuktadoṣaśāntiprakāraḥ>
mānonmānamāṇāderhānivṛddhyaghaśāntaye|
pratimālayapīṭhānāṃ śāntihomaṃ samācaret || 7.14 ||
pīṭhe bimbāni vinyasya kṣālayitvā śubhodakaiḥ|
pīṭhāni ca tathā dhāmādyaliṃpedgomayādibhiḥ || 7.15 ||
bimbānāṃ purato vahniṃ pratiṣṭhāpya samīdalaiḥ|
sākṣatairvyāhṛtībhiśca homaścāṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 7.16 ||
pūrṇāhutyavasāne tu vahnyupasthānamācaret|
`namastubhyaṃ bhagavate jātavedaḥsvarūpiṇe || 7.17 ||
nārāyaṇāya havyasya kavyasya ca yathākramam|
hotre yaṣṭavyadevānāmātmane paramātmane || 7.18 ||
sannidhatsva ciraṃ deva pratimāyāṃ hitāya naḥ|'
ityupasthāya medhāvī śuddhasnapanamācaret || 7.19 ||
<bimbādhivāsakrame ghṛtasnānaprakāraḥ>
bimbānāṃ purataḥ kuṃbhaṃ dānyapīṭhe niveśayet|
sūtraveṣṭitasarvāṅgaṃ gandhavastrādyalaṅkṛtam || 7.20 ||
āḍhakadvayamātreṇa godhṛtena prapūritam|
sakūrcaṃ pallavayutaṃ śarāveṇāvakuṇṭhitam || 7.21 ||
saṃsthāpya kṛtvā puṇyāhaṃ prokṣayitvā yathāvidhi|
vāsudevaṃ samāvāhya `dvādaśākṣara'vidyayā || 7.22 ||
abhyarcya gandhapuṣpādyairnaivedyāntaṃ yathākramam|
`ghṛtaṃ mimikṣi' mantreṇa ghṛtena snapanaṃ caret || 7.23 ||
`mānastoke'ti mantreṇa snāpayeduṣṇavāribhiḥ|
tato bimbāni sarvāṇi bhūṣiyitvā navāmbaraiḥ || 7.24 ||
bhūṣaṇairgandhamālyaiśca yathāvidhi yathāvasu|
<jalādhivāsaprakāraḥ>
jalādhivāsaṃ kurvīta nadyādau maṅgalodake || 7.25 ||
jalādhivāsāyogyānāṃ trivastukamṛdātmanām|
citrāṇāṃ paṭajānāṃ vā gurūṇāṃ ca śilādiṣu || 7.26 ||
chāyādhivāsaṃ kurvīta pratiṣṭhatantrasāravit|
jaladroṇyāṃ kaṭāhe vā samuddhṛtya mahajjalam || 7.27 ||
dhānyarāśau vinikṣipya gandhaistadadhivāsya ca|
gaṅgādyāssaritastatra samāvāhya samarcayet || 7.28 ||
aṣṭāviṃśatibhirdarbhaiḥ kṛtaṃ kūrcaṃ vinikṣipet|
tatra devaṃ samāvāhya pūjayitvā yathāvidhi || 7.29 ||
saṃhārakramamācāryaḥ saṃsmarettatra tantravit|
cakramudrāṃ darśayitvā rakṣākumbhārcanaṃ caret || 7.30 ||
dhānyapīṭhe'tha tatpārśve toyapūrṇān ghaṭān nava|
sālaṅkārān sakarakān saratnān lohasaṃyutān || 7.31 ||
saṃsthāpya madhye brahmāṇaṃ karake cāyudheśvaram|
indrādidikpatīnanyakalaśeṣvarcayed budhaḥ || 7.32 ||
sarvāṇyaṅgāni bimbasya veṣṭayennavavāsasā|
`rakṣohaṇe'ti mantreṇa siddhārthān vikiredbudhaḥ || 7.33 ||
devīnāṃ tu śriyādīnāṃ trayoviṃśatibhiḥ kuśaiḥ|
pañcaviṃśatibhirdarbhaiḥ rudrasya parameṣṭhinaḥ || 7.34 ||
dvāviṃśadbhiḥ kuśaiḥ kūrcaṃ kuryādanyāmṛtāsinām|
āropya yāne bimbāni vedavādyasamanvitam || 7.35 ||
sarvamaṅgalasaṃyuktaṃ prāpayecca nadītaṭam|
tīre niveśya nadyādeḥ prokṣya puṇyāhavāriṇā || 7.36 ||
śoṣayedvāyubījena `vahnimantreṇa' dāhayet|
sudhākṣareṇa saṃplāpya gaṅgāmāvāhya pūjayet || 7.37 ||
evaṃ śuddhe nadītoye pīṭhaṃ tatra vinikṣipet|
kalpayitvā yogapīṭhaṃ bimbaṃ prākśirasaṃ nyaset || 7.38 ||
śāyayecca sambhyarcya saṃhāraṃ cintayettataḥ|
cakramudrāṃ pradarśyātha nadītīre yathāpuram || 7.39 ||
rakṣākumbhārcanaṃ kuryāt siddhārthān dikṣupātayet|
āropayedanirvāṇān dīpān api suvarcasaḥ || 7.40 ||
trayī pravartanaṃ kuryāt brāhmaṇaiḥ vedapāragaiḥ|
tasmin jale na kurvīran snānapānādikāḥ kriyāḥ || 7.41 ||
vādyāni ca vicitrāṇi ghoṣayeṣuḥ samantataḥ|
trirātramekarātraṃ vā yadvā yāmaṃ muhūrtakam || 7.42 ||
jalādhivāsaṃ kurvīta tatra nadyādyasaṃbhave|
kaṭāhe vā jaladroṇyāṃ bimbānāmadhivāsanam || 7.43 ||
|| iti bhārgavatantre saptamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<8>
|| aṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<pratiṣṭhāṅgabhūtapratimānetronmīlanādinirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<yāgamaṇḍapālaṅkaraṇaprakāraḥ>
śrīrāmaḥ
yāgamaṇḍapasādya yajamānena deśikaḥ|
sthūṇāṃśca maṇḍape tasmin vāsobhirveṣṭayennavaiḥ || 8.1 ||
vitānayecchubhairvastraiḥ patākābhiśca bhūṣayet|
muktādāmabhiranyaiśca kadalīkramukādibhiḥ || 8.2 ||
saphalairdarbhamālābhirmaṇikāñcanabhūṣaṇaiḥ|
śobhayenmaṇḍapaṃ sarvaṃ toraṇāni ca bandhayet || 8.3 ||
khātvā dvitālaṃ dharaṇīṃ dvāre dvāre caturdaśam|
sthāpayedapi pūrvādipatākāścāpi bandhayet || 8.4 ||
aśvatthodumbaravaṭaplakṣaklṛptāni vai kramāt|
śobhayitvā yathādṛśyaṃ dīpānuddīpya sarvataḥ || 8.5 ||
<adhivāsitabimbotthāpanam yāgamaṇḍapānayanaṃ ca>
aparāhṇe tu samprāpte kāle saṃkalpite gate|
utthāpya bimbānyudakāt kumbhācca kalaśādapi || 8.6 ||
udvāsya devatāḥ sarvāḥ pratimāstīraviṣṭare|
niveśya prāṅmukhaṃ śuddhairvāribhiḥ kṣālayedguruḥ || 8.7 ||
lohajāni ca bimbāni tintriṇīphalavāriṇā|
vastrābharaṇapuṣpādyairalaṅkṛtya vicakṣaṇaḥ || 8.8 ||
yānamāropya viprendrairvedavādyādimaṅgalaiḥ|
prādakṣiṇyena vai dhāmno gamayedyāgamaṇḍapam || 8.9 ||
uttare viṣṭare bimbaṃ prāṅmukhaṃ viniveśayet|
tata utthāpya kūrcāni bimbe tāṃ śaktimudvaset || 8.10 ||
kumbhebhyaḥ devatāḥ sarvāḥ pūrvavacca visarjayet|
<pratimānayanonmīlanakramaḥ>
nayononmīlanaṃ kuryāt bimbānāṃ tu yathāvidhi || 8.11 ||
sauvarṇaṃ rājataṃ cāpi pātrayugmaṃ śalākayā|
rājatyā svarṇamayyā ca dhānyarāśau niveśayet || 8.12 ||
sauvarṇaṃ pūrvadigbhāge paścime rājataṃ nyaset|
paritaḥ kalaśānaṣṭau caṇakaiḥ paripūritān || 8.13 ||
sthāpayedvācayetpaścāt puṇyāhaṃ prokṣayedapi|
āvāhayenmadhughaṭe `udvaṃya tamase'tyṛcā || 8.14 ||
ādityamitare'pīnduṃ `somodhenvi'ti vidyayā|
sampūjya gandhapuṣpādyairdve pātre saśalākike || 8.15 ||
`madhutrayeṇa' ca madhu `savirājeti' vai ghṛtam|
kanyakārucirāścāṣṭau gāḥ savatsāḥ manoharāḥ || 8.16 ||
sthāpayitvātha tatpārśve trayī ghoṣaṃ pravartayet|
śalākayā ca sauvarṇyā madhvāktamukhayā guruḥ || 8.17 ||
unmīlya dakṣiṇaṃ netraṃ rājatyā ca ghṛtāktayā|
unmīlanaṃ tu vāmākṣṇaḥ dakṣiṇottarayormanuḥ || 8.18 ||
`citraṃ devā' ca `tacchakṣu'riti mantradvayaṃ smṛtam|
evamunmīlya netre dve ācchādanapaṭaṃ tyajet || 8.19 ||
makarandaśca sarpiśca caṇakaṃ cāpi kanyakāḥ|
gāḥ savatsāśca devāya darśayeddeśikottamaḥ || 8.20 ||
sarveṣāmapi bimbānāmevamunmīlanaṃ dṛśām|
<pratisarabandhanāvasaranirdeśaḥ>
kautukaṃ tu kare dhāryaṃ snapane'mbvadhivāsane || 8.21 ||
pratiṣṭhāyāṃ ca bimbānām ācāryasyāpi ṛtvijām|
tatprakāramito vakṣye śṛṇu kumbhasamudbhava || 8.22 ||
<pratisaraparikalpanam>
sauvarṇaṃ vātha kauśeyaṃ kārpāsaṃ vā yathāvasu|
saptabhirpañcabhirvāpi sūtraiḥ pratisaraṃ bhaveta || 8.23 ||
niṣkapramāṇasvarṇena kautukaṃ parikalpayet|
<kautukasaṃskāraḥ kautukabandhanaprakāraśca>
kamale taṇḍulaiḥ pūrṇe tāmbūlīdalaśobhite || 8.24 ||
kadalīnārikerādyaiḥ phalaiśca parimaṇḍite|
vinyasya tatra sūtrāṇi prokṣya puṇyāhavāribhiḥ || 8.25 ||
sudarśanaṃ hetirājaṃ tatrāvāhya samarcayet|
sudarśanaṣaḍarṇena saptavārābhimantraṇam || 8.26 ||
aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ tu candanakṣodavāriṇā|
āliṃpet kautukaṃ samyagaparājitavidyayā || 8.27 ||
mūlamantreṇa devasya vadhnīyāddakṣiṇe kare|
devīnāṃ tu śriyādīnāṃ vāme kautukamaṅgalam || 8.28 ||
ācāryāṇāṃ mūrtipānāmaṅkurārpaṇakarmaṇi|
kautukaṃ dakṣiṇe haste yena dīkṣā prasidhyati || 8.29 ||
mantrastu devadevīnāmanyāsāṃ vā munīśvara|
bandhane sva sva vidyaiva cakramantrābhimantraṇam || 8.30 ||
viśvetāteti mantreṇa bandhanaṃ kautuke guroḥ|
ṛtvijāmapi sarveṣāṃ yo brahmetyabhimantraṇam || 8.31 ||
vṛhatsāmeti mantreṇa rakṣaiṣāṃ dhūpabhasmanā|
praṇamya puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ dvādaśākṣaravidyayā || 8.32 ||
<snapanakuṃbhānāṃ sthāpanakramaḥ>
snānapīṭhe tu bimbāni samāropya gurūttamaḥ|
kalaśān saptadaśa ca dhānyapīṭhe niveśayet || 8.33 ||
ghṛtakumbhaṃ pade brāhme soṣṇamambu divaspatau|
phalodakaṃ yamapade snānīyaṃ vāruṇe diśi || 8.34 ||
saumye bhavedakṣatāmbu vahnau tu maṇijīvanam|
yātudhāne lohatīrthaṃ gandhatīrthaṃ tu mārute || 8.35 ||
somaiśānayorakṣatayave prācyāṃ pādāmbu kalpanam|
ardhyatīrthaṃ pade yāmye ācāmaṃ vāruṇe diśi || 8.36 |
pañcagavyamudīcīne dadhyagnau nairṛte payaḥ|
mārute madhu bhūteśe kāṣāyāmbuprakalpanam || 8.37 ||
<homābhiṣecanakramaḥ>
devatāvāhanaṃ kṛtvā tattannāmabhiraṣṭadhā|
āvṛtya juhuyādagnau sampātaṃ tatra secayet || 8.38 ||
mantrastu vaiṣṇavaḥ sūktaḥ puṃsūktenābhiṣecanam|
ghṛtakumbhasya mantrastu gāyatrī vaiṣṇavī matā || 8.39 ||
<bimbādhivāsanaprakāraḥ>
śāyayedvedikāmadhye śālitaṇḍulakalpite|
satile mañcamāropya kṛṣṇājinapariṣkṛtam || 8.40 ||
tūlikāṃ tatra vinyasya ratnakaṃbalamaṇḍitām|
kṣaumaṃ ca citravastraṃ ca gaṇḍūṣāṇi ca vinyaset || 8.41 ||
śiropadhānaṃ ca tathā sugandhairadhivāsayet|
`idaṃviṣṇu'riti prāyairmantraiḥ paramapāvanaiḥ || 8.42 ||
śāyayeddevadeveśaṃ sāṃgaṃ vai dakṣiṇe śiraḥ|
kambalaiśca dukūlaiśca pratimāṃ chādayedguruḥ || 8.43 ||
<kumbhasthāpanaprakāraḥ>
āśāsvaṣṭasvaṣṭakumbhān sūtravastrādiveṣṭitān|
sakūrcān pallavayutān saratnān svarṇasaṃyutān || 8.44 ||
veṣṭitān vastrayugmena yadvaikena samaṣṭinā|
gandhodakena sampūrṇān dhānyapīṭhe niveśayet || 8.45 ||
śālīnāṃ taṇḍulānūrdhvaṃ teṣāmūrdhvaṃ tilaṃ bhavet|
antarāntaravastrāḍhyaṃ dhānyapīṭhamidaṃ smṛtam || 8.46 ||
tattatpratikṛtiṃ tatra sauvarṇīṃ viniveśayet|
kumbhānāmantarāleṣu vinyaseṣṭamaṅgalān || 8.47 ||
apidhāya śaraveṇa nālikerādibhiḥ phalaiḥ|
alaṅkṛtya yathāyogyaṃ mahākumbhaṃ tu paścime || 8.48 ||
karakeṇa samāyuktaṃ navaratnasamanvitam|
sarvalohayutaṃ sarvagandhayuktaṃ sakūrcakam || 8.49 ||
piplacchadasaṃyuktaṃ mūlapratimayānvitam|
sūtraveṣṭatasarvāṅgaṃ jambīraphalasāntaram || 8.50 ||
vedikāyāṃ dhānyapīṭhe saśarāvaṃ niveśayet|
alaṅkuryādviśeṣeṇa vastrayugmena veṣṭayet || 8.51 ||
tasya dakṣiṇapārśve tu tādṛśaṃ karakaṃ nyaset|
toraṇānāmubhayataḥ dvārakumbhau niveśayet || 8.52 ||
alaṅkuryādyathāyogaṃ pūrayedgandhavāriṇā|
mahākumbhaṃ ca karakaṃ pūrayedgandhavāribhiḥ || 8.53 ||
evaṃ saṃsthāpya medhāvī vedaghoṣe pravartite|
tūryavāditraninade pravṛddhe'ti kutūhale || 8.54 ||
āvāhayenmahākumbhe vāsudevaṃ sanātanam|
|| iti bhārgavatantre'ṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<9>
|| navamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<pratiṣṭhāṅgakuṃbhārcanādividhinirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<kuṃbhatoraṇādyārādhyadevatāvivekaḥ>
śrīrāmaḥ
gambhīravedanirghoṣavāditradhvanimaṇḍite|
digantare dvijagaṇe prasannavadanānvite || 9.1 ||
maṅgalālāpacature saṃhṛṣṭavanitājane|
ākāśe nirmale jyotsnāmaṅgale candramaṇḍale || 9.2 ||
sukhasparśe gandhavahe nirmale mānase guroḥ|
sumuhūrte śubhe lagne kumbhāvāhanamācaret || 9.3 ||
puṇyāhaṃ vācayet sārdhaṃ brāhmaṇaiḥ vedapāragaiḥ|
prokṣayedakhilān kuṃbhān yāgadravyāṇi toraṇān || 9.4 ||
bhūtaśuddhiṃ purā kṛtvā mānasaṃ yāgamācaret|
pūrvasmin toraṇe pūjyo mahendraḥ pākaśāsanaḥ || 9.5 ||
dhvajayorantarayostatra pārśvayorveṣṭayedguruḥ|
kumudaṃ kumudākṣaṃ ca paurvāparyeṇa pūjayet || 9.6 ||
kuṃbhayorubhayostatra pūjayetpūrṇapuṣkarau|
dharmarājaṃ tathā yāmye toraṇe dhvajayostathā || 9.7 ||
puṇḍarīko vāmanaśca kuṃbhayorubhayorapi|
ānando nandanaścobhau pūjanīyau yathākramam || 9.8 ||
vāruṇe toraṇe cāpi varuṇaṃ devamarcayet|
pārśvayordhvajayordevau śaṅkuśrutisunetrakau || 9.9 ||
kuṃbhayorvīrasenaṃ ca suṣeṇaṃ ca samarcayet|
saumye tu toraṇe somaṃ baddhayordhvajayostayoḥ || 9.10 ||
sumukhaṃ supratiṣṭhaṃ ca sambhavaprabhavau tathā|
kumbhayorarcayaiddevau dakṣiṇāditrayo dhvajāḥ || 9.11 ||
pītanīlāstathā śvetāḥ kalpitāḥ kalaśodbhava|
<tattadvedapārāyaṇasthānanirdeśaḥ>
ṛcaṃ prācyāṃ yajuryāmye sāmanāma ca vāruṇe || 9.12 ||
atharvaṇaṃ tathodīcyāṃ pāṭhayedbrāhmaṇottamaḥ|
<kuṃbhe bhagavadarcanaṃ homaprakāraśca>
āvāhanaṃ tu sarvatra yogapīṭhapurassaram || 9.13 ||
śāyitānāṃ tu bimbānāṃ paritaḥ sthāpiteṣvapi|
kumbheṣvāvāhanaṃ kuryāt prāgādyāśāpurassaram || 9.14 ||
viṣṇvādimūrtiraṣṭau ca tadasyetyādibhiḥ kramāt|
aṣṭabhi`rviṣṇusūkta'sya teṣāṃ pūjā yathākramam || 9.15 ||
tatastu paramātmānaṃ vāsudevaṃ sanātanam|
mahākumbhe samāvāhya `dvādaśākṣara'vidyayā || 9.16 ||
sudarśanaṃ ca karake sahasrādityasannibham|
āvāhya pūjayeddhīmān ardhyādyaiḥ sva sva vidyayā || 9.17 ||
ardhyaṃ pādyaṃ tathācāmaṃ snānavastropavītake|
gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ dhūpadīpau arcanaṃ cākṣataṃ stutiḥ || 9.18 ||
naivedyaṃ paramānnādi tāmbūlaṃ gandhavastubhiḥ|
nīrājanaṃ stutiścānte namaskāraṃ pradakṣiṇam || 9.19 ||
evaṃ samāpya tāṃ pūjāṃ tataḥ kuṇḍe'gnimarcayet|
samidbhiścarubhiścājyairvījaiścāhutimācaret || 9.20 ||
<bhagavataḥ vāsudevasya dhyānaprakāraḥ>
agastyaḥ
rāma rāma jagannātha vāsudeva sanātana|
kathaṃ dhyāyedvāsudevaṃ brūhi me kṛpayā vibho || 9.21 ||
śrīrāmaḥ
vāsudevamajaṃ śāntaṃ koṭyarkendvagnisannibhavam|
anekahastacaraṇamanekākṣiśiromukham || 9.22 ||
atīndriyamamaryādam atyadbhutavapurdharam|
bhāsayantaṃ diśaḥ sarvāḥ dehaniryatprabhācayaiḥ || 9.23 ||
anekābharaṇairyuktamanekāyudhamaṇḍitam|
nānāvarṇamayaṃ devaṃ svayaṃ surabhivigraham || 9.24 ||
ṛṣibhiśca marīcyādyairbrahmādyairapi daivateḥ|
acintyamahimānaṃ tamindirādyairabhiṣṭutam || 9.25 ||
ko vā dhyātuṃ kṣamo loke ko vā stotuṃ bhuvi kṣamaḥ|
tathāpi parameśānaṃ puruṣaṃ bhaktavatsalam || 9.26 ||
caturbhujamudārāṅgaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam|
kirīṭahārakaṭakakāñcīkuṇḍalamaṇḍitam || 9.27 ||
śrīvatsakaustubhasvacchayajñasūtrāṅgulīyakam|
divyapītāmbaradharaṃ kalakvaṇitanūpuram || 9.28 ||
aṣṭabhiścāpi devībhiḥ prāñcalībhiśca sevitam|
pakṣīndraviṣvaksenābhyāṃ parivāraiśca sevitam || 9.29 ||
atiprasannavadanaṃ bālakaṃ tilakojvalam|
abhayapradamāsīnaṃ śeṣabhogāsane śubhe || 9.30 ||
divyagandhaviliptāṅgaṃ nānāmālyopaśobhitam|
smitena vijitajyotsnaṃ nīlanīrajalocanam || 9.31 ||
kambukaṇṭhaṃ mahāsatvaṃ pīnottuṅgāṃsaśobhitam|
uttuṃgapīnavipulasamasnigdhamahorasam || 9.32 ||
puṣṭodaraṃ magnanābhiṃ pṛthuśroṇībharāñcitam|
ūrudvayakṛtadvandvaraṃ bhāsaṃraṃbhalakṣitam || 9.33 ||
caraṇadvandvavijitavikasatsarasīruham|
aṅguṣṭhanakhaniryadbhirbhānubhirjitabhāskaram || 9.34 ||
ājānudīrghaiḥ pṛthulaiḥ hastairāyudhadhāriṇam|
dhyātvaivaṃ vāsudevaṃ taṃ kuryādāvāhanakriyām || 9.35 ||
<tattaddevānāṃ homakramanirdeśaḥ>
mathitaṃ maṇijaṃ yadvā laukikaṃ vā hutāśanam|
`dvādaśākṣara'mantreṇa prokṣayitvā sasarpiṣā || 9.36 ||
paryagnikaraṇaṃ kuryāt kuṇḍānāṃ darbhamuṣṭibhiḥ|
uddīpya jvalanaṃ samyak `parivājatī'tyṛcā || 9.37 ||
saṃskuryātpaścime kuṇḍe jvalantaṃ jālavedasam|
prāgādiṣu ca kuṇḍeṣu pratyak kuṇḍāt samāharet || 9.38 ||
agnimadhye tu kuṇḍeṣu yogapīṭhaṃ prakalpayet|
śunāsīre vāsudevaṃ yāmye saṅkarṣaṇaṃ tathā || 9.39 ||
pradyumnaṃ vāruṇe kuṇḍe aniruddhamudakpade|
evamāvāhya deveśaṃ pūjayetsarpiṣā tataḥ || 9.40 ||
<homakrame samidhādivivekaḥ>
catasraḥ samidhaḥ proktā prāgādiṣu yathākramam|
palāśakhadirāśvatthavilvodumbarabhūruhāḥ || 9.41 ||
alābhe sarvakuṇḍeṣu pālāśyekaiva śasyate|
pāyasaṃ kṛsaraṃ cāpi guḍānnaṃ ca yathākramam || 9.42 ||
haridrānnaṃ ca pūrvādikuṇḍeṣu caravaḥ smṛtāḥ|
pāyasānnena vā kuryāt śuddhānnenāthavā bhavet || 9.43 ||
tilaśāliyavā veṇurhome bījānyanukramāt|
tilairvā sarvakuṇḍeṣu bījālābhe samācaret || 9.44 ||
vāsudevādibhirmantrairhomāḥ prāgādivahniṣu|
samidbhirājyaiścarubhiḥ bījaiḥ kāryāśca mūrtipaiḥ || 9.45 ||
aṣṭottaraśataṃ homaḥ pratyekaṃ kalaśodbhava|
evaṃ juhvatsu ṛtvikṣu mūrtihomaṃ yathākramam || 9.46 ||
<tattvahomavidhiḥ>
tattvahomaṃ svayaṃ kuryāt brāhmakuṇḍe gurūttamaḥ|
purastāddevadevasya bimbaṃ bhūtamayaṃ smaret || 9.47 ||
ardhyādyairupacaryātha tattvasaṃhāramācaret|
saṃharetpṛthivīmapsu tāṃ vahnau taṃ ca mārute || 9.48 ||
tamākāśe ca manasi tacca tanmātrayā saha|
tadīyaiścāpi tattvaiśca tanmano'haṃkṛtau haret || 9.49 ||
ahaṃkṛtiṃ tato buddhau prakṛtau tāṃ samāharet|
tāṃ ca jīve tato'bhyarcya punarutpādayedguruḥ || 9.50 ||
prācīnakuṇḍe juhuyāt tattvāni kapilāghṛtaiḥ|
varmaṇā pariṣicyādau sṛṣṭipanthānamāsthitaḥ || 9.51 ||
tattvavyāhṛtibhiḥ kuryāt pṛthagaṣṭottaraṃ śatam|
aṣṭāviṃśati vāṣṭau vā pratimantrāhutirbhavet || 9.52 ||
<makārādyakṣaratattvānāṃ pratimāṅgeṣu nyāsakramaḥ>
jīvamantro makāraḥ syāt bhakāraḥ prakṛtirbhavet|
buddhitattvaṃ bakāraḥ syāt phakāraḥ syādahaṃkṛtiḥ || 9.53 ||
manastattvaṃ pakāraḥ syāt pratimāhṛdayasthitāḥ|
nakāraśśabdatanmātraṃ śrotraikaviṣayaṃ bhavet || 9.54 ||
dhakāraḥ sparśatanmātraṃ tvaci nityaṃ pratiṣṭhitam|
dakāro rūpatanmātraṃ netrayorviṣayaṃ bhavet || 9.55 ||
thakāro rasatanmātraṃ jihvāyāmeva vartate|
takāro gandhatanmātraṃ vartate nāsikāñcale || 9.56 ||
ṇakāraḥ śrotratattvaṃ syāt śrotrayornityamāvaset|
ḍhakārastu tvagātmā syāt tvaci sarvatra vartate || 9.57 ||
ḍakāro netra tattvātmā nityaṃ nayanayorvaset|
jihvātmā tu ṭhakāraḥ syāt jihvārūpaṃ jagatraye || 9.58 ||
ghrāṇātmā tu ṭakāraḥ syāt nāsikāgre niveśitaḥ|
ñakāro vāṅmayo varṇo vāci nityaṃ pratiṣṭhitaḥ || 9.59 ||
jhakāraḥ karatattvaṃ syāt karayorvidhinirmitaḥ|
jakāraḥ pādatattvārṇaḥ nityaṃ pādatale sthitaḥ || 9.60 ||
chakāraḥ pāyutattvātmā pāyumūlamupāśritaḥ|
cakāro mehanātmā syāt mehane sanniveśitaḥ || 9.61 ||
ṅakāraḥ pṛthivītattvam pādājjānupravartakam|
aptattvaṃ tu dhakārassyāt jānorguhyāvadhisthitaḥ || 9.62 ||
tejastattvaṃ gakāraḥ syāta guhyādānābhi vartate|
khakāro vāyutattvātmā nābhernāsāntamāsthitaḥ || 9.63 ||
kakāro'mbaratattvātmā tato brahmabilāvadhi|
evaṃ tattvākṣakaḥ proktaḥ praṇavādi samuddharet || 9.64 ||
praṇavastattvamantraśca natiśca sthānanāma ca|
caturthyantaṃ samuddhṛtya vahnijāyāṃ gurūttamaḥ || 9.65 ||
hutvā sampātamāhṛtya tattadaṅgeṣu secayet|
<pratimāyāṃ śaktiniveśaprakāraḥ>
evaṃ hutvā tataḥ prāṇavāyuhomaṃ samācaret || 9.66 ||
prāṇaṃ hutvā suṣumnāyāmilāyāṃ tadanantaram|
apānaṃ piṅgalāyāntu samānaṃ gandhasaṃjñike || 9.67 ||
udānaṃ hastijihvāyāṃ vyānavāyuṃ niveśayet|
pūṣāyāmapi nāgendraṃ yaśasvinyāṃ tu kacchapam || 9.68 ||
alambusāyāṃ kṛkaraṃ devadattaṃ kuhūpade|
dhanañjayaṃ tu kaiśinyāṃ hṛtvā hṛtvā svavidyayā || 9.69 ||
sampātaṃ secayedbimbe tatra śaktiṃ niveśayet|
saṅkhaṃ cakraṃ gadāpadmaṃ śārṅgaṃ śrīvatsakaustubhe || 9.70 ||
vanamālāṃ khageśaṃ ca hutvā sampātasecanam|
`asunīte'ti mantreṇa aṣṭottaraśatāhutīḥ || 9.71 ||
hṛtvā prāṇapratiṣṭhārṇa śiraskenājyasecanam|
sampātasecane kāle tattacchaktiṃ niveśayet || 9.72 ||
<caṇḍādiparivāṇām adhivāsanādividhiḥ>
caṇḍādīnāṃ ca pīṭhānāṃ parivāragaṇasya ca|
anyeṣāmapi sarveṣāṃ dhānyapīṭhe'dhivāsanam || 9.73 ||
kṛtvā tattadvidyayaiva hutvā cāṣṭottaraṃ śatam|
sarpiṣā'hṛtya sampātaṃ tattadbimbeṣu secayet || 9.74 ||
evaṃ niravaśeṣeṇa kṛte home yathāvidhi|
śāntihomaṃ tataḥ kuryāt sarvadoṣāpanuttaye || 9.75 ||
oṃ bhūrbhuvaḥ suvaḥ svāhetyaṣṭottarasahasrakam|
aṣṭottaraśataṃ yadvā sarpiṣā pūrvavarhiṣi || 9.76 ||
hutbā sampātamādāya sarveṣvaṅgeṣu secayet|
<ekaberabahaberapratiṣṭhāvidhau viśeṣaḥ>
brāhme pade sthāpanīye dhruvabere munīśvara || 9.77 ||
vedikādighaṭāmbobhirabliṅgaiḥ prokṣayettataḥ|
āgneyādighaṭāmbhobhi`rviṣṇugāyatriyo'kṣaṇam || 9.78 ||
bahubere tu sadanaṃ praviśyaivokṣaṇaṃ bhavet|
tataścaturvidhānnaṃ ca haraye vinivedayet || 9.79 ||
praṇavenopacārāṃstān sarvānapi samācaret|
<balisamarpaṇavidhiḥ>
bimbamutthāpya tejasvī prāṅmukhaṃ viniveśya ca || 9.80 ||
abhyarcya gandhapuṣpādyaiḥ balidānaṃ samācaret|
māṣodanena bhūtebhyaḥ sarvebhyo'pi yathāvidhi || 9.81 ||
gomayālepite bhūmitale samyagalaṅkṛte|
ādyāśca karmajāścaiva bhūtāḥ prāgādiṣu sthitāḥ || 9.82 ||
parvateṣvapyaraṇyeṣu vṛkṣāgreṣu vasanti ye|
ālayeṣu ca tīrtheṣu ṛṣīṇāmāśrameṣu ca || 9.83 ||
kūpeṣu ca taṭākeṣu ye vasanti gṛhādiṣu|
uttamāḥ madhyamāścāpi rājasāstāmasāstu vā || 9.84 ||
balyarthinaḥ samāyāntu tebhyaḥ pūjāṃ karomyaham|
idamardhyamidaṃ pādyamidamācamanīyakam || 9.85 ||
snānametat sagandhodaṃ vastramettasunirmalam|
idaṃ gandhamidaṃ puṣpam idaṃ dhūpaṃ sadīpakam || 9.86 ||
anarghamakṣatamidaṃ gṛhṇantu balidevatāḥ|
māṣaudanaṃ tat saghṛtaṃ dadhimiśraṃ guḍānvitam || 9.87 ||
bhakṣyaiśca vividhairyuktaṃ pāyasena ca miśritam|
tāmbūlapūgasahitaṃ madhudravasamanvitam || 9.88 ||
bhoktuṃ tripuruṣairyojñaṃ miśrayetpayasā gavām|
vibhajya tatttridhā dhīmāna balidānaṃ samācaret || 9.89 ||
kurvanti vighnaṃ ye bhūtāḥ dharmakāryeṣu ye bhuvi|
ye haranti ca puṇyāni tebhyastebhyaḥ ayaṃ baliḥ || 9.90 ||
viniyujya ca kāryeṣu phalārtha vindate ta ye|
rājasā madhyamaguṇāḥ tebhyastebhyaḥ ayaṃ baliḥ || 9.91 ||
yāgādiṣu pravṛtteṣu dṛṣṭvā tuṣṭimavāpnuyuḥ|
ye bhūtāḥ uttamaguṇāḥ tebhyastebhyaḥ ayaṃ baliḥ || 9.92 ||
<pratiṣṭhāsthānāt gantuṃ balidevaprārthanākramaḥ>
dattvaiva balimācāryaḥ prārthayedbalidevatāḥ|
pratiṣṭhā devadevasya kriyate paramātmanaḥ || 9.93 ||
itaḥ prabhṛti yuṣmabhirnaiṣā vastuṃ kṣamā mahī|
tasmādanyatra gacchadhvaṃ balidānena toṣitāḥ || 9.94 ||
deśikaḥ prārthayedevaṃ vinīto balidevatāḥ|
te tuṣṭāḥ balidānena prārthitāḥ yānti niścayam || 9.95 ||
<piṇḍikādyadhivāsaḥ tadaṅgabhūtahomaśca>
piṇḍikāṃ devadevasya dhānyapīṭhe'dhivāsayet|
ratnānyapi ca lohāni dhānyānyapyadhivāsayet || 9.96 ||
pṛthak pṛthak bhājaneṣu vāsobhirveṣṭayennavaiḥ|
viṣṇugāyatriyājyena homaścāṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 9.97 ||
<pratisarabandhaḥ pūrṇāhutikramaḥ maṅgalasūcakalakṣaṇāni ca>
hemasūtraṃ pratisaraṃ pratiṣṭhārtha gurūttamaḥ|
bandhayeddevadevasya haste puṇyāhapūrvakam || 9.98 ||
tataḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ kuryuḥ kuṇḍe kuṇḍe yathākramam|
puṇyagandhavaho dhūmaḥ jvālādakṣiṇamaṇḍalā || 9.99 ||
agniḥ kanakavarṇābhaḥ yadvā kamalasannibhaḥ|
ācāryasya manastuṣṭirime maṅgalasūcakāḥ || 9.100 ||
<rātriyāpanakramaḥ>
gītavāditranirghoṣaiḥ brahmaghoṣaiśca maṅgalaiḥ|
nayeyustāṃ niśāmṛtvik yajamānagurūttamāḥ || 9.101 ||
|| iti bhārgavatantre navamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<10>
|| daśamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<pratiṣṭhākrame pratimāpīṭhasthāpanādinirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<garbhagṛhe bhagavadānayanam ādhāraśilāsthāpanañca>
śrīrāmaḥ
prabhātāyāṃ tu śarvaryāṃ nirmale bhāskarodaye|
śubhe muhūrte samprāpte mañcādutthāpayeddharim || 10.1 ||
arcayedgandhapuṣpādyaiḥ alaṅkuryādviśeṣataḥ|
garbhamandiramāsādya tatrādhāraśilāṃ kṣipet || 10.2 ||
āyāmasamavistārāṃ yadvā vṛttāṃ manoharām|
sudhayā tāṃ dṛḍhīkṛtya ratnanyāsaṃ samācaret || 10.3 ||
<ādhārapīṭhagarte bījaratnanyāsakramaḥ>
yavo brīhiśca niṣpāvaḥ priyaṅgutilamāṣakāḥ|
nīvārāḥ śālayaścāṣṭau garte prāgādi vinyaset || 10.4 ||
prathamāvaraṇe paścāt dvitīyāvaraṇe tathā|
vajramauktikavaiḍūryavālajasphaṭikānyapi || 10.5 ||
puṣparāgaṃ candrakāntam indranīlaṃ kramānnyaset|
tṛtīyāvaraṇe svarṇarajatāyastrapūṇi ca || 10.6 ||
tāmrasīsakakāṃsyāni kacchapaṃ svarṇanirmitam|
krameṇa nikṣipedgarte madhyagarte vinikṣipet || 10.7 ||
śālibījaṃ pāradaṃ ca navaratnāni kāñcanam|
evaṃ vinyasya medhāvī viṣṇugāyatrividyayā || 10.8 ||
apidhāya strīśilayā śrīsūktena yathādṛḍham|
sudhayā tāṃ dṛḍhīkṛtya puṇyāhaprokṣaṇaṃ caret || 10.9 ||
<piṇḍikāyāṃ lakṣmyāḥ āvāhanamarcanaṃ ca>
āvāhayet piṇḍikāyāṃ lakṣmīṃ kamalavāsinīm|
arcayedgandhapuṣpādyaiḥ piṇḍikādhiṣṭhitāṃ śriyam || 10.10 ||
<bimbārthaṃ pīṭhārthaṃ ratnanyāsārthaṃ ca svīkāryāḥ śilāḥ>
bimbaṃ puṃśilayā kuryāt pīṭhaṃ strīśilayā bhavet|
ratnanyāsastu kartavyo napuṃsakaśilopari || 10.11 ||
<garbhagṛhe piṇḍikāyuktabimbasya niveśaḥ tatsthāpanañca>
navena vāsasācchādya piṇḍikāṃ yāgasadmani|
harimabhyarcya vidhivat agnīnapi visarjayet || 10.12 ||
dvāratoraṇakumbhasthān pālikādhiṣṭhitānapi|
somakuṃbhasthitaṃ devaṃ visṛjya tadanantaram || 10.13 ||
mahākuṃbhopakuṃbhāṃśca dhārayeyuśca mūrtipāḥ|
ācāryaḥ purato yāyāt gṛhītvā karakaṃ śubham || 10.14 ||
agrataḥ secayenmārgaṃ galantyā vāridhārayā|
garbhagehaṃ vimānasya nītvā tatra niveśayet || 10.15 ||
ācāryo ṛtvijaścānye devamutthāpya maṅgalaiḥ|
vedairvādyaiśca vividhairgaccheyurmandiraṃ prati || 10.16 ||
antaḥ praveśayeddevaṃ piṇḍikāyāṃ niveśayet|
śubhe muhūrte samprāpte brahmasūtraṃ vilokayan || 10.17 ||
`ātvā hārṣaṃ pratiṣṭhāsi' `dhruvādyau'riti vidyayā|
ācāryaḥ purataḥ sthitvā sthāpayetsarvavidyayā || 10.18 ||
<piṇḍikāpratimayoḥ saṃyojanam aṣṭabandhakalpanaprakāraśca>
brahmasūtraṃ gururdṛṣṭvā samatāṃ ca vilokayet|
aṣṭabandhena badhnīyāt lāṅgalena dṛḍhaṃ yathā || 10.19 ||
sārdhamekaṃ cūrṇabhāgaṃ bhāgamekaṃ ca guggulam|
ardhabhāgāṃ tathā lākṣāṃ kuruvindaṃ tadardhakam || 10.20 ||
madhūcchiṣṭaṃ tadardhaṃ tu tūlaṃ sarpiśca gairikam|
yatheṣṭaṃ yojayeddhīmān yathāmṛdu bhavettathā || 10.21 ||
<mūlabimbe parivāradevatābimbeṣu ca bhagavataḥ āvāhanam>
puṇyāhaṃ vācayet paścāt prokṣayedapi piṇḍikām|
bimbaṃ ca vāsudevasya paścādāvāhanaṃ caret || 10.22 ||
dhyāyetparātparaṃ viṣṇuṃ hṛtpadme karṇikopari|
āvāhayettato bimbe yogapīṭhapurassaram || 10.23 ||
āvāhya mūlamantreṇa vardhanīsthena vāriṇā|
pariṣicya ṣaḍarṇena prokṣayetkuṃbhavāribhiḥ || 10.24 ||
ṛtvikṣu śāntimantrāṇi paṭhatsu brāhmaṇeṣvapi|
ghoṣayatsu brahmaghoṣaṃ tauryanṛttādimaṅgalaiḥ || 10.25 ||
dvādaśākṣaramantreṇa brahmakūrcodakokṣaṇam|
upakuṃbhāṣṭakajalaistathā vai sva sva vidyayā || 10.26 ||
tattatkuṃbhagatāṃ śaktiṃ tattanmantreṇa yojayet|
kuṃbhatoyāvaśeṣeṇa parivārān prakalpayet || 10.27 ||
aṣṭākṣarasya mantrasya nyāsaṃ bimbe samarpayet|
sānnidhyaṃ sarvadā tatra prārthayeddeśikottamaḥ || 10.28 ||
evamāvāhya deveśaṃ prokṣya śaktiṃ niveśya ca|
parivārān yathāsthānam sthāpayitvā yathāvidhi|| 10.29 ||
āvāhanaprokṣaṇādi sarvaṃ karma samāpya ca|
abhyarcya devadeveśamupacāraiḥ pṛthagvidhaiḥ || 10.30 ||
mahāhavirnivedyātha sthāpiteṣu yathākramam|
baliṃ dadyāt yathāśāstramekabere tvayaṃ vidhiḥ || 10.31 ||
<bahuberapratiṣṭhāvidhau dhruvādibimbānāṃ sthānanirdeśaḥ>
bahuberapratiṣṭhāyāṃ viśeṣo'styatra kaścana|
divyamānuṣayoḥ sandhau dhruvabimbaniveśanam || 10.32 ||
brāhme pade'rcanāpīṭhaṃ karmārcā madhyame pade|
tatraiva makhabimbaṃ ca saumye snapanakautukam || 10.33 ||
tīrthabimbaṃ ca yāmye tu śayyārthabalikautuke|
laukikaṃ bimbamanyacca bāhyabhūmau niveśayet || 10.34 ||
<pratiṣṭhāṅgabhūtakarmasampādanasthānanirdeśaḥ>
sumuhūrte śubhe lagne sthāpayeddhruvakautukam|
tattvasaṃhārajanane tattvanyāsādi karma ca || 10.35 ||
śāntyantamakhilaṃ karma garbhagehe samācareta|
anyeṣāmaṅgabimbānāṃ sarvaṃ tadyāgamandire || 10.36 ||
<pratiṣṭhākrame kumbhanyāsavidhiḥ>
pratiṣṭhāsamaye prāpte kumbhānutthāpya deśikaḥ|
paścāttadaṅgabimbaiśca prādakṣiṇyena mandiram || 10.37 ||
antaḥpraviśya vinyasya kumbhānapi yathāvidhi|
<mūlabimbe śaktiniveśaprakāraḥ>
āvāhayenmūlabere paramātmānamavyayam || 10.38 ||
pañcopaniṣadairmantraiḥ tatprakāramitaḥ śrṛṇu|
sadāviṣṇuṃ mahāviṣṇau taṃ viṣṇau viniveśayet || 10.39 ||
hṛtpuṇḍarīke taṃ viṣṇuṃ taṃ ca svāñjalipaṅkaje|
dhruvabimbabrahmarandhrāt bimbe taṃ viniveśayet || 10.40 ||
pariṣiñcettato bimbaṃ vardhanīsthena vāriṇā|
mahākumbhaprokṣaṇaṃ ca `dvādaśākṣara'vidyayā || 10.41 ||
upakumbhāmbubhiḥ paścāt prokṣayetsvasvavidyayā|
śaktīstattatkumbhagatāḥ mūlabere niveśayet || 10.42 ||
apidhāya brahmarandhraṃ praṇavena samādhinā|
mantranyāsaṃ tataḥ kuryāt sargasṛṣṭyādikarmavit || 10.43 ||
<karmabimbādiṣu śaktyāvāhanakramaḥ>
sarveṣāṃ karmabimbānāṃ mūlādāvāhanaṃ matam|
kumbhatoyāvaśeṣeṇa prokṣaṇaṃ parikalpayet || 10.44 ||
brahmādiparivārāṇāmanyeṣāṃ prāṅgaṇe satām|
caṇḍādīnāṃ śriyādīnāṃ balipīṭhāvasānikam|| 10.45 ||
mahākumbhaṃ samāhṛtya deśiko brāhmaṇaiḥ saha|
svasvamantreṇa samprokṣya karmajālaṃ samāpayet || 10.46 ||
arcayeddevadeveśaṃ naivedyāntaṃ yathāpuram|
<gurvādibhyo dakṣiṇādānādinirdeśaḥ>
pūjayedyajamāno'pi pratiṣṭhāsamaye gurum || 10.47 ||
sauvarṇakusumairvastraiḥ keyūrairaṅgulīyakaiḥ|
kuṇḍalaiḥ kaṭakairyajñasūtraiśca sumanoharaiḥ || 10.48 ||
paṭṭavastrairdukūlaiśca sasvarṇairuttarīyakaiḥ|
uṣṇīṣairgandhapuṣpaiśca bhūṣayedvividhairapi || 10.49 ||
ghaṭodhnīrgāssavassāśca dharaṇīṃ sasyaśālinīm|
svarṇaniṣkasahasraṃ ca dadyāttasmai svayaṃ prabhuḥ || 10.50 ||
aśaktaścetttripādaṃ vā dalaṃ vā pādameva vā|
yadvā niṣkaśataṃ dadyāt gurave dakṣiṇāṃ prabhuḥ || 10.51 ||
tadardham ṛtvijāṃ dadyāt tadardhaṃ paricāriṇām|
pratiṣṭhāyāṃ tu devasya bhojayedbrāhmaṇān bahūn || 10.52 ||
lakṣaṃ vāpyayutaṃ vāpi sahasraṃ vā yathābalam|
sarvāśaktaḥ śataṃ viprān bhojayedyajñasiddhaye || 10.53 ||
bhojayitvā dvijān nityaṃ pratiṣṭhādivase prabhuḥ|
dakṣiṇāṃ vitarebhyo tāmbūlaparimaṇḍitām || 10.54 ||
trayīvidbhyaḥ śāstravidbhyaḥ stotṛbhyaśca viśeṣataḥ|
itarebhyo dvijebhyaśca deyaiva dvijadakṣiṇā || 10.55 ||
<dakṣiṇārahitayāgasya phalarāhityakathanam>
dakṣiṇārahito yajñaḥ prabhave na phalapradaḥ|
tasmāddvijebhyaḥ sarvebhyaḥ vitareddakṣiṇāṃ prabhuḥ || 10.56 ||
<pratiṣṭhākarmaṇaḥ samāptau deśikasya gṛhānayanam>
evaṃ saṃsthāpya deveśaṃ sarvamaṅgalasaṃyutam|
ṛtvigbhiḥ saha santuṣṭaṃ bhojanena dhanena ca || 10.57 ||
gītavāditradhoṣeṇa chatracāmarabījanaiḥ|
svastibrūvāṇairvipraiśca deśikaṃ ninayedgṛham || 10.58 ||
<pratiṣṭhāsampādakasya sahāyakānāṃ ca kṛte phalakīrtanam>
evaṃ yaḥ sthāpayeddevaṃ yajamāno mahītale|
tasya vaṃśarddhiratulā dhanena dhanadopamaḥ || 10.59 ||
vidyāvān balavān dhīmān bhūyāccāpi mahīpatiḥ|
vinaśyanti ca vighnāni pāpāḥ yānti kṣayaṃ dhruvam || 10.60 ||
atulā śrīśca tasya syāt dīrghāyuśca bhaviṣyati|
amaraiḥ stūyate nityaṃ puṇyakṛdbhiśca pūjyate || 10.61 ||
upakurvanti ye martyāḥ teṣāṃ puṇyaṃ na tarkyate|
bahunā kiṃ devaloke vasanti divi devavat || 10.62 ||
paṭhatāṃ ca trayīṃ śāstraṃ vāso bhūyātttriviṣṭape|
vādyavādanaśaktānāṃ gītanṛttādi kurvatām || 10.63 ||
gandharvaloke vasatirannapānādidāyinām|
na punarjananaṃ teṣāṃ yānachatrādidhāriṇām || 10.64 ||
yānādhikāriṇo devāḥ devaloke na saṃśayaḥ|
ācāryāṇāmṛtvijāṃ ca tathaiva paricāriṇām || 10.65 ||
karmiṇāṃ na panarjanma sthāpito yairjanārdanaḥ|
kāmanārahitaṃ karma kāritaṃ yaistu mānavaiḥ || 10.66 ||
teṣāṃ triviṣṭape vāsaḥ punarāvṛttivarjitaḥ|
<yāgadarśanasya māhātmyam>
kevalaṃ darśanaṃ viṣṇoḥ sarvakāmaphalapradam || 10.67 ||
kiṃ punaḥ phalamicchadbhiḥ yāgakāle viśeṣataḥ|
<pratiṣṭhānte bhagavataḥ snapanasya mahotsavasya ca nirdeśaḥ>
evaṃ saṃsthāpya medhāvī vāsudevaṃ sanātanam || 10.68 ||
snāpayitvā pratiṣṭhānte dhvajapūrvaṃ mahotsavam|
kuryāttatprītaye bhūyācchārṅgiṇaḥ paramātmanaḥ || 10.69 ||
|| iti bhārgavatantre daśamo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<11>
|| ekādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<mahotsavāṅgadhvajārohaṇādinirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<mahotsavavidhinirūpaṇārthaṃ prārthanā>
agasatyaḥ
kathaṃ mahotsavavidhaḥ kartavyaḥ paramātmanaḥ|
kṛpayā bhārgava śrīman brūhi me bhaktavatsala || 11.1 ||
<mahotsavamāhātmyam mahotsavadināvadhiśca>
śrīrāmaḥ
śṛṇu tvaṃ muniśārdūla devadevamahotsavam|
yenārādhya hariṃ śakro vimukto vṛtrahatyayā || 11.2 ||
tamadya tava vakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣva vinayānvitaḥ|
dvādaśāhena kurvīta kalyāṇaṃ śārṅgadhanvanaḥ || 11.3 ||
yadvā navabhirahobhiḥ saptabhirvātha pañcabhiḥ|
<dhvajapaṭe karuḍasya svarūpaṃ tadakṣimocanaṃ ca>
ārambhadivasāt pūrvaṃ vainateyadhvajotsavaḥ || 11.4 ||
tadarthamaṅkuranyāsaṃ kṛtvā śāstravidhānataḥ|
paṭe tu vilikheddevaṃ vainateyaṃ khagādhipam || 11.5 ||
navatālapramāṇaṃ taṃ taptakāñcanasannibham|
dvibhujaṃ kañtukayutaṃ puṣpāñcalidharaṃ vibhum || 11.6 ||
kirīṭakuṇḍaladharaṃ nāgābharaṇabhūṣitam|
pṛṣṭhavinyastasavyāṅghrimitareṇāpi kuñcitam || 11.7 ||
pakṣavikṣepasahitaṃ gaganotpatanonmukham|
nīlanāsāñcalaṃ devaṃ nayanonmīlanaṃ caret || 11.8 ||
<garuḍasya kumbhādīnāṃ ca adhivāsanam>
śilpinānyāḥ kriyāḥ sarvāḥ pratiṣṭhoktavidhānataḥ|
chāyādhivāsanaṃ kṛtvā snāpayitvā ca darpaṇe || 11.9 ||
vainateyasya gāyatryā pūjayitvā yathāvidhi|
śālipīṭhe suvistīrṇe sāyayitvā tato guruḥ || 11.10 ||
taddakṣiṇe dhānyarāśau sopakuṃbhaṃ salakṣaṇam|
vardhanyā stāpayetkumbhaṃ sarvālaṅkārasaṃyutam || 11.11 ||
vainateyaṃ mahākumbhe vardhanyo tu sudarśanam|
aṣṭaṣvanyeṣu cendrādyāḥ pūjanīyā yathāvidhi || 11.12 ||
<garuḍādhivāsanāṅgahomakramaḥ>
ekasminneva kurvīta prākkuṇḍe vā mahānase|
mūrtihomaṃ bṛhatsāmnā gāyatryā vā yathāruci || 11.13 ||
tattvasaṃhārajanane tattvahomādi pūrvavat|
<garuḍāvāhanārcanaprakāraḥ>
paṭaṃ vistārya tadanu mandirābhimukhaṃ guruḥ || 11.14 ||
pradarśya devadevāya tadanvāvāhanaṃ cerat|
bhagavan puṇḍarīkākṣa sarvśvarajaganmaya || 11.15 ||
tvayā yathā tu kathitaṃ tathā kartuṃ na śakyate|
asvāntryādasāmarthyāt śraddhādīnāmabhāvataḥ || 11.16 ||
tvadātmanādya sarveṣāṃ nyūnādhikyopaśāntaye|
samālokaya netrābhyāṃ sītalābhyāṃ paṭasthitam || 11.17 ||
sarvadoṣāpahāribhyāṃ vainateyaṃ prasādaya|
darśayitvaivamācāryo vyāhṛtyāvāhanaṃ caret || 11.18 ||
mahābala mahābāho vainateya vayodhipa|
sannidhatsva paṭe tubhyaṃ namaḥ praṇavamūrtaye || 11.19 ||
iti samprārthya sānnidhyaṃ gāyatryaiva tadīyayā|
pūjayitvārdhyapādyādyaiḥ nivedyānnaṃ caturvidham || 11.20 ||
kumbhāya ca yathāśāstraṃ japenmantraṃ ca gāruḍam|
vedaghoṣaistūryaghoṣairjāgareṇa nayenniśām || 11.21 ||
<utsavāṅgadhvajārohaṇavidhiḥ>
tataḥ prabhāte vimale samārādhya khageśvaram|
alaṅkṛtya yathāyogaṃ yānamāropya maṅgalaiḥ || 11.22 ||
prādakṣiṇyena ninayet dvajayaṣṭeḥ puro bhuvi|
prāsādābhimukhaṃ devaṃ vistārya paṭasaṃsthitam || 11.23 ||
puṇyāhaṃ vācayet paścāt prokṣayetkumbhavāribhiḥ|
`gāruḍenai'va mantreṇa vardhanyā pariṣecayet|| 11.24 ||
svaiḥ svaiḥ mantraistathendrādikumbhaprokṣaṇamācaret|
vinyasya `gāruḍaṃ'mantraṃ sānnidhyaprārthanaṃ caret || 11.25 ||
utsavāntaṃ paramadhāmnaḥ sannidhatsva paṭodare|
bhagavan pakṣibhūpāla pālayāsmān vilokaya || 11.26 ||
iti prārthya khageśānaṃ muhūrte sarvamaṅgale|
manaḥprahlādane lagne dhvajārohaṇamācaret || 11.27 ||
utsavapratimāṃ tatra yānamāropya cānayet|
aṅguṣṭhaghanamātreṇa pāśena trivṛtena ca || 11.28 ||
tāntavena ca badhnīyānmantreṇa `puruṣā'tmanā|
`suparṇo'sī'ti mantreṇa dhvajārohaṇamucyate || 11.29 ||
āropya caivaṃ vihagaṃ pūjayitvā yathāvidhi|
pāyasānnaṃ tathā'pūpaṃ phalāni vividhāni ca || 11.30 ||
nivedya vainateyāyā indradīn parito'rcayet|
pūjayitvā yathānyāyaṃ baliṃ dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 11.31 ||
makhakautukamāsādya praṇamyābhyarcya deśikaḥ|
anujñāṃ ca samāsādya puṣpāṇyādāya śārṅgiṇaḥ || 11.32 ||
udagravadano bhūtvā samālokya khageśvaram|
asmāddinātsamārabhya yāvattīrthadināntikam || 11.33 ||
sannidhiṃ kuru pakṣīndra rājño janapadasya ca|
grāmasya yajamānasya vaiṣṇavānāṃ mahātmanām || 11.34 ||
tuṣṭaye puṣṭaye caiva sarvaśatrujayāya ca|
apamṛtyuharārthāya vainateya prasīda om || 11.35 ||
iti puṣpāñjiṃ samyak vikiredvihagopari|
<dhvajārohaṇāṅgabrāhmaṇasammānanam>
dadyādgobhūhiraṇyādi brāhmaṇebhyastadā prabhuḥ || 11.36 ||
<utsavapratimāpūjanakramaḥ>
utsavapratimāṃ paścānninayenmaṇḍapāntaram|
pūjayecca yathānyāyaṃ prāpayedgarbhamandiram || 11.37 ||
<bherītāḍanadevātāhvānaprakāraḥ>
āhvānārthamaśeṣāṇāṃ lokānāṃ deśikottamaḥ|
devānāmapi sarveṣāṃ bherītāḍanamācaret || 11.38 ||
dhvajapīṭhasamīporvīṃ gomayena viśodhayet|
bhūṣabhedraṅgaballībhiḥ pīṭhaṃ tatra niveśayet || 11.39 ||
balibimbaṃ samādāya tatra nyasya samarcayet|
puratastasya bimbasya dhānyapīṭhaṃ prakalpayet || 11.40 ||
tatra bherīṃ ca koṇaṃ ca vinyasya tadanantaram|
snātaṃ śuddhāmbaradharaṃ sordhvapuṇḍraṃ svalaṅkṛtam || 11.41 ||
yajñopavītinaṃ pārśve dakṣiṇe gaṇikājanam|
sthāpayedindradigbhāge nṛttavidyāviśāradam || 11.42 ||
gītavāditrakuśalān uttare sthāpayedbudhaḥ|
puṇyāhavāribhirbherīṃ tathā pāraśavādikān || 11.43 ||
prokṣayitvā mahābherīṃ pūjayedbhāratīmayīm|
navena vāsasā bherīṃ veṣṭayecchabdarūpiṇīm || 11.44 ||
puṣpādibhiralaṅkuryāt gandhaiśca vividhairapi|
bherīmadhye'rcayeddevīṃ triguṇāṃ bhāratīmayīm || 11.45 ||
dakṣiṇottarayorviṣṇuṃ lakṣmīṃ ca paripūjayet|
koṇe samarcayeddevaṃ vāyuṃ kalaśasaṃbhava || 11.46 ||
tato mūrtitrayaṃ dhyāyan śabdatanmātravidyayā|
koṇena tāḍayedbherīṃ devatānāma kīrtayan || 11.47 ||
vaikuṇṭhavāsino ye ca satyalokanivāsinaḥ|
rudralokasthitā ye ca tathā svarlokavāsinaḥ || 11.48 ||
pātālādiṣu lokeṣu ye vasanti mahītale|
anye'pi ye ca lokeṣu sarvatrāhvānamācaret || 11.49 ||
ṛṣīṇāmagnikalpānāṃ devānāṃ ditijanmanām|
sarveṣāṃ ca samāhvānaṃ parivārapadānvitam || 11.50 ||
āyudhaiḥ saha devībhiḥ vāhanaiśca mahotsave|
āhvānaṃ kārayeddhīmān sevārthaṃ madhuvidviṣaḥ || 11.51 ||
<pratyāhvānakramaḥ>
pratyāhvānaṃ mahābheryāḥ tāḍanatrayamācaret|
teṣāṃ nṛttaṃ svaraṃ tālaṃ trayaṃ tatra prakāśayet || 11.52 ||
<utsavakrame devatāhvānāṅgabalinivedanam>
āhūya devatāḥ sarvāḥ balibimbapurassaram|
grāmamadhyaṃ samāsādya brahmāṇaṃ sanniveśayet || 11.53 ||
grāmasya parito vīthyāṃ sthaṇḍileṣu yathākramam|
indrādilokapālāṃstān balyarthaṃ sanniveśayet || 11.54 ||
evamāhūya medhāvī devadevasya śārṅgiṇaḥ|
utsavāya yatetātha deśakālānusārataḥ || 11.55 ||
|| iti bhārgavatantre ekādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<12>
|| dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<utsavavidhinirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<utsavāṅgabhūtāṅkurārpaṇakālaḥ>
utsavārambhadivasāt pūrvamevāṅkurārpaṇam|
tṛtīye'hani vā kuryāt pūrvasmin divase'pi vā || 12.1 ||
<utsavakrame prākārādīnāṃ śodhanaṃ bhūṣaṇañca>
prākāraṃ mandiraṃ cāpi valajaṃ vīthikā api|
prapābhistoraṇaiścānyairmālikābhiḥ samantataḥ || 12.2 ||
śodhayedgomayāṃbhobhiḥ raṅgabalyakṣatādibhiḥ|
anyaiśca vividhairdṛśyaiḥ bhūṣayecca gṛhāṇyapi || 12.3 ||
<yāgamaṇḍapavedikuṇḍakalpanaprakāraḥ>
sambhāryāḥ yāgasambhārāḥ utsavārthaṃ viśeṣataḥ|
antarāle diśāmindre kalpayedyāgamaṇḍapam || 12.4 ||
saptabhirbāhudaṇḍaiśca samantāccaturaśrakam|
tasya madhye bhavedvediḥ hastamātrasamucchrayā || 12.5 ||
hastadvayena vistīrṇā kuṇḍāni paritastataḥ|
yadvaikakuṇḍaṃ prāgbhāge caturaśraṃ manoharam || 12.6 ||
prācyāmāhavanīyaṃ syāt dakṣiṇasyāṃ tu dakṣiṇam|
gārhapatyaṃ pratīcīne sabhyamanyatra kalpayet || 12.7 ||
toraṇādikriyāḥ sarvāḥ pūrvapanna viśiṣyate|
<utsavārthamācāryādivaraṇādikam>
