ācāryāṇāmṛtvijāṃ ca tathaiva paricāriṇām || 12.8 ||
varaṇaṃ caturaḥ pūrvaṃ deśikān varayet prabhuḥ|
śobhayedbhūṣaṇairvastraiḥ gandhamālyādibhiḥ purā || 12.9 ||
<utsavārtha devādhivāsaprakāraḥ>
adhivāsaṃ prakurvīta pūrvasmin divase niśi|
snāpayitvātha devībhyāṃ śayane śāyayeddharim || 12.10 ||
tadarthaṃ kautukaṃ badhvā paścācchayanakalpanam|
koṇeṣu sthāpayeddīpān śayyāyā deśikottamaḥ || 12.11 ||
samidbhiraṣṭabhirhomo nṛsūktena caruṃ punaḥ|
sarpiṣāṣṭau punarhutvā mūlena munipuṅgava || 12.12 ||
stutvātha devadeveśaṃ vedairvādyaiśca maṅgalaiḥ|
japaiśca mūlamantrasya jāgareṇa nayenniśām || 12.13 ||
<adhivāsānantaraṃ maṇḍape devasya snapanāmarcanaṃ ca>
tataḥ prabhāte vimale dṛṣṭe bhāskaramaṇḍale|
`uttiṣṭha' iti mantreṇa harimutthāpayedguruḥ || 12.14 ||
`idaṃ viṣṇu'ritiśrutyā śivikāyāṃ niveśayet|
baheyurbrāhmaṇāḥ yānam dhyāyanto vihagādhipam || 12.15 ||
nṛttaiśca vividhairgeyairvādyaiśca vividhairapi|
brahmaghoṣaistathā stotrairnītvā grāmapradakṣiṇam || 12.16 ||
maṇḍape'laṅkṛte devamavaropyāsane śubhe|
upacarya yathāyogaṃ sambhārān saṃbhṛtān guruḥ || 12.17 ||
devāya darśayet sarvān ānītān janasaṃsadi|
atha saṃsnāpya deveśamabhyarcya ca nivedya ca || 12.18 ||
kṛtvā ca nityahomāntaṃ ninayet garbhamandiram|
<utsavakrame pratisarabandhaprakāraḥ>
sāyaṃ praviśya sadanaṃ yajamānena deśikaḥ || 12.19 ||
praṇamya tamanujñāpya baddhvā kalyāṇakautukam|
utsavasnānabalyarcā pratimāsvapyanantaram || 12.20 ||
baddhvā pratisaraṃ paścāt teṣāṃ haste yathāvidhi|
utthāpya balibimbaṃ tat prādakṣiṇyena mandiram || 12.21 ||
yāgamaṇḍapamānīya sthāpayedāsane harim|
<kuṃbhanyāsaḥ tatra tattaddevānāmarcanakramaśca>
vedikāyāṃ dhānyapīṭhe madhye kuṃbhuṃ ca dakṣiṇe || 12.22 ||
karakaṃ vinyasetkuṃbhān aṣṭau prāgādiṣu kramāt|
dvāratoraṇakumbhānāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmivārcanam || 12.23 ||
mūlamūrtiṃ yajetkumbhe vardhanyāmastradevatā|
prāgādidikṣu kumbheṣu vāsudevādayaḥ smṛtāḥ || 12.24 ||
āgneyādiṣu koṇeṣu puruṣādīn samarcayet|
puruṣaśca tathā satyaḥ acyuto'nanta ityamī || 12.25 ||
abhyarcayecca vidhivat havīṃṣi ca nivedayet|
balibimbaṃ tathābhyarcya nivedya ca yathāvidhi || 12.26 ||
<yāgamaṇḍape vedapārāyaṇādikramaḥ>
prāgādyāśāsu sarvāsu ṛgyajuḥ sāmapāṭhakān|
atharvāṅgirasābhijñān pāṭhayitvā yathākramam || 12.27 ||
tūryādivādyaghoṣeṇa pūrite ca digantare|
<yāgamaṇḍape tattaddhomakuṇḍeṣu tattaddevoddeśyakahomakramaḥ>
prāgādyāśāsu kuṇḍeṣu vāsudevādikān yajet || 12.28 ||
tīrthāvasānikaṃ tatra sānnidhyaṃ prārthayedapi|
evamabhyarcite kumbhe bhagavatyakhileśvare || 12.29 ||
sarpirādyairbhaveddhomaḥ tattanmantrairyathākramam|
sarpibhiḥ kusumairdhūpadravyaiśca samidhā punaḥ || 12.30 ||
payasā cāpi dadhnā ca tilavrīhiyavairapi|
kuṇḍādhidaivatairmantraiḥ pṛthagaṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 12.31 ||
aṣṭaviṃśatiraṣṭau vā homaḥ prāgādipūrvavat|
evaṃ hutvātha vidhivat nṛsūktena caruṃ punaḥ || 12.32 ||
pratikuṇḍaṃ brahmakuṇḍe pāyasena ghṛtena vā|
brahmādidevānuddiśya juhuyāt kalaśodbhava || 12.33 ||
brahmaṇe cātha rudrāya prajānāṃ pataye tathā|
indrāya ca mahendrāya bhānave jātavedhase || 12.34 ||
dharmarājāya marute kuberāya ca yātune|
varuṇāya ṛṣibhyaśca vedebhyaḥ puṣpadhanvane || 12.35 ||
viśvebhyaścāpi devebhyaḥ chandobhyaḥ kālamūrtaye|
dharaṇyai adrirājāya nadībhyo vanamūrtaye || 12.36 ||
bhūtebhyaśca paśubhyaśca oṣadhībhyaḥ śriyai tathā|
apsarobhyaśca lokebhyaḥ samudrebhyo'mbarāya ca || 12.37 ||
gandharvebhyaśca yakṣebhyaḥ pārṣadebhyaśca cakriṇe|
caṇḍādidvārapālebhyaḥ sarvebhyaścāpi viṣṇave || 12.38 ||
praṇavādyagnijāyāntaṃ uktvā homaṃ samācaret|
tatastu mūlamantreṇa hutvedaṃ viṣṇurityṛcā || 12.39 ||
<utsavakrame balidānaprakāraḥ>
kṛtvā tu sviṣṭakṛddhomaṃ balidānaṃ samācaret|
māṣānnaṃ ghṛtasaṃsiktamādāya paricārakāḥ || 12.40 ||
dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ tathā gandhaṃ puṣpāṇi surabhīṇi ca|
anyāni pūjādravyāṇi vedavādyapurassaram || 12.41 ||
yānamāropya balyarcāṃ tayā sākaṃ baliṃ caret|
prathamaṃ yāgaśālāyāṃ pratidvāraṃ baliṃ kṣipet || 12.42 ||
caṇḍādidvārapālānāṃ dadyānnityotsave yathā|
grāmamadhyaṃ samāsādya brahmaṇe vitaredbalim || 12.43 ||
prāgādīśānaparyantam indrādīnāṃ baliṃ kṣipet|
baliśiṣṭaṃ mahāpīṭhe nikṣipetpīṭhamūrdhani || 12.44 ||
toyapūrvottaraṃ dadyādbaliṃ kalaśasaṃbhava|
tatra devaṃ samabhyarcya ninayedyāgamandiram || 12.45 ||
<utsavaprasaṅge vīthikāsu bhagavataḥ parikramaṇaprakāraḥ>
makhakautukamārādhya rathaṃ vā śivikādikam|
āropya samalaṅkṛtya brāhmaṇaiḥ svastivācakaiḥ || 12.46 ||
niṣkramya mandirāttasmāt sarvāvaraṇabhūmikāḥ|
parikramedvīthikāśca vidvadbhirvedapāṭhakaiḥ || 12.47 ||
śāstravidbhiśca kavibhiḥ tathā paurāṇikairapi|
mauhūrtikairbhiṣagbhiśca gadyapadyavimiśrakam || 12.48 ||
paṭhadbhiśca stutiparaiḥ nānābhāṣāviśāradaiḥ|
nṛtyadbhiścāpi gāyadbhiḥ gāṇikyena ca nṛtyatā || 12.49 ||
vedāntibhiśca yatibhiḥ yogibhirbhagavanmayaiḥ|
bruvadbhirbhagavacchāstraṃ māhātmyaṃ ca punaḥ punaḥ || 12.50 ||
nānāvādyarataiḥ puṃbhi strībhiścākṣimanoharaiḥ|
sarvālaṅkārayuktābhirgamayedvīthikāṃ harim || 12.51 ||
chatraṃ ca vālavyajanaṃ tālavṛntaṃ ca śobhanam|
patākādīpikāsstaṃbhāḥ pradīpāśca viśeṣataḥ || 12.52 ||
gandhodgārīṇi dhūpāni toraṇaṃ ca gavākṣikam|
patākāhastinaścāśvā vādyāni vividhāni ca || 12.53 ||
anyaiśca maṅgalaiḥ sākaṃ parikramya pradakṣiṇam |
<vīthiparikramaṇānantaraṃ mandire bhagavata ārādhanam>
mandirābhyantare yānādavaropyāsane śubhe || 12.54 ||
niveśya prāṅmukhaṃ devamupacāraiḥ pṛthagvidhaiḥ|
upacarya tataḥ snāpya nivedya ca mahāhaviḥ || 12.55 ||
nīrājya parameśānaṃ ninayenmaṅgalāvanim|
āropya śayane devam arcayitvā yathocitam || 12.56 ||
śayyopacāramakhilam nṛttagītādibhiḥ saha|
śriyā dharaṇyā dvābhyāṃ vā svāpayetpuruṣottamam || 12.57 ||
<bhagavataḥ dainandinotsavakramaḥ>
tataḥ prabhāte taṃ devam utthāpya śayanādguruḥ|
arcayitvā yathāśāstraṃ kumbhādīn yāgamandire || 12.58 ||
balibimbaṃ samabhyarcya hutvā vahnau yathāpurā|
baliṃ ca pūrvavaddatvā yātrāṃ kuryāt yathā niśi || 12.59 ||
sāyaṃ prātaḥ pratidinaṃ pūjanaṃ havanaṃ baliḥ|
yātrā ca vidhivatkāryā snapanaṃ ca yathākramam || 12.60 ||
divase divase kuryādutsavaṃ śārṅgadhanvanaḥ|
devībhyāṃ vā śriyā vāpi vinā vā kevalaṃ hareḥ || 12.61 ||
<bhagavataḥ maṅgalotsavakramaḥ>
evaṃ mahotsave viṣṇorvardhamāne mahāmune|
devībhyāṃ saha vā lakṣmyā kārayenmaṅgalotsavam || 12.62 ||
pūjādibalidānāntamanuṣṭhāya yathāvidhi|
alaṅkṛtya ca devībhyāṃ yānamāropya maṇḍapam || 12.63 ||
anyaṃ nītvāvaropyātha hemarsihāsane śubhe|
niveśya prāṅmukhaṃ devaṃ snāpayenmaṅgalodakaiḥ || 12.64 ||
dhānyapaṭhisya purataḥ musalaṃ cāpyulūkhalam|
śuddhodakena saṃkṣālya mūlamantraṃ samuccaran || 12.65 ||
sthāpayitvā navairvastraiḥ pariveṣṭya ca pallavaiḥ|
kusumaiścandanairdravyeḥ alaṅkṛtya manoharam || 12.66 ||
haridrāṃ tatra nikṣipya śriyaṃ trailokyamātaram|
dhyāyannabhyarcayet gandhapuṣpadhūpādibhiḥ kramāt || 12.67 ||
tato maṅgalavādinyo devadāsyaḥ svalaṅkṛtāḥ|
āvahanyurniśāṃ sarvāṃ yathāsūkṣmaṃ yathāvidham || 12.68 ||
sauvarṇādimayaṃ kuṃbhaṃ sūtravastrādiveṣṭitam|
dhānyarāśau samāveśya niśācūrṇena pūrayet || 12.69 ||
sauvarṇalakṣmīpratimāṃ ratnāni ca niveśayet|
kūrcaṃ ca pallavaṃ cāpi śarāveṇa pidhāpayet || 12.70 ||
tatra śriyaṃ samāvāhya sāṅgaṃ pūjāṃ samācaret|
mūlamantreṇa vidhivattaiścūrṇairabhiṣecayet || 12.71 ||
śrīsūktavidyayābhyarcya yathāvidhi yathāpuram|
mahāhavirnivedyānte deśikendraśca ṛtvijaḥ || 12.72 ||
pradakṣiṇīkṛtya hariṃ vikiret kusumāñjalim|
stotrayedvividhaiḥ stotraiḥ manaḥprahlādanairapi || 12.73 ||
<maṅgalotsavakrame jaladroṇyāṃ bhagavataḥ snapanaprakāraḥ>
kasmiṃścit divase paścāt kārite maṅgalotsave|
snāpayetsaha devībhyāṃ jaladroṇyāṃ jagadgurum || 12.74 ||
aparāhṇe tu samprāpte śibikāyāṃ jagadgurum|
śrībhūmibhyāṃ samāropya nadītīraṃ nayedguruḥ || 12.75 ||
maṇḍape vā prapāyāṃ vā sarvālaṅkārasaṃyute|
bherīmṛdaṅgādiravaiḥ pūrite ca digantare || 12.76 ||
ṛgyajussāmavedānāṃ ghoṣeṇa parimaṇḍite|
nṛtyantīnāṃ tu dāsīnāṃ gītisvananinādite || 12.77 ||
śāstraśravaṇasaktānāṃ parasparajigīṣuṇām|
anyonyākrośavinyastapāruṣyaninadānvite || 12.78 ||
dṛśyaiśca dīpikāstambhairdarpaṇaiśca virājite|
kadalīkramukastambhaiḥ saphalaiḥ parimaṇḍite || 12.79 ||
lambamānaiḥ puṣpasaraiḥ muktādāmabhiranvite|
atisvacchena vastreṇa savitāne manohare || 12.80 ||
tatra madhye dhānyapīṭhaṃ parikalpya manoharam|
sauvarṇīṃ vā rājatīṃ vā jaladroṇīṃ niveśayet || 12.81 ||
kuṅkumakṣodakarpūrakastūrīreṇumiśritaiḥ|
candanakṣodabharitairambubhiḥ paripūrayet || 12.82 ||
puṇyāhavācanaṃ kṛtvā brāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ|
prokṣayettāṃ jaladroṇīmādhārādi vicintayet || 12.83 ||
gaṅgādyāssaritastatra mantrairāvāhayejjale|
pūjayecca nivedyāntaṃ tāssarvāstīrthadevatāḥ || 12.84 ||
`aghamarṣaṇasūktaṃ' tat japeyuḥ sāmavāruṇam|
atthāpya devaṃ devībhyāṃ jaladroṇyāṃ niveśayet || 12.85 ||
tatra devaṃ samabhyarcya sauvarṇānyakṣatānyapi|
parito vikireddikṣu cāstramantrābhimantritān || 12.86 ||
tata utthāpya deveśaṃ devībhyāṃ siṃhaviṣṭare|
vinyasyābhyarcya vidhivannivedya ca mahāhaviḥ || 12.87 ||
yānamāropya vidhivat prāpayenmaṅgalāvanim|
<bhagavataḥ mṛgayotsavavidhiḥ>
anyasmin divase kuryānmṛgayāṃ śārṅgadhanvanaḥ || 12.88 ||
rātrau praśastā devasya mṛgayā bhunipuṅgava|
balyantamakhilaṃ kṛtvā turage vāraṇe'pi vā || 12.89 ||
āropya devaṃ bhūṣādyairalaṅkṛtya manoharam|
śārṅgaṃ ca sāyakaṃ tūṇīṃ yathāsthānaṃ niveśayet || 12.90 ||
uṣṇīṣaṃ kavacaṃ cāpi kalpayetkanakāmbaram|
kamanīyatamaṃ sarvaṃ vāhanaṃ ca paricchadam || 12.91 ||
sāyudhaiḥ parivāraiśca maṅgalairakhilaiḥ saha|
araṇyabhāgamakhilaṃ nītvā tatra prapābhuvi || 12.92 ||
abhyarcayedyathāyogaṃ naivedyāntaṃ madhudviṣam|
tataḥ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya yathāpūrvaṃ niveśayet || 12.93 ||
<tīrthayātrārtham anujñāprārthanaṃ pratisarabandhanaṃ ca>
ardharātre tu samprāpte gatvā garbhagṛhaṃ guruḥ|
praṇamya yajamānena prārthayetpuruṣottamam || 12.94 ||
tīrthayātrā tvayā kāryā śvaḥ prabhāte surottama|
tatra pratisarāraṃbhaṃ tvamanujñātumarhasi || 12.95 ||
prārthyaivaṃ tīrthabimbasya badhnīyātkautukaṃ kare|
śāyayitvārcayitvā ca prātarutthāpya taṃ punaḥ || 12.96 ||
abhyarcya vidhivatsarvaṃ karma balyantamācaret|
<tīrthayātrākrame bhagavataḥ abhiṣekakramaḥ>
tīrthabimbaṃ samādāya niveśya mukhamaṇḍape || 12.97 ||
sthāpayitvāgratastasya dhānyapīṭhaṃ prakalpayet|
sthāpitān yāgaśālāyāṃ kuṃbhānādāya deśikaḥ || 12.98 ||
pariṣicya ca vardhanyā taiḥ kuṃbhairabhiṣecayet|
prokṣaṇaṃ mūlamantreṇa nṛsūktenābhiṣecanam || 12.99 ||
ekabere mūlaberaṃ taiḥ kumbhairabhiṣecayet|
pūrvādimadhyakumbhāntamabhiṣekakramaḥ smṛtaḥ || 12.100 ||
vastrādidīpaparyantam abhyarcya ca nivedya ca|
<tīrthaṃ prati bhagavataḥ ānayanam tatra snapanakramaśca>
sarvamaṅgalasaṃyuktam āropya makhakautukam || 12.101 ||
hastyādi vā rathaṃ yānaṃ tīrthabimbapurassaram|
nadītīraṃ samudraṃ vā taṭākaṃ vāthavā saraḥ || 12.102 ||
gamayitvotsavāṅgena kalpite tatra maṇḍape|
avaropyāsane yānānniviṣṭaṃ tīrthakautukam || 12.103 ||
snāpayennavabhiḥ kuṃbhairarcitaiśca yathāvidhi|
tīrtheṣu saridādīnāṃ gaṅgādyāvāhanaṃ caret || 12.104 ||
<tīrthayātrākrame tīrthabimbādyalābhe vikalpāḥ>
tīrthabimbamalābhe tu cakraṃ vā trirnimajjayet|
cakreṇa tīrthe kartavye sati nadyādi vāribhiḥ || 12.105 ||
kūrcāgrapatitairdhīmān prokṣya kalyāṇakautukam|
saritsu cakramādāyā cakramantreṇa majjayet || 12.106 ||
alābhe cāpi cakrasya kūrcenaiva samācaret|
<tīrthasnānaphalakīrtanam>
snānti ye devadevena te narā muktakalmaṣāḥ || 12.107 ||
yānti dhruvaṃ suduṣprāpaṃ śāśvataṃ kamalāpateḥ|
<tīrthasnapanānantaraṃ bhagavataḥ arcanamānayanañca>
tato maṇḍapamānīya samabhyarcya yathāvidhi || 12.108 ||
prādakṣiṇyena gamanaṃ prāpaṇaṃ maṅgalāvaneḥ|
<utsavakrame brāhmaṇabhojanavidhānam>
sahasraṃ vā śataṃ vāpi lakṣaṃ vāpi yathāvasu || 12.109 ||
bhojanaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ tu pratyahaṃ ca mahotsave|
<utsavāntasnapanaṃ havirnivedanaṃ tattaddhomaprakāraḥ agnivisarjanaṃ ca>
tadrātrau snapanaṃ kuryāt utsavāntocitaṃ kramāt || 12.110 ||
havirnivedanāntaṃ tu samārādhya yathāvidhi|
yāgamaṇḍapamāsādya homaṃ kṛtvā yathāpuram || 12.111 ||
śāntihomaṃ tataḥ kuryāt saṃpātājyaṃ na cāharet|
prāyaścittāya juhuyāt pañcopaniṣadā śatam || 12.112 ||
agnīn visarjayet paścāt pālikātoraṇāsthitān|
<garuḍārādhanam tadudvāsanam dhvajāvarohaṇañca>
devamāropya yānādau dhvajastaṃbhaṃ samānayet || 12.113 ||
vainateyaṃ samabhyarcya nivedya ca mahāhaviḥ|
saṃhārakramamāśritya paṭāttārkṣyaṃ tamudvaset || 12.114 ||
sannidhāne tu bimbasya dhvajaṃ tamavarohayet|
<utsavaprasaṃge āvāhitānāṃ devānāmudvāsanavidhiḥ>
āvāhitāṃstato devān sthāne sthāne sthitānapi || 12.115 ||
kevalenaiva tūryeṇa baliṃ dattvā gurūttamaḥ|
udvāsya nāmabhiḥ svaiḥ svaiḥ mūkaiḥ parikaraiḥ saha || 12.116 ||
<ālaye tattatsthāne utsavādibimbānāṃ niveśaḥ ārādhakasya gamanaṃ ca>
praviśya dhāma devasya garbhagehe niveśayet|
utsavasnānabalyarcātīrthabimbāni pūrvavat || 12.117 ||
aṣṭākṣaraṃ tato mantraṃ sahasraṃ vā śatāvaram|
japtvā praṇamya deveśamanujñāpya samāvrajet || 12.118 ||
|| iti śrī bhārgavatantre dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<13>
|| trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<maṇḍalalpanapūrvakapuṣpayāgavidhinirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<maṇḍalakalpanāt pūrvaṃ bhagavataḥ ārādhanam>
śrīrāmaḥ
prabhātāyāṃ triyāmāyām āditye'bhyudite guruḥ|
nirvartya niyamān svīyān bhaktairbhāgavataiḥ saha || 13.1 ||
ālayābhyantaraṃ gatvā saṃpūjya ca yathāvidhi|
samāpya balidānāntam ācāryo maṇḍapāntera || 13.2 ||
<maṇḍalavedikākalpanaprakāraḥ>
cakrābjaṃ varttayeddhīmān vakṣyamāṇena vartmanā|
āyāmasama vistāraivedikāṃ parikalpayet || 13.3 ||
pañcabhirvā caturbhirvā hastairdṛśyāṃ manoharām|
gomayābhyobhirālipya kuryāddarpaṇasannibhām || 13.4 ||
<maṇḍalakalpanārthaṃ sūtrapātavidhiḥ>
prāñci tiryañci sūtrāṇi daśa sapta ca pātayet|
ṣaṭ pañca dve ca koṣṭhāni santi madhye'tha ṣaṭtriṣu || 13.5 ||
<karṇakādipañcakṣetravibhāgakramaḥ>
madhye śaṅkuṃ sthāpayitvā bhrāmayitvātha pañcadhā|
bhuvaṃ vibhajya sūtreṇa madhyamaṃ karṇikāpadam || 13.6 ||
dvitīyaṃ kesaradalaṃ nābhīnāṃ parikalpane|
tṛtīyaṃ ca turīyaṃ ca dve cārakṣetramucyate || 13.7 ||
pañcamaṃ nemibhūreṣāṃ bahiḥ koṣṭhāni mārjayet|
<maṇḍaladvārakṣetrakalpanaprakāraḥ>
tridhā tadvibhajetkṣetraṃ sūtrabhramaṇavartmanā || 13.8 ||
karṇiṃkākṣetramānane sūtrapātaṃ caturdiśa|
tathākṛte caturdikṣu dvārakṣetraṃ bhaviṣyati || 13.9 ||
<maṇḍale dvādaśābjanirmāṇasthānanirdeśaḥ>
pratyāvaraṇabhūbhāge dvādaśābjāni kalpayet|
koṇe koṇe bhavedekaṃ dve dve valajapārśvayoḥ || 13.10 ||
<maṇḍalakoṇeṣu pāñcajanyakalpananirdeśaḥ>
caturṣvapi ca koṇeṣu bahirāvaraṇatrayāt|
pāñcajanyaṃ suruciraṃ kalpayecchāstravittamaḥ || 13.11 ||
<karṇikākṣetre dvādaśabindukalpananirdeśaḥ>
kalpayetkarṇikākṣetre bindudvādaśakaṃ budhaḥ|
<maṇḍale abjasya kesarādipadanirmāṇaprakāraḥ>
sūtrabhramaṇamārgeṇa dvitīyaṃ vibhajetttridhā || 13.12 ||
prathamaṃ kesarapadaṃ dvitīyaṃ dalabhūrbhavet|
tṛtīyaṃ nābhivalayaṃ kesarān navatissaṣaṭ || 13.13 ||
dvādaśāmbujarekhāśca dalabhūmau prakalpayet|
digvidigbhāgakalitāścatatro viṃśatistathā || 13.14 ||
<maṇḍale arakalpanaprakāraḥ>
ekīkṛtya dvayaṃ kṣetramararekhāḥ prakalpayet|
aradvayaṃ vidikṣu syāt ekaikaṃ dikṣu kalpayet || 13.15 ||
ambhojadalatulyāni nīlotpalanibhāni ca|
maṇḍalaṃ śobhayetpañcavidhairvarṇairyathākramam || 13.16 ||
<maṇḍale varṇakalpanārthamupādānadravyāṇi>
śālipiṣṭairgandhacūrṇaiḥ śvetavarṇo haridrayā|
pītavarmastu yājñīyairaṅgārairnīlamucyate || 13.17 ||
yājñīyaiśca dalaiścūrṇaiḥ śyāmalo varṇa īritaḥ|
kuṅkumaireva raktaṃ syādevaṃ pañcavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ || 13.18 ||
<abjakarṇikādīnāṃ varṇakalpanavivekaḥ>
karṇikāṃ pītavarṇena bandūn śuklena kalpayet|
pāṭalaiḥ karṇikārekhāḥ kṛṣṇaiḥ kesarabhūtalam || 13.19 ||
dvidhā kṛtvā pūrvabhāgaṃ śvetairbhāgamathottaram|
pītairagre'rjunairbindūn raktaiśca dalakoṭiṣu || 13.20 ||
ādyantayostu patrāṇāṃ sitairaktaiśca śobhanam|
dalāntavalayaṃ raktaiḥ bhūṣayedathavārjunaiḥ || 13.21 ||
śyāmena bhūṣayennābhimasitenārabhūtalam|
arāṇi raktavarṇena tadantavalayaṃ sitaiḥ || 13.22 ||
asitairnemivasatiṃ sitairnemyantabhūtalam|
prathamāvaraṇaṃ kṛṣṇairdvitīyāvaraṇaṃ sitaiḥ || 13.23 ||
tṛtīyāvaraṇaṃ raktaiḥ padmāni ca yathāpuram|
raktapītāsitasitairdvārāṇīndrādidikkramāt || 13.24 ||
śaṅkhānarjunavarṇena madhyarekhāruṇāmatā|
<cakramaṇḍalasya varṇakalpanaprakāraḥ>
śvetaiḥ raktaiśca pītaiśca syāmaiḥ kṛṣṇaiśca pañcabhiḥ || 13.25 ||
varṇairvā kālakusumaiḥ rañjayeccakramaṇḍalam|
<maṇḍale bhagavataḥ ārādhanam anujñāyācanañca>
maṇḍalaṃ citrayitvaivaṃ prokṣya puṇyāhavāriṇā || 13.26 ||
bhadrapīṭhaṃ nyasedasmin tasmin devaṃ niveśayet|
ardhyapādyādibhirdevamarcayitvā praṇamya tam || 13.27 ||
bhagavan puṇḍarīkākṣa sarvalokeśvara prabho|
prītaye'nuṣṭhitaste'dya dhvajapūrvo mahotsavaḥ || 13.28 ||
mayā ca yajamānena ṛtvigbhiḥ sādhakairapi|
tatra nyūnātirekābhyāṃ duṣkṛtaṃ yadi jāyate || 13.29 ||
taddoṣaśāntaye puṣpairyaṣṭumicchāmi samprati|
anujānīhi māṃ deva pūrayasva kriyāmapi || 13.30 ||
iti vijñāpya deveśaṃ prārabhetkusumārcanam|
<maṇḍale tattaddevānām ārādhanasthānavidhinirdeśaḥ>
`dvādaśākṣara'varṇeśān binduṣu dvādaśasvapi || 13.31 ||
karṇikāyāṃ vāsudevamāvāhyābhyarcanaṃ tataḥ|
ardhyaṃ pādyaṃ tathācāmaṃ snānaṃ vastropavītakam || 13.32 ||
gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ ca upacārā nava smṛtāḥ|
pratyakṣaraṃ ca kusumairarcayetkalaśodbhava || 13.33 ||
aṅgāni hṛdayādīni kusumairañjalisthitaiḥ|
phalaṃ bhavatu me puṣpairnama ityantamuccaran || 13.34 ||
bhūṣaṇāni tatoddiśya śrīvatsaprabhṛtīni ca|
sudarśanādyāyudhāni puṣpaibhyarcanaṃ caret || 13.35 ||
kesareṣu śriyādyaṣṭau tathā puṣpaissamarcanam|
tato dvādaśaśaktīśca yajeta dalabhūmiṣu || 13.36 ||
dalāntavalaye śaktīrvyāptyādyāstāssamarcayet|
nābhibhāge'rvayedviṣṇumāvāhya kusumairbudhaḥ || 13.37 ||
viṣṇvādīn dvādaśāreṣu kusumaiḥ paripūjayet|
arāntavalaye devān matsyādīn nemibhūtale || 13.38 ||
śaṅkhādīnyāyudhānyaṣṭau prathamāvaraṇe bahiḥ|
keśavādīn dvādaśābje dvitīyāvaraṇāmbuje || 13.39 ||
vāsudevādayo'bhyarcyāstṛtīyāvaraṇe tathā|
ādityā dvādaśārādhyā padmeṣu dvādaśasvapi || 13.40 ||
pūrvadvāre khageśānaṃ dakṣiṇe tu sudarśanam|
gadāmapāṃpatidvāre some śaṅkhaṃ samarcayet || 13.41 ||
<maṇḍale bhagavataḥ puṣpārcanavidhiḥ>
tataḥ `puruṣasūktena' tathā `nārāyaṇena'ca|
`pañcopaniṣadai'rmantrairdevasya caraṇāmbuje || 13.42 ||
puṣpārcanaṃ prakurvīta dhūpadīpau pradarśayet|
vedaiścāpi tathā sāṅgaiḥ stotrayejjagadīśvaram || 13.43 ||
annaṃ nivedayettasmai pāyasādicaturvidham|
<maṇḍalārcanakrame homavidhiḥ>
āgneye havanaṃ kuṇḍe sthaṇḍile vā mahānase || 13.44 ||
samidājyacaruprāyaiḥ śatamaṣṭottaraṃ bhavet|
`mūlena' juhuyādagnau pūrṇāhutyavasānakam || 13.45 ||
<maṇḍalasthadevodvāsanādikramaḥ>
pradakṣiṇaṃ praṇāmaṃ ca kṛtvā stutvā śriyaḥ patim|
maṇḍalasthān samudvāsya prathamāvaraṇaṃ nayet || 13.46 ||
sevārtham āgatāndevān sthānaṃ gantuṃ niyojayet|
anyāṃśca mānavān sarvān parikramya pradakṣiṇam || 13.47 ||
nityairabhyantaraissārdhaṃ satataṃ nityasevakaiḥ|
mandirābhyantaraṃ devaṃ ninayeddeśikottamaḥ || 13.48 ||
<mahotsavānte guroḥ sammānanam>
evaṃ mahotsavaṃ kṛtvā sampūjya gurumṛtvijaḥ|
alaṅkṛtya viśeṣeṇa manaḥ prāhlādanairdhanaiḥ || 13.49 ||
taṃ guruṃ yānamāropya sarvamaṅgalasaṃyutam|
grāmaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya tadgṛhaṃ prāpayetprabhuḥ || 13.50 ||
<mahotsavaphalaśrutiḥ>
evaṃ mahotsavaṃ kurvan iha sarvatra modate|
paratra ca viśeṣeṇa kiṃ vā bhūyāditaḥ param || 13.51 ||
aśvamedhasahastrāṇi vājapeyāyutāni ca|
aptoryāmārbudaṃ kṛtvā yat phalaṃ jāyate nṛṇām || 13.52 ||
anena sarvaṃ devasya makhena bhavati dhruvam|
kevalaṃ devadevaṃ taṃ sevatāṃ ca mahotsave || 13.53 ||
phalamiṣṭaṃ karatale bhavatyeva na saṃśayaḥ|
kiṃ punastatra yuktānāṃ kaiṅkaryaniratātmanām || 13.54 ||
|| iti bhārgavatantre trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<14>
|| caturdaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<vivāhotsavanirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<pāṇigrahaṇotsavaprayojanaṃ vidhiṃ ca varṇayituṃ agastyasya prārthanā>
agastyaḥ
kathaṃ nu devadevīyaṃ pāṇigrahaṇamaṅgalam|
tatkārayitvā kiṃ bhūyāt tanmahyaṃ brūhi bhārgava || 14.1 ||
<udvāhotsavasya phalakīrtanam>
śrīrāmaḥ
devena saha devīnāṃ śayane parikalpite|
nodvāhakarma kurvīta tatkuryādanyathā yadi || 14.2 ||
sarvakalyāṇasampattirāyurvṛddhiśca jāyate|
abhipretārthasiddhiḥ syāddevīkalyāṇakāriṇām || 14.3 ||
naśyanti sarvaduḥkhāni pāpaiścāpi na lipyate|
dhanaṃ dhānyaṃ ca labhate pāṇigrahaṇakarmakṛt || 14.4 ||
<vivāhotsavakrame aṅkurārpaṇadinam>
udvāhadivasātpūrvam aṅkurārpaṇamācaret|
<devasya devyāśca kalyāṇamaṇḍape ānayanam>
kalyāṇe'hani kartavyaṃ pṛthakkaustukamaṅgalama || 14.5 ||
devaṃ devīṃ ca bhūṣādyairambaraiḥ kusumādibhiḥ|
prasādhayitvā śibikāmāropya gṛhadhāma ca || 14.6 ||
pradakṣiṇīkṛtya devau nayetkalyāṇamaṇḍapam|
<aṅkurārpaṇavidhinirūpaṇam>
viṣvaksenaṃ samārādhya karmavighnośāntaye || 14.7 ||
aṅkurāṇyarpayet sadyo yadvodvāhe prasannadhīḥ|
brahmādidevānārādhya pālikāsvapi pañcasu || 14.8 ||
bījapātraṃ samādāya namasyāmi diśāṃ patīn|
iti prārthya samāsīno `yā jātā' iti vidyayā || 14.9 ||
iti bījāni saṃjaptvā bījāvāpaṃ samācaret|
madhye tu brahmajajñānaṃ yata indraḥ purandare || 14.10 ||
yo'sya koṣṭhyaṃ yamadiśi `imaṃ me varuṇe'bhavet|
somo dhenuṃ tathā some bījāvāpassakṛdbhavet || 14.11 ||
tūṣṇīṃ dviranyai ṛtvigbhistūṣṇīṃ nyasanamucyate|
`mūrdhānami'ti mantreṇa mṛdbhirācchādya deśikaḥ || 14.12 ||
`imaṃ me varuṇe'tyeva secayecchuddhavāribhiḥ|
<devasya devyāśca haste kautukabandhaprakāraḥ>
puṇyāhapūrvaṃ kṛtvaivaṃ kuryātkautukabandhanam || 14.13 ||
saptavāraṃ pratisara'mastramantre'ṇa mantritam|
`tryambakena' ṛcālipya candanakṣodavāriṇā || 14.14 ||
`viśvettāte'ti mantreṇa haste devasya dakṣiṇe|
bandhayet svasvamantreṇa devīnāmitare kare || 14.15 ||
<rakṣāvidhiḥ>
spṛṣṭvā dakṣiṇahastena `yo brahme'tyabhimantrayet|
`bṛhatsāme'ti mantreṇa rakṣā syāddhūpabhasmanā || 14.16 ||
trikṛtvaḥ praṇameddevaṃ `puṇḍarīkākṣa'vidyayā|
<śubhikābandhanavidhiḥ puṣpamālikābandhanavidhiścaḥ>
ullikhyāgniṃ pratiṣṭhāpya `śubhike śira'ityṛcā || 14.17 ||
śubhikāṃ bandhayenmūrdhni dampatīnāṃ yathākramam|
`yāmāharadi'ti ṛcā bandhayetpuṣpamālikām || 14.18 ||
pādaprakṣālanaṃ viṣṇoḥ tathā trīni padeti ca|
`mayi maha' iti mantreṇa ātmānamabhimarśayet || 14.19 ||
<pāṇigrahaṇavidhinirūpakakrame prokṣaṇaprakāraḥ>
tatastu mṛṇmaye pātre sākṣate puṣpasaṃyute|
arhaṇāyāmbunāpūryaṃ gṛhṇīyātkarayordvayoḥ || 14.20 ||
`arhaṇīyā āpa' iti prārthya devaṃ tataḥ param|
`āmāganni'ti mantreṇa japetpātrāmbudeśikaḥ || 14.21 ||
haste tu devadevasya `virojo doha' ityṛcā|
ninīya nītamudakaṃ `samudraṃ' veti coccaran || 14.22 ||
prokṣayetkūrcamādāya gāṃ ca dadyāttadā hareḥ|
`yuvaṃ vastreti' mantreṇa vastreṇācchādayeddharim || 14.23 ||
<yajñopavītakalpanaṃ pādayoḥ akṣatārpaṇañca>
yajñopavītamantreṇa yajñasūtraṃ prakalpayet|
`irāvatīti'mantreṇa pādayorakṣatārpaṇam || 14.24 ||
<madhuparkanivedanāpośanakramaḥ>
madhuparkaṃ svarṇapātre gṛhītvā taṃ triruccaran|
`trayyai' `āmāgannita' ca dvābhyāṃ tamabhimantrayet || 14.25 ||
`amṛtopastaraṇama'sītyāpośanamatha smṛtam|
`yanmadhune'ti ca japan madhuparkaṃ nivedayet || 14.26 ||
`amṛtādhāna'mantreṇa punarāpośanaṃ matam|
<govisarjanaprakāraḥ>
gāṃ pradarśyātha haraye `gaurgau`ra'sīti vidyayā || 14.27 ||
praṇavo caṃ cikituṣe iti gāmabhimantrayet|
`omutsṛjata'ityuktvā gāṃ visṛjya tato guruḥ || 14.28 ||
<annanivedanakramaḥ>
`bhūtaṃ seti' vadan dhīmān annaṃ tasmai nivedayet|
`oṃ kalpayata' ityuccaiḥ brūyāddeśikasattamaḥ || 14.29 ||
<bhagavate devyāḥ dānaprakāraḥ>
tato nūtanavastreṇa paridhāya `yuvāsviti'|
divyairābharaṇairmālyairalaṅkṛtya śriyaṃ bhuvam || 14.30 ||
prāṇānāyamya saṃkalpya dātā bhūṣaṇabhūṣitām|
svagotranāmakathitāṃ devīmudakapūrvakam || 14.31 ||
suvarṇorvīyutāṃ dadyāt viṣṇave ca svayaṃbhuve|
kanyāṃ kanakasampannāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām || 14.32 ||
dāsyāmi viṣmave tubhyaṃ brahmalokajigīṣayā|
ityuktvā dakṣiṇaṃ hastaṃ gītavāditranisvanaiḥ || 14.33 ||
`devasya tveti' mantreṇa tāṃ guruḥ paramātmanaḥ|
arpayeddakṣiṇe haste sahiraṇyodakaṃ karam || 14.34 ||
praṇavonātha yuñjīta devena kamalāṃ bhuvam|
tataḥ pāṇigṛhītāyāḥ devyā devasya mūrdhani || 14.35 ||
`dhravaṃ teti' prakīryante kusumānyakṣatānyapi|
`abhrātṛdhnīmiti' ṛcaṃ japtvā paścāt svayaṃbhuve || 14.36 ||
`aghoracakṣuṣā' devīṃ darśayetkalaśodbhava|
kartavyānyapi karmāṇi pālanīyāstathā prajāḥ || 14.37 ||
āvābhyāmiti saṃkalpastayostaṃ ca vicintayet|
darbhaṃ saṃgṛhya tadanu japannidamahaṃ tviti || 14.38 ||
bhruvormadhyena saṃspṛśya prācyāṃ diśi visarjayet|
tato`jīvāṃ rudantīti' japtvā vedavido dvijān || 14.39 ||
`vyukṣakrūrami'ti śrutyā snānīyāya niyojayet|
`aryamna' iti mantreṇa darbheṇa valayaṃ śriyaḥ || 14.40 ||
`nidhāya khena sa' iti yugacchidraṃ niveśayet|
`śaṃ te hiraṇyam' ityuktvā svarṇaṃ chidre pidhāya ca || 14.41 ||
dvijāhṛtadalairdevīm abliṅgābhiśca pañcabhiḥ|
snāpayitvā'gnimūrdheti' plotavastreṇa mārjayet || 14.42 ||
`paritvā' `gīrvaṇā' dvābhyāṃ vastrābhyāṃ chādanaṃ śriyaḥ|
`dvādaśākṣaramantreṇa' namaḥ praṇavavidyayā || 14.43 ||
suvarṇaṃ bandhayetkaṇṭhe sarvamaṅgalasaṃyutam|
`āśāsāna saumanasam' iti yoktraṇa bandhayet || 14.44 ||
<udvāhahomaprakāraḥ>
udvāhahomaṃ kurvīta uttarāśāmukho guruḥ|
paristīrya yathānyāyaṃ purastāt tantramācaret || 14.45 ||
samidbhiścaruṇā cājyairhoma udvāhakarmaṇi|
samidbhirājyairjuhuyāt `devyāḥ sūktena' ṣoḍaśa || 14.46 ||
caruṇā `viṣṇugāyatryā' caturviṃśāhutirbhavet|
`śrīsūktastu' śriyo devyāḥ `bhūsūkta'stu bhuvo bhavet || 14.47 ||
anyāsāṃ yadi devīnāṃ tāsāṃ mūlena ṣoḍaśa|
<lājāhomaprakāraḥ prāyaścittāhutyādiprakāraśca>
tataḥ `puruṣasūktena' lājahomaṃ samācaret || 14.48 ||
analaṃ triḥparikramya lājaśeṣaṃ sakṛdguruḥ|
juhuyātpraṇaveṇāgnau `pañca vāruṇikaiḥ' punaḥ || 14.49 ||
prāyaścittāhutiṃ hutvā dvādaśākṣaravidyayā|
pūrṇāhutividhānaṃ syāt brāhmaṇānapi bhojayet || 14.50 ||
toṃṣayitvāthadeveśaṃ devīṃ cābhyarcya deśikaḥ|
puṇyāhaṃ vācayetpaścāt madhuparkaṃ nivedayet || 14.51 ||
nīrājayettadāśīśca piṇḍikākṣepaṇaṃ tathā|
<vivāhotsavakrame dainandinakṛtyam>
pratyahaṃ bhojayedviprān snāpayecca yathāvidhi || 14.52 ||
utsavaṃ cānvahaṃ kuryāt prātaḥ sāyaṃ yathāvidhi|
agnau ca juhuyānnityaṃ sandhyayorubhayorapi || 14.53 ||
pālikādevatāssarvāḥ pūjayedanvahaṃ guruḥ|
<vivāhotsavakrame caturthadinakṛtyam>
caturthe'hani samprāpte turyayāme yathāvidhi || 14.54 ||
udvāhaśeṣahomaṃ tat kārayetkamalāpateḥ|
sarpiṣā `dampatīmantraiḥ' kuryādaṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 14.55 ||
prāyaścittāhutiṃ kuryāt `pañcopaniṣadā' punaḥ|
pūrṇāhutiṃ tataḥ kuryāt `mūlamantreṇa' deśikaḥ || 14.56 ||
sailādipūgaṃ tāmbūlaṃ praṇavena nivedayet|
tāmbūlaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ cāpi brāhmaṇebhyaḥ pradāpayet || 14.57 ||
<vivāhotsavakrame pañcamadinakṛtyam>
pañcame divase tailābhyaṃgaṃ tayorbhavet|
mahāhavirnivedyātha kārayedutsavaṃ mahat || 14.58 ||
<itarāsāṃ devīnāṃ vivāhavidhiḥ>
udvāhānantaraṃ devaṃ devīsahitamarcayet|
devīnāmitarāsāṃ ca vivāhavidhirīdṛśaḥ || 14.59 ||
|| iti bhārgavatantre caturdaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<15>
|| pañcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<vividhapratiṣṭhāvidhinirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<vidhyādidevānāṃ vimānādīnāṃ ca pratiṣṭhākramaviṣayakaḥ praśnaḥ>
agastyaḥ
vidhirudrārkasutrāmukuberādyamṛtāśinām|
tārkṣyavighneśasarpendracaṇḍādyamalasūriṇām || 15.1 ||
vimānamaṇḍapādīnāṃ balipīṭhādikasya ca|
ghaṇṭākṣamālāyānānāṃ devīnāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ || 15.2 ||
prabhāpadmāsanādīnāṃ pādukāyāśca bhārgava|
vāpīkūpataṭākānāṃ pratiṣṭhā kīdṛśī prabho || 15.3 ||
<vividhapratiṣṭhākrame sāmānyaviṣayāḥ>
śrīrāmaḥ
śṛṇu vakṣye muniśreṣṭha pratiṣṭhā vividhā api|
aṅkurārpaṇamārabhya kriyāḥ pūrvaṃ yathā tathā || 15.4 ||
<svatantraparatantrapratiṣṭhayoḥ kumbhasaṃkhyādivicāraḥ>
svātantrye navakuṃbhāḥ syurekakuṃbhaḥ paratra cet|
vardhanyaiva mahākuṃbhasthāpanaṃ sarvakarmasu || 15.5 ||
āvāhanaṃ mahākuṃbhe pratiṣṭhā yasya kārayet|
upakuṃbhāṣṭake kuryādindrādīnāṃ samarcanam || 15.6 ||
ekasminneva kuṇḍe syāddhavanaṃ nānyathā bhavet|
tattannāma catuthyaiva mantroddhāraṃ samācaret || 15.7 ||
<vāhanādipratiṣṭhākrame viśeṣaḥ>
praṇavādinamontaṃ syādarcanaṃ vāhanādiṣu|
svāhāntaṃ havane mantraḥ pratiṣṭhotsavakarmasu || 15.8 ||
snapanaṃ navabhiḥ kuṃbhairnānyathā parikalpayet|
mṛtivastukacitrāṇāṃ snapanaṃ darpaṇe bhavet || 15.9 ||
devīnāṃ tu śriyādīnāṃ viśeṣaḥ kiñciducyate|
prāgbhāge kamale kuṇḍhe śriyamāvāhya pūjayet || 15.10 ||
dvāre caṇḍīṃ pracaṇḍīñca viṣvaksenapade punaḥ|
sumukhīṃ sthāpayeddhīmān kuṃbheṣu navasu kramāt || 15.11 ||
mahākuṃbhe'rcayeddevīṃ pratiṣṭhāpya svanāmataḥ|
sudarśanaṃ ca karake prāgādyaṣṭasu sa kramāt || 15.12 ||
vāgīśvarī kriyā kīrtirlakṣmīḥ sṛṣṭistathaiva ca|
prahvī satyā tathā brāhmī yaṣṭavyāḥ kalaśeṣvimāḥ || 15.13 ||
anyatsarvaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ viśeṣo nāsti kaścana|
āyudhanyāsayajanaṃ viṣṇorekasya kīrtitam || 15.14 ||
<vimānamaṇḍapādīnāṃ pratiṣṭhākrame viśeṣaḥ>
vimānamaṇḍapādīnāṃ viśeṣamavadhāraya|
nṛsūktarūpiṇaṃ devaṃ dhyātvā nārāyaṇaṃ harim || 15.15 ||
chāyāsnapanamādarśe sakūrce parikalpayet|
śayanaṃ ca mahāvedyāṃ kūrcadvārā samācaret || 15.16 ||
`agnirme vāci' mantreṇa kūrcaṃ spṛṣṭvābhimantrayet|
mahākubhe ca kuṇḍe ca viṣṇugāyatriyā guruḥ || 15.17 ||
āvāhya pūjayeddhomaṃ tathā kalaśasaṃbhava|
samidbhirājyaiścarubhistilairhutvā yathākramam || 15.18 ||
mahāvyāhṛtibhirhutvā saṃpātājyeta secayet|
anyatsarvaṃ yathāpūrvamevaṃ vai maṇḍapasya ca || 15.19 ||
<balipīṭhapratiṣṭhākrame viśeṣaḥ>
balipīṭhāni sarvāṇi sākṣāttoye'dhivāsayet|
snāpayecca yathānyāyyaṃ sthāpayeddhānyarāśiṣu || 15.20 ||
dhyānamāvāhanaṃ cāpi homaścāpi vimānavat|
oṃ bhūtebhya nama iti mahāpīṭhamanurbhavet || 15.21 ||
anyeṣāmevamovohyaṃ pīṭhānāṃ parikalpane|
<dhvajapratiṣṭhāyāṃ viśeṣaḥ>
dhvajastaṃbhapratiṣṭhāyāṃ devatā tu khageśvaraḥ || 15.22 ||
na dṛṣṭimokṣaṇaṃ tatra tathā prāsādapīṭhayoḥ|
nyāsahome tu samprāpte vimānavadihācaret || 15.23 ||
<vedyāḥ pratiṣṭhākrame viśeṣaḥ>
vedyāṃ śayanamapyevaṃ snānaṃ cāmbvadhivāsanam|
balipīṭhavidhānoktamanyat sarvaṃ yathāpuram || 15.24 ||
<pādukāyāḥ ghaṇṭāyāśaaca pratiṣṭhākrame viśeṣaḥ>
pādukāyāśca ghaṇṭāyāḥ balipīṭhakriyā bhavet|
pādukādevatā śeṣaḥ ghaṇṭāyāḥ kamalālayā || 15.25 ||
<mahānasapratiṣṭhāyāṃ viśeṣaḥ>
mahānasapratiṣṭhāyāṃ lakṣmīṃ tatra niveśayet|
culyā madhye tu dhātāraṃ vidhātāraṃ tu pṛṣṭhataḥ || 15.26 ||
<dvāradevatāpratiṣṭhāyāṃ viśeṣaḥ>
dharmādharmadvayaṃ sthāpyaṃ savye savyetare tathā|
caṇḍapracaṇḍau taddvāre śiṣṭaṃ karma vimānavat || 15.27 ||
<vāpīkūpādīnāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāṃ viśeṣaḥ>
vāpīkūpataṭākānāṃ varuṇo devatā bhavet|
aṅkurārṇyarpayitvādau kumbhasthāpanamācaret || 15.28 ||
kautukaṃ bandhayetkumbhe kūrcena śayanaṃ bhavet|
tattvābhimantraṇaṃ homo vimānavidhinā bhavet || 15.29 ||
jalādhivāsanaṃ naiva tathā nayanamokṣaṇam|
sarvamanyadvidhātavyaṃ pratiṣṭhāsamaye guruḥ || 15.30 ||
gaṅgādyāḥ saritastatra mantrairāvāhayejjale|
mahākuṃbhajalaṃ tatra varuṇarcāniyojayet || 15.31 ||
<akṣamālāpratiṣṭhākrame viśeṣaḥ>
akṣamālāpratiṣṭhāyāṃ gomūtre tvadhivāsayet|
pañcagavyena saṃkṣālya padmapatre niveśayet || 15.32 ||
puṇyāhavāribhiḥ prokṣya kuṅkumenānulepayet|
śālipīṭhe vinikṣipya `agnirme'tyabhimantrya ca || 15.33 ||
viṣṇuśaktirbhaveddevī manuraṣṭākṣaro bhavet|
prokṣaṇādikriyāṃ sarvāṃ yathāvidhi samācaret || 15.34 ||
<dhūpādīnāṃ pratiṣṭhākrame viśeṣaḥ>
dhūpasya devatā vahnirdīpasya ca divākaraḥ|
chatracāmaravādyānāṃ puṇyāhaprokṣaṇaṃ bhavet || 15.35 ||
<caṇḍādidevānāṃ hetīnāṃ ca pratiṣṭhākrame viśeṣaḥ>
caṇḍādidevatānāṃ ca hetīnāṃ ca jagadguroḥ|
indrādīnāmiva bhavet viśeṣo nāsti kaścana || 15.36 ||
<bhaktabimbapratiṣṭhāvidhiḥ>
bhaktabimbapratiṣṭhāyāṃ kuryātpratisarāṅkure|
vivāhoktena vidhinā kumbhamekaṃ samarcayet || 15.37 ||
ekasminneva kurvīta kuṇḍe homādi pūrvavat|
na homo na balistīrthaṃ na dhvajārohaṇakriyā || 15.38 ||
mṛgayā ca jaladroṇī na bhaktotsavakarmaṇi|
<rathādiyānapratiṣṭhāyāṃ viśeṣaḥ>
rathādiyānajātasya devatā vihagādhipaḥ || 15.39 ||
kumbhamekaṃ samabhyarcya kuṇḍe vā sthaṇḍile'pi vā|
mūrtihomamanuṣṭhāya prokṣayetkuṃbhavāriṇā || 15.40 ||
<gṛhārcāpratiṣṭhākramaḥ tatphalañca>
gṛhārcāsthāpane sarvaṃ pratiṣṭhākarma pūrvavat|
gṛhavaṃśarddhikṛddevaḥ sadanasthāpito hariḥ || 15.41 ||
<mandire devasthāpanaphalam>
mandire sthāpito devaḥ pālayedbhuvanatrayam|
|| iti bhārgavatantre pañcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<16>
|| ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<samārādhanavidhinirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<ārādhakasyālayapraveśakramaḥ>
śrīrāmaḥ
snātvā pūrvāhnikaṃ kṛtvā gururdevālayādbahiḥ|
prakṣālya pādau pāṇī ca trirācamyālayaṃ viśet || 16.1 ||
<kavāṭeddhāṭanaṃ bhagavatprabodhanañca>
prathamāvaraṇaṃ gatvā natvoddiśya śriyaḥ patim|
kṛte tālatraye paścāt kavāṭoddhāṭanaṃ caret || 16.2 ||
tadā prabodhayeddevaṃ vāsudevaṃ sanātanam|
prabuddha jagadīśāna prabuddha kamalāpate || 16.3 ||
prabuddha puṇḍarīkākṣa prabuddha ramayā saha|
prabuddhānantakalyāṇavāridhe karuṇāmbudhe || 16.4 ||
prabuddha candravadana kamalāṅghrisaroruha|
stuvanti vedāścatvāraḥ namanti druhiṇādayaḥ || 16.5 ||
astācalaṃ gataścandraḥ udayaṃ yāti bhāskaraḥ|
unmīlayākṣiyugalaṃ nṛtyatyapsarasāṃ gaṇaḥ || 16.6 ||
tumbururnāradaścobhau vīṇayā mañju gāyataḥ|
tiṣṭhanti ṛṣayaḥ sarve sevārthaṃ gopurādbahiḥ || 16.7 ||
bālātapā visarpanti prabuddhānyambujānyapi|
ṣaṭpadā makarandāya bhramanti kamalodare || 16.8 ||
cakravākadvijagaṇairgārhasthyamanubhūyate|
uttiṣṭha iti mantraistvāmupāsante dvijottamāḥ || 16.9 ||
uttiṣṭhotiṣṭha govinda uttiṣṭha puruṣottama|
<nirmālyaviniyogaḥ pātraprakṣālanaṃ ca>
evaṃ prabodhya deveśaṃ dīpeṣu jvaliteṣvapi || 16.10 ||
mālyagandhādidevasya viṣvaksenāya dāpayet|
pūjāyogyāni pātrāṇi kṣālayecca yathātatham || 16.11 ||
<bhūtaśuddhiprakāraḥ>
sapavitrakaro bhūtvā sordhvapuṇḍraḥ svalaṅkṛtaḥ|
nādite sakale vādye brahmadhoṣe ca ghoṣite || 16.12 ||
śabdabrahmamayīṃ dhyātvā dhaṇṭāṃ hastena cālayet|
`astramantreṇa' badhniyāddeśikaḥ kakubho daśa || 16.13 ||
`mūlamantreṇa' kurvīta prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ tataḥ|
recakaṃ purakaṃ caiva kubhakaṃ recakaṃ tathā || 16.14 ||
evaṃ caturvidhairyuktaḥ prāṇāyāma udāhṛtaḥ|
saptabhirdviguṇairvāpi punastadvaccaturguṇaiḥ || 16.15 ||
ādyaiśca `mūlamantra'sya saṃkhyoktā śāstravittamaiḥ|
gandhena sahitāṃ pṛthvīṃ saṃharedapsu yogavit || 16.16 ||
tā rasena samaṃ vahnau taṃ vāyau rūpamātrayā|
tamākāśe sparśayutaṃ tacca śabdena mānase || 16.17 ||
saṃharottadahaṃkāre taṃ buddhau tāṃ mahatyapi|
mahāntaṃ saṃharejjīve jīvaṃ taṃ brahmaṇi dhruve || 16.18 ||
niveśya vahnibījena dagdhaṃ dhyāyedvapurguruḥ|
vāruṇenaiva bījena siktaṃ jātaṃ punaḥ smaret || 16.19 ||
brahmaṇo nirgataṃ jīvaṃ smṛtvā tasmādgurūttamaḥ|
mahāntaṃ ca tato buddhiṃ buddherjātāmahaṃkṛtim || 16.20 ||
ahaṃkṛtermano dhyāyet tasmācchabdena cāmbaram|
sparśana cāmbarādvāyuṃ vāyo rūpeṇa cānalam || 16.21 ||
analādrasasaṃyuktā āpaśtābhyo mahīṃ smaret|
gandhena sahitāmevaṃ viśodhya vapurātmavān || 16.22 ||
<bhūtaśuddhikrame aṅgeṣu varṇanyāsakramaḥ>
nyasedaṅgeṣu `mūla'sya varṇānaṣṭau yathākramam|
vinyasetpraṇavaṃ nābhau nakāraṃ mehane nyaset || 16.23 ||
mokāraṃ jānuyugale nākāraṃ pādayordvayoḥ|
rākāraṃ vinyasenmūrdhni yakāraṃ nayanadvaye || 16.24 ||
ṇākāraṃ vadanāmbhoje yakāraṃ hṛdayāmbuje|
evaṃ vinyasya varṇāṃstān dhyātvā taṃ puruṣottamam || 16.25 ||
<mānasayāgavidhiḥ>
manasārādhayeddevamardhyādyairvividhaiḥ kramaiḥ|
ardhyaṃ pādyaṃ tathācāmaṃ snānaṃ vastropavītake || 16.26 ||
gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ tathā dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ puṣpāñjaliṃ tathā|
naivedyaṃ vividhaṃ cāti tāmbūlaṃ mukhavāsanām || 16.27 ||
ityādi mānasenaiva `mūlamantre'ṇa kārayet|
mūlamantraṃ japetpaścādaṣṭottaraśatāvaram || 16.28 ||
<pūjāpātrādisannāhaḥ>
devasya purataḥ pātre ādhāre pañca vinyaset|
pātrāpi pariśuddhāni śuddhalohamayāni ca || 16.29 ||
āvāhanāhvayaṃ pātramanyadekaṃ niveśayet|
pūrayedgalitāmbhobhiḥ `praṇave'nābhimantrayet || 16.30 ||
śoṣaṇaṃ plāvanaṃ cāpi kuryā`dagnyamṛtākṣaraiḥ'|
darśayetsurabhīmudrāṃ taccaturthyā namo'ntayā || 16.31 ||
pādyaṃ madhye dakṣiṇe'rdhyaṃ vāme cācāmavāri ca|
snānīyaṃ śāṅkare koṇe śuddhodamitaratra ca || 16.32 ||
spṛśan dakṣiṇahastena tattannāma smaran dvijaḥ|
uccārya kalpayāmīti pātrādardhyājjalaṃ haret || 16.33 ||
kiñcidanyena pātreṇa ninayennāsikāsamam|
saṃmantrya `mūlamantre'ṇa saptavāraṃ dvijottamaḥ || 16.34 ||
prokṣayetpañcapātrāṇi pūjādravyāṇyaśeṣataḥ|
<nityārcanātpūrvaṃ yogapīṭhakalpanaprakāraḥ>
pādapīṭhe tu devasya mūlabere gurūttamaḥ || 16.35 ||
`āyāntvādyena' mantreṇa yogapīṭhaṃ prakalpayet|
ādhāraśaktiḥ prathamā kūrmakālānalastataḥ || 16.36 ||
ananto'tha mahīpaścādūrdhvamūrdhvaṃ yathākramam|
dharmo jñānaṃ ca vairāgyamaiśvaryaṃ ca yathākramam || 16.37 ||
prāgādidikṣu kramaśaḥ adharmādicatuṣṭayam|
āgneyyādiṣu koṇeṣu teṣāṃ madhye sadāśivaḥ || 16.38 ||
ete pādamayāḥ proktāḥ khaṭvāyāḥ kamalāpateḥ|
ṛgādivedāścatvāraḥ khaṭvākāṣṭhacatuṣṭayam || 16.39 ||
vaikārikastaijasaśca bhautikaśceti te tridhā|
pūrvāparaguṇāḥ proktāstathā satvādayo guṇāḥ || 16.40 ||
dakṣiṇottarasūtrāṇi tūlikā pañcabhūmikā|
jīvātmāstaraṇaṃ tasmādūrdhvaṃ padmāsanaṃ smṛtam || 16.41 ||
pārśvayorubhayoḥ kalpyau parameṣṭhitrilocanau|
sanatkumāraḥ sanakaḥ sanandaḥ pṛṣṭhatastrayaḥ || 16.42 ||
eteṣāntu namo'ntena caturthyā praṇavādinā|
arcayenmantramuddhṛtya tantraśāstrārthatattavavit || 16.43 ||
tato devaṃ samālokya sāmmukhyaṃ śāśvatīṃ sthitim|
prārthya mantrākṣaranyāsaḥ kartavyaḥ pūrvavartmanā || 16.44 ||
<karmārcāyāḥ yogapīṭhakalpanakramaḥ>
karmārcāsahaje pīṭhe yogapīṭhaṃ yathāpuram|
<bhagavataḥ nityārcanavidhiḥ>
prakalpyāvāhane pātre jalamardhyārthamāharet || 16.45 ||
lalāṭāntaṃ samuddhṛtya mūlenāgaccha cañcunā|
āvāhayenmūlaberāt siñcenmūrdhani karmaṇaḥ || 16.46 ||
praṇavena brahmarandhraṃ pidhāya kamalāpateḥ|
sāmmukhyaṃ svāgataṃ coktvā sthitiṃ yāgāvasānikīm || 16.47 ||
pārthya mantrākṣarānnyasya yajedardhyādibhiḥ kramāt|
`sāvitryā' vitaredardhyaṃ pādyaṃ `trīṇi padeti' ca || 16.48 ||
ācāmo `viṣṇugāyatryā' `tadviṣṇo' dantadhāvanam|
jihvānirlehanaṃ kuryāt `tadviprāso' viparyayāt || 16.49 ||
`ṛtaṃ satyami'ti śrutyā gaṇḍūṣaparikalpanam|
`pauruṣeṇaiva sūktena' snāpayetpuruṣottamam || 16.50 ||
`yuvā suvāse'ti ṛcā vastre dve dhautanirmale|
`yajñopavītamantreṇa' brahmasūtraṃ prakalpayet || 16.51 ||
`gandhadvāreti'mantreṇa gandhenālepanaṃ hareḥ|
`viṣṇornukami'ti śrutyā puṣpaiśca parimaṇḍayet|| 16.52 ||
`jitanta' iti mantreṇa bhūṣayetsarvabhūṣaṇaiḥ|
`irāvatīti' mantreṇa pādayorakṣatārpaṇam || 16.53 ||
dhūpaḥ kālāgarumukhaiḥ `dhūrasī'ti manuṃ japet|
`uddīpyasve'ti mantreṇa dīpānujvālya darśayet || 16.54 ||
ādhoṣya caturo devān nṛttaṃ gītaṃ ca darśayan|
<bhagavataḥnityārcanakrame naivedyasamarpaṇaprakāraḥ>
arcayedvividhaiḥ puṣpairnāmabhirbahubhirharim || 16.55 ||
havīṃṣi sukhapakvāni vividhāni mahānasāt|
siktāni sarpibhiḥ samyak sopadaṃśāni pānakaiḥ || 16.56 ||
ānītāni yathāyogaṃ vinyastānyāsane'grataḥ|
prokṣayedastramantreṇa varmaṇā pariṣecayet || 16.57 ||
`devasya tveti' mantreṇa darśayat grāsamudrayā|
tripañca sapta vāraṃ vā devāya vinivedayet || 16.58 ||
evaṃ nivedya vidhivat sodakaṃ sopadaṃśakam|
tāmbūlīṃ vāsanāyuktāṃ dadyāccaraṇamityapi || 16.59 ||
<nityārādhanakrame homādisampādananirdeśaḥ>
tato nirgatya sadanānnityahomaṃ samāpayet|
baliṃ ca dadyādbhūtebhyo nityotsavamathācaret || 16.60 ||
<nittārcanaprasaṃge nīrājanodvāsanādikramaḥ>
tataḥ praviśya sadanaṃ nīrājya `carameṇa' ca|
udvāsayenmūlabere yathāpūrvaṃ gurūttamaḥ || 16.61 ||
japerdaṣṭākṣaraṃ mantraṃ' yathāśakti praṇamya ca|
svīkṛtya pādasalilaṃ pibetttriḥ śirasā punaḥ || 16.62 ||
dhārayetpādukāṃ cāpi tulasyādi tathā haret|
sevārthamāgatānāṃ ca yatīnāṃ viduṣāmapi || 16.63 ||
anyeṣāṃ viṣṇubhaktānāṃ pādyādi vitaredguruḥ|
<bhagavanniveditānnaviniyogaḥ>
viṣṇorniveditānnādi svīkṛtya svayamātmanaḥ || 16.64 ||
sevārthamāgatebhyaśca sarvebhyo'pi pradāpayet|
viṣṇorniveditānnena yaṣṭavyā gṛhadevatāḥ || 16.65 ||
bhuñjīta viṣṇunaivedyam devībhyo viniveditam|
<caṇḍādiniveditānnaviniyogādiḥ>
caṇḍādiparivārāṇāṃ yadannaṃ viniveditam || 16.66 ||
tanna bhuñjīta manujo jale'gnau vā vinikṣit|
taranti pitaraścāpi tena tṛptāḥ na saṃśayaḥ || 16.67 ||
idamārādhanaṃ viṣṇoḥ kathitaṃ te samāsataḥ|
|| iti bhārgavatantre ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<17>
|| saptadaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<agnikāryanirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<agnikuṇḍa homasampādanātpūrvaṃ kartavyāni kṛtyāni>
śrīrāmaḥ
dvārapārśvasthitau devau pūjayitvāgnisadmanaḥ|
praviśya gomayāmbhobhiḥ kuṇḍamālipya paścime || 17.1 ||
āsane prāṅmukho bhūtvā samāveśyātmadakṣiṇe|
kusumāni tathā vāme dravyāṇyanyāni kalpayet || 17.2 ||
prāṇānāyamya saṅkalpya kuṇḍasyottarabhūtale|
prāgagrānnikṣipeddarbhān kramātteṣu vinikṣipet || 17.3 ||
ājyāpātraṃ caruṃ sthālīṃ prokṣaṇīpātramapyatha|
praṇītāṃ samidho darbhāṃstaṇḍulān paridhitrayam || 17.4 ||
sruksruvau lakṣaṇopetau darvīṃ vyajanamekṣaṇe|
kāṣṭhaṃ ghaṇṭāmakṣatāṃśca gandhadravyāṇi cetarān || 17.5 ||
vinyasya kuṇḍāntarāle prāgagra udagāyatāḥ|
tisrastisro likhedrekhāḥ praṇavena pṛthak pṛthak || 17.6 ||
visṛjya lekhanaṃ kūrcaṃ hastau prakṣālya vāriṇā|
darbhastaṃbadvayenādbhirabhyukṣyāgniṃ niveśayet || 17.7 ||
adbhiḥ parisamūhyāstraiḥ paristīryānalaṃ kuśaiḥ|
daśabhirdaśabhiḥ sāgraiḥ prāgargrarudagañcalaiḥ || 17.8 ||
dravyāgnimadhye'pāṃ pūrṇāṃ praṇītāṃ viniveśayet|
sākṣatāṃ darbhayugmena kūrcaṃ nirmāya vinyaset || 17.9 ||
tatrāvāhyārcayeddevaṃ vāsudevaṃ sanātanam|
agnerāgneyakoṇe tāmāsane viniveśayet || 17.10 ||
pavitritaistribhirdarbhaiḥ ācchādya prokṣaṇīṃ puraḥ|
vinyasedātmanastatra praṇītāṃ kūrcamānakaiḥ || 17.11 ||
adbhirāpūrya cotpūya pātrāṇyuttānayan guruḥ|
prokṣayetprokṣaṇāṃvobhirājyasthālīṃ samāharet || 17.12 ||
tasyāṃ vinyasya tatkūrcaṃ kūrce tanmayaviṣṭare|
agnerdakṣiṇato devamarcayeccaturānanam || 17.13 ||
pūrayitvājyapātraṃ tat galitenaiva sarpiṣā|
udagagniṃ niveśyāsmin ājyasthālīṃ niveśayet || 17.14 ||
dīptena barhiṣā tasya paryagnikaraṇaṃ caret|
darbhāgre dve niveśyāsmin punaḥ paryagnimācaret || 17.15 ||
udaguttīrya tatpātram agnimagnau niveśayet|
śrapayeddhṛdayādyaṅgaiḥ carupātre hutāśane || 17.16 ||
agneruttarattaḥ sthāpyaṃ carupātraṃ yathākramam|
ājyapātraṃ puraskṛtya trirutpūya gurūttamaḥ || 17.17 ||
kūrcagranthiṃ visṛjyādbhiḥ spṛṣṭvāgnau viniveśayet|
saurabheyīṃ darśayitvā ghṛtaṃ tadamṛtaṃ smaret || 17.18 ||
indrādīnagnikuṇḍasya paritaḥ pūjayet kramāt|
atitaptena toyena strukstruvau kṣālayedguruḥ || 17.19 ||
darbhapañcakakūrcena mūlamadhyāgrabhūmiṣu|
saṃmṛjyāgnau ca niṣṭapya tāvudīcyāṃ niveśayet || 17.20 ||
granthiṃ visṛjya kūrcasya spṛṣṭvāmbvagnau niveśayet|
homavastuṣu saṃsiñcet struveṇājyaṃ sakṛtsakṛt || 17.21 ||
udagagrā ca paridhirnyasanīyā ca paścime|
prāgagrā dakṣiṇe tadvadudīcyāṃ diśi vinyaset || 17.22 ||
āgneyaiśānayoḥ sthāpyavādhārasamidhāvubhau|
`aditenvi'tyādimantraiḥ pariṣecanamācaret || 17.23 ||
samidhaḥ pañcadaśa ca sikttvājyena tatassakṛt|
japa`nnaṣṭākṣaraṃ mantraṃ' dhyātvā ca parameṣṭhinam || 17.24 ||
ādadyānmudrayā muṣṭhyā veṣṭayitvā kuśaiḥ karam|
ājyapātraṃ samādāya prājāpatyaṃ tataḥ param || 17.25 ||
aindraṃ ca juhuyādagnau āgneyaṃ saumyameva ca|
vyastābhirvyāhṛtibhiśca samastābhiryathākramam || 17.26 ||
<homakrame vahnyādidhyānasvarūpam>
pariṣicya tato vahniṃ dhyāyeccatvāri śṛṅgayā|
omagnaye namaḥ svāhetyagnau pūrṇāhutirbhavet || 17.27 ||
dviśīrṣakaṃ saptahastaṃ tripādaṃ saptajihvakam|
varadaṃ śaktipāṇiṃ ca vibhrāṇaṃ sruksruvau tathā || 17.28 ||
abhītidaṃ carmadharaṃ vāme cājyadharaṃ kare|
<vahneḥ saptajihvānāṃ nāmasthānādivivekaḥ>
kālī karālī sumanā lohitā dhūmra eva hi || 17.29 ||
sphuliṅginī viśvarūpī sapta jihvāḥ prakīrtitāḥ|
kālyāstu madhyamaṃ sthānaṃ karālyāḥ pūrvadigbhavet || 17.30 ||
manojavāyāścāgneyyāṃ lohitāyāstu vāruṇe|
sudhūmrā somanilayā sphuliṅginyanilāśrayā || 17.31 ||
aiśānyāṃ visvarūpī tu evaṃ sthānaṃ smaret kramāt|
jihvāyāṃ dakṣiṇe vaktre dhūmrāyāṃ māraṇādikam || 17.32 ||
lohitāyāṃ vaśīkāraḥ kālyāṃ karma ca śāntikam|
sarvasiddhiḥ sphuliṅginyāmānane dakṣiṇetare || 17.33 ||
karālikā vijayadā puṣṭidā ca manoharā|
kārṣṇyaṃ lauhityametāsāṃ varṇaḥ śyāmatvameva ca || 17.34 ||
sphuliṅgarūpaṃ ca tato varṇaḥ spaṭikasannibhaḥ|
tapanīyanibhaḥ proktaḥ jihvāyāmānupūrvaśaḥ || 17.35 ||
<agnitrayalakṣaṇaṃ teṣāmupayogaśca>
nityāgnirvṛddha ityuktaḥ utsavāgniryuvā bhavet|
dīkṣāśāntipratiṣṭhāsu bālāgnirabhidhīyate || 17.36 ||
araṇīmathanājjātaḥ bālāgniriti kathyate|
sūryakāntāśmano jāto yauvanāgniḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 17.37 ||
laukikāgnistu vṛddhāgniragnitrayamudrāhṛtam|
<samidhaḥ pramāṇaṃ doṣayuktasamidhaḥ prayogaphalanirdeśaśca>
āyāmaḥ samidhāṃ tālaḥ kaniṣṭhānāhanaṃ matam || 17.38 ||
carmahīne vināśaḥ syāt bhede kṣīṇakulaṃ bhavet|
ārdrā tu bandhunāśaḥ syāt putranāśaḥ purātane || 17.39 ||
takṣāsu bhāryāmaraṇaṃ kalahāya ca śākhinī|
udvegāya bhavetsthūlā hnasvāvagrahakāriṇī || 17.40 ||
dīrghātivṛṣṭijananī tyājyā doṣayutā samit|
<kāmanānurūpaṃ samidhāṃ homadravyāṇāṃ ca vivaraṇam>
yajñavṛkṣodbhavaiśśāntissaubhāgyaṃ kusumairbhavet || 17.41 ||
dhūpadravyaissadārogyaṃ puṣṭirdadhnā payaśśuciḥ|
annena vividhān kāmān ājyenāyuṣmatīḥ prajāḥ || 17.42 ||
śvetapadmaistu juhuyādicchan brahmaśriyaṃ naraḥ|
lakṣmīpuṣpaistu juhuyāllakṣmīkāmo'tha vāruṇaiḥ || 17.43 ||
padmairbilvasamidbhirvā jñānakāmastu sarpiṣā|
kanyākāmo huvellājaiḥ gokāmo gomayaiḥ punaḥ || 17.44 ||
āyuṣkāmastu dūrvābhirbhūmikāmastu mṛtsnayā|
yavairindriyakāmastu tilaiḥ sarvajanapriyaḥ || 17.45 ||
brahmavarcasakāmastu brahmavṛkṣasamudbhavaiḥ|
vaiṇavaiśca yavaiścaiva nīvāraiḥ śālibhistathā || 17.46 ||
sarve kāmāśca siddhyanti hoturbījairyathoditaiḥ|
lājahomena siddhyanti sarvakāmāḥ na saṃśayaḥ || 17.47 ||
annenānnādikāmastu putrakāmastu pāyasaiḥ|
nimbāpuṣpairhiraṇyārthī sarve sidhyanti sarpiṣā || 17.48 ||
<tattaddhomadravyaparimāṇanirdeśaḥ>
home sarpirmadhukṣīradhārāḥ syuścaturaṅgulāḥ|
śuktirdadhnāhutirgrāsaṃ pāyasādyāhutirbhavet || 17.49 ||
bhakṣyāhutistadardhena phalaiḥ puṣpairakhaṇḍitaiḥ|
niṣpāvabījamānena dhūpadravyāhutirbhavet || 17.50 ||
aṣṭāṅgulā tadardhā vā samiddūrvā ṣaḍaṅgulā|
mṛdvikākṣapramāṇā tu gomayāhutirucyate || 17.51 ||
pūrṇāhutirghṛtasya syātkuḍavena munīśvara|
nyūnādhikapramāṇena havyakavyāhutīḥ kṛtāḥ || 17.52 ||
bhuñjate dānavāḥ daityāḥ na phalāya prakalpate|
<agnikuṇḍe hareḥ āvāhanam ārādhanaṃ ca>
varmaṇā pariṣicyāgnimagnau pīṭhaprakalpanam || 17.53 ||
ādhāraśaktyādimantrairvahnāvāvāhanaṃ hareḥ|
pūrvavatsakalaṃ kuryāt upacāramuṣarbudhe || 17.54 ||
taistairmantraiśca svāhāntaissarpiṣaiva sakṛt sakṛt|
nivedanāntaṃ kṛtvaivaṃ parivārārthamapyatha || 17.55 ||
hutvā kavacamantreṇa pūrvavatpariṣecayet|
etannityārcanaṃ vahnau pratiṣṭhādāvanantaram || 17.56 ||
<agnau nityārādhanavidhyādau viśeṣaḥ>
tattatkriyoktavidhinā homamanyatsamācaret|
nitye dhāryo vītihotraḥ na praṇītā na cātmabhūḥ || 17.57 ||
nedhyaprakṣepaṇaṃ cāgnau na caruśrapaṇaṃ bhavet|
ājyena kevalaṃ vahnau viṣṇoḥ pūjā japāvadhiḥ || 17.58 ||
nā'ghārahomaḥ kartavyaḥ agnau naṣṭe tu niṣkṛtiḥ|
pratiṣṭhādiṣu sarveṣu sarvametatsamācaret || 17.59 ||
karmāvasānaṃ brahmādīn nodvāsettantravittamaḥ|
<pūrṇāhutisampādanavidhiḥ>
prāyaścittāhutīrhutvā kuryātpūrṇāhutiṃ tataḥ || 17.60 ||
ātmano'gre sruvaṃ yāmye cājyasthālīṃ tathottare|
srucaṃ vinyasya hastābhyām ādadyātsāditān kuśān || 17.61 ||
ājyasthālyāṃ nyasenmūlaṃ madhye madhyaṃ srucītaram|
spṛṣṭvā trirevaṃ juhuyādagnau darbhān tathā dahet || 17.62 ||
paridhīnātmanaḥ pūrvaṃ praṇītāṃ vinyasedguruḥ|
āpūrya prokṣaṇītīrthaiḥ pūrvādyāśāsu secayet || 17.63 ||
avanyāṃ visṛjeccheṣaṃ prokṣayedātmanaḥ śiraḥ|
brahmāṇamudvasetpaścātsrucaṃ toyena pūrayet || 17.64 ||
pariṣicya tataḥ kuṇḍaṃ devamagneḥ samuddharet|
bhasmanā tilakaṃ kuryāt hutenādhāpanodinā || 17.65 ||
`agnenaye'ti mantreṇa hyupasthāya tato'nalam|
bhaktyā yadagnau vihitaṃ yathāśakti yathāvidhi || 17.66 ||
ārādhanaṃ tvaṃ deveśa gṛhāṇa parameśvara|
iti devapadāmbhoje nyaseddhavanajaṃ phalam || 17.67 ||
<pūrṇāhutividhiḥ>
agastyaḥ
śruto mayā paraṃdhāmnaḥ vahnau pūjākramo'dhunā|
pūrṇāhutividhānaṃ tatkathamadya bravīhi me || 17.68 ||
śrīrāmaḥ
arghyādyairarcayeddevamanale sarpiṣā gavām|
srucamājyena sampūrya kuḍavena gurūttamaḥ || 17.69 ||
darbheṇa samidhā yuktaṃ phalena paripūritam|
gandhaiḥ puṣpairakṣataiśca pūjayitvā srucaṃ guruḥ || 17.70 ||
apidhāya sruveṇātha nāsikāgrāntamuddharan|
utthāya vādya ghoṣeṣu sarvato nāditeṣu ca || 17.71 ||
`pūrṇāhutimi'ti manuṃ ghoṣayastu dvijātiṣu|
`mūlamantre'ṇa juhuyādanale vimale guruḥ || 17.72 ||
kurvīta sviṣṭakṛddhomaṃ kukkuṭāṇḍapramāṇakam|
caruṃ ghṛtāplutaṃ sarvairupadaṃśaissamanvitam || 17.73 ||
saśarkaraṃ ca samadhu sadarbhasamidhaṃ sruci|
vinyasya sarpiṣāplāvya sruvāgreṇāpidhāya ca || 17.74 ||
juhuyānmūla
<18>
|| aṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<nityotsavabalisamarpaṇavidhinirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<agnikāryasampādanānantaramutsavācaraṇa nirdeśaḥ>
śrīrāmaḥ
nityotsavavidhiṃ vakṣye yathāvadavadhāraya|
abhyarcyāgnau jagannāthamatha nityotsavaṃ caret || 18.1 ||
<nityotsavakrame balidravyānayanakramaḥ>
antaḥ praviśya sadanaṃ balibimbaṃ samarcayet|
śibikādau samāropya balidravyaṃ samāharet || 18.2 ||
sopadaṃśaṃ mahānnaṃ vā payodadhi ghṛtānvitam|
athavā śuddhamannaṃ vā balyarthaṃ parikalpayet || 18.3 ||
sarvārthatoyaṃ śuddhākhyaṃ gandhapuṣpākṣatāni ca|
dhūpaṃ dīpaṃ ca vibhrāṇāḥ vrajeyuḥ paricārakāḥ || 18.4 ||
gāyantaścāpi nṛtyantaḥ tālamaddalapāṇayaḥ|
vrajeyuragre devasya pradīpairbahubhiḥ saha || 18.5 ||
vyajanaṃ cāmaraṃ chatramutsavāṅgaṃ tathetarat|
sarvamādāya gaccheyurdevasyāgre'dhikāriṇaḥ || 18.6 ||
<balidānakramanirdeśaḥ>
caṇḍapracaṇḍāvārabhya balidānaṃ samācaret|
tattannāma tu devānāṃ caturthyā namasānvitam || 18.7 ||
praṇavādi samuccārya balidānaṃ samācaret|
idamardhyaṃ balirayam idaṃ te udakaṃ tviti || 18.8 ||
toyaṃ pūrvottaraṃ dadyāt tālanṛttasvarānvitam|
gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ tathā dīpamardhyaṃ balistataḥ || 18.9 ||
udakaṃ ca pradātavyaṃ prāṅgaṇe balikarmaṇi|
<tattaddevānāṃ svaranṛttatālavivekaḥ>
caṇḍādidvārapālānāṃ sarveṣāmṛṣabhasvaraḥ || 18.10 ||
nṛttaṃ tu vaijayantī syānmallatālamudāhṛtam|
viṣṇukrāntaṃ khageśasya nṛttaṃ tālaṃ tu gāruḍam || 18.11 ||
svarastu madhyamaḥ proktaḥ viṣvaksenasya kathyate|
nṛttaṃ svastikamityuktaṃ balitālarṣabhasvarau || 18.12 ||
tadvannṛttaṃ gaṇeśasya bhadratālaṃ tu pañcamaḥ|
svaraśca kuṃbhatanaya durgāyāḥ dhaivataḥ svaraḥ || 18.13 ||
sarvamaṅgalanṛttaṃ syāt bhadratālamudīritam|
brahmaṇo brahmatālaṃ syāt nṛttaṃ syāt sarvamaṅgalam || 18.14 ||
svarastu madhyamastasya śunāsīrasya kathyate|
vilāsanṛttamuditaṃ svaraḥ ṣaḍja udāhṛtaḥ || 18.15 ||
samatālaṃ tathā vahneḥ kathyate ṛṣabhasvaraḥ|
baddhāvatālaṃ nṛttaṃ tu sarvatobhadramīritam || 18.16 ||
yamasya kheṭakaṃ nṛttaṃ gāndhāraḥ svara ucyate|
tālaṃ tu śṛṅgiṇī proktā nirṛtermadhyamaḥ svaraḥ || 18.17 ||
mallatālaṃ tathā nṛttaṃ pathitaṃ cakramaṇḍalam|
varumasya svaraḥ proktaḥ pañcamo maṅgalāhvayaḥ || 18.18 ||
tālaṃ kāntāranṛttaṃ syāt maruto dhaivataḥ svaraḥ|
jayatālaṃ tu kathitaṃ nṛttaṃ syāt pṛṣṭhakuṭṭimam || 18.19 ||
somasya bhadratālaṃ syāt nartanaṃ kaṭibandhanam|
svaro niṣādaḥ kathitaḥ śaṅkarasya tu dhaivatam || 18.20 ||
svarastu ḍhakkarī tālaṃ vāmajānūrdhvanartanam ||
anyeṣāmapi devānāṃ svaraḥ pañcama iṣyate || 18.21 ||
bhadratālaṃ tu kathitaṃ sarvamaṅgalanartanam|
bhūtānāṃ balitālaṃ syāt svaro gāndhāra ucyate || 18.22 ||
sarvamaṅgalanṛttaṃ ca darśayetkumbhasambhava|
<utsavabhramaṇe balidānakramaḥ>
tṛtīyāvaraṇāntaṃ tat parikramya yathākramam|| 18.23 ||
tattannṛttādisahitaṃ baliṃ datvā yathāvidhi|
mahāpīṭhe balidravyaṃ baliśiṣṭaṃ vinikṣipet || 18.24 ||
utsavānte hariṃ tatra prāpaṇāntaṃ yathocitam|
pūjayitvātha yānāderavaropya gurūttamaḥ || 18.25 ||
mandirāntarbhuvaṃ nītvā yathāsthānaṃ niveśayet|
śriyādīnāṃ ca devīnāṃ brahmādīnāṃ tathaiva ca || 18.26 ||
anyeṣāṃ parivārāṇāmāśritānāṃ ca pūjanam|
tattanmantraiṇa kurvīta mukhyebhyastu nivedanam || 18.27 ||
<dvārapālādibhyo balipradānādividhiḥ>
balistu dvārapālānāṃ tathā vai vidhirudrayoḥ|
indrādilokapālānāṃ sarvāvaraṇavāsinām || 18.28 ||
pīṭhādhidevatānāñca balimevaṃ vinikṣipet|
devīnāmāśritānāṃ ca tārkṣyaśeneśayorapi || 18.29 ||
āśritānāṃ ca bhaktānāṃ prāpaṇāntaṃ samarcan|
|| iti bhārgavatantre aṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<19>
|| ekonaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<jīrṇoddhāravidhinirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<jīrṇoddhārāvasaraḥ>
dhāmno vā kautukānāṃ vā vaikalye'vayavādiṣu|
sandhātuṃ yadi śakyeta tadā sandhānamācaret || 19.1 ||
<saṃdhānānarhe bimbādau vidhiḥ>
aśakye tucchite dhāmni bimbe tyaktvā punaḥ sṛjet|
pūrvadravyaṃ punaḥ sṛṣṭau yogyaṃ saṃyojya kalpayet || 19.2 ||
ayogyaṃ tucchitaṃ dhīmān agādhe nikṣipejjale|
bhinnaṃ vā sphuṭitaṃ bimbaṃ bhūtikāmo na pūjayet || 19.3 ||
<sandhānāvasare bhagavataḥ ārādhanasthalam|>
laghusādhye'ṅgasandhāne kuṃbhe śaktiṃ niveśayet|
anantajīrṇe bimbādau bālabimbe'rcayeddharim || 19.4 ||
<ajīrṇadhāmabimbatyāganiṣedhaḥ>
ajīrṇa dhāma bimbaṃ vā tyaktvā nānyatra kalpayet|
kalpane tu mahān doṣaḥ ācāryayajamānayoḥ || 19.5 ||
<vimānādijīrṇoddhārakrame śaktyāvāhanavyavasthā>
sandhāne tu vimānasya śaktimāvāhayeddhruve || 19.6 ||
dhruvaberasya sandhāne kumbhe vā bālakautuke|
vaikalye parivārāṇāṃ teṣāmāvāhanaṃ dhruve || 19.7 ||
aṅgabimbāṅgasandhāne tatrāpi parivāravat|
evaṃ sarvaṃ samālocya jīrṇoddhāraṃ samācaret || 19.8 ||
<bālālayakalpanavivekaḥ>
diśaśca vidiśaścāpi madhye mādhavasadmanaḥ|
sāvakāśe'bhirucite bālasadma prakalpayet || 19.9 ||
pañcavā bāhudaṇḍena sapta saṃkucite trikam|
caturaśraṃ manohāri kalpayet bālasadma tat || 19.10 ||
yasyāṃ diśyabhavaddvāraṃ mandire pūrvanirmite|
tasyāṃ bālālaye cāpi balajaṃ parikalpayet || 19.11 ||
<bālābimbakalpanaprakāraḥ>
lohajaṃ dārujaṃ yadvā śailajaṃ vā manoharam|
bālabimbaṃ prakapyeta yathā prācīnamandire || 19.12 ||
utsedho bālabimbasya vṛddhau pañcabhiraṅgulaiḥ|
sākaṃ hastena hānau tu viṃśatyāṅgulibhirbhavet || 19.13 ||
pūrvavatkalpayedbimbaṃ nānyathā kalaśodbhavaḥ|
<aṅgavaikalyaśāntyarthaṃ homakramaḥ>
evaṃ prakalpya medhāvī bālabimbaṃ ca mandiram || 19.14 ||
śubhe kāle tu samprati pūjayitvā dvijottamān|
āśiṣo vācayitvā ca dattvā tebhyo'pi dakṣiṇām || 19.15 ||
bhūsuraistairanujñātaḥ ācārya varayetpurā|
taṃ praṇamya yathāśāstraṃ maṇḍayitvābhipūjayet || 19.16 ||
<bālabimbasthāpanakrame ācāryavaraṇakramaḥ>
tataḥ śāstravidācāryaḥ aṅgavaikalyaśāntaye|
dūrvābhirmadhuyuktābhiḥ sahasraṃ homamācaret || 19.17 ||
vyāhṛtyā sarpiṣā paścāt `pañcopaniṣadā' śatam|
hutvā pūrṇāhutiṃ kuryāt tena śāntirna saṃśayaḥ || 19.18 ||
<bālabimbapratiṣṭhārādhanakramaḥ>
aṅkurāṇyarpayetpaścāt badhvā pratisaraṃ tataḥ|
jalādhivāsādyakhilaṃ karma kuryāt yathāpuram || 19.19 ||
bālālayasya madhye vā divye vā mānuṣe'pi vā|
hastamātronnatāṃ vediṃ kṛtvā tasyāṃ niveśayet || 19.20 ||
`mūlamantreṇa' vidhivat ratnanyāsaṃ na kārayet|
prokṣayecca yathāpūrvaṃ tato mūlālayaṃ vrajet || 19.21 ||
mūlabimbaṃ samārādhya kuṃbhaṃ nyasyāgrato hareḥ|
tatra tasmāt samāvāhya saṃhārakramamāśrayan || 19.22 ||
abhyarcya kumbhamardhyārvedavādyapurassaram|
ādāya kuṃbhamarcābhiḥ anyābhiḥ saha deśikaḥ || 19.23 ||
prādakṣiṇyena taddhāmnaḥ bālasadma praviśya ca|
karmārcādīni bimbāni tatra sthāne niveśayet || 19.24 ||
tataḥ kuṃbhagatāṃ śaktiṃ bālabimbe niyojayet|
pūrvavatsakalaṃ kuryāt sānnidhyaṃ yācayettataḥ || 19.25 ||
bhagavan punaruddhāraḥ tava dhāmnaśca varṣmaṇaḥ|
yāvatsamāpyate tāvat sānnidhyamiha ācara || 19.26 ||
iti prārthya tato devam aṅgaiḥ sākaṃ samarcayet|
vaikalyarahitān sarvān parivāram samarcayet || 19.27 ||
vikalānarpayeddevān devapādāravindayoḥ|
luptānāmapi pīṭhānāṃ parivāravadiṣyate || 19.28 ||
āśritānāṃ tu pārthakyaṃ bhaktānāṃ parivāravat|
devīnāṃ ca śriyādīnāṃ sarvāsāṃ parivāravat || 19.29 ||
nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ samācaret|
athavā nityameva syāt jīrṇoddhārasamāptaye || 19.30 ||
mahotsavaṃ prakurvīta yadyannityaṃ purātanam|
sthaṇḍile balidānaṃ syāt satāyāṃ pīṭhamastake || 19.31 ||
<jīrṇabimbaprakṣepaprakāraḥ>
evaṃ samāpya medhāvī gatvā gabhagṛhaṃ guruḥ|
haimaṃ lāṅgalamādāya mocayetpīṭhabandhanam || 19.32 ||
kuśaiḥ kāśaistṛṇaiścānyaiḥ tathā govālanirmitaḥ|
rajjubhirveṣṭayedvimbaṃ rathamāropayettataḥ || 19.33 ||
grāmaṃ pradakṣiṇīkṛtya samudraṃ vā sarovaram|
agādhaṃ cāpyaśoṣyaṃ ca salilaṃ yatra nityaśaḥ || 19.34 ||
tatra gatvā kṣipedvimbaṃ yadvā bhūmau khanedapi|
<saṃskārānantaraṃ punaḥ pratiṣṭhākramaḥ>
tataḥ snātvā vidhānena sandhāne yadi karmaṇi || 19.35 ||
sandhānāya gururdadyāt bimbaṃ devasya śārṅgiṇaḥ|
kārayitvā punaḥ sṛṣṭiṃ sandhānaṃ vā yathāvidhi || 19.36 ||
jalādhivāsādyakhilaṃ sarvaṃ karma yathāpuram|
punaḥ sṛṣṭau prakurvīta sandhāne naitadiṣyate || 19.37 ||
samprokṣaṇaṃ prakurvīta bālaśaktiṃ niveśayet|
evaṃ samprokṣya vidhivat karmabimbaiḥ samanvitam || 19.38 ||
abhyarcayedyathāpūrvaṃ bhagavantaṃ munīśvara|
|| iti bhārgavatantre ekonaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<20>
|| viṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<snapanavidhinirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<snapanabhedanirdeśaḥ>
śrīrāmaḥ
snapanaṃ devadevasya bahudhā parikīrtyate|
tatra brāhmaṃ viśeṣaṃ syāt sarvakarmasu yojayet || 20.1 ||
athavā vaiṣṇaṃva snānaṃ yadvā puṇyaṃ samācaret|
śreṣṭhamadhyakaniṣṭhāni trividhāni mahāmune || 20.2 ||
<brāhmādisnapanalakṣaṇam>
brāhmaṃ tu pañcaviṃśadbhiraṣṭonairmadhyamaṃ bhavet|
etattu vaiṣṇavaṃ proktaṃ puṇyaṃ tu navabhirghaṭaiḥ || 20.3 ||
<snapanapīṭhakalpanaprakāraḥ>
snānamaṇḍapabhūmadhye kalpayedvedikāṃ guruḥ|
pañcahastasamāyāmavistārāṃ locanapriyām || 20.4 ||
prāgagrāṇyudagagrāṇi caturdaśa nipātayet|
candanārdrāṇi sūtrāṇi koṣṭhānyekonasaptatiḥ || 20.5 ||
tatra brāhmaṃ ca daivaṃ ca mānuṣaṃ ca padatrayam|
ekāntarāntaraṃ teṣu vīthyarthaṃ parimārjayet || 20.6 ||
brāhme tu vīthikāṃ hitvā navaiva kalaśāspadam|
daive tu kalaśāḥ sthāpyā aṣṭau dikṣu vidikṣu ca || 20.7 ||
śiṣṭāni parimārjyāni mānuṣe'ṣṭau tathā bhavet|
parimārjyāni śiṣṭāni dhānyaiḥ pīṭhaṃ prakalpayet || 20.8 ||
<brāhme pade sthāpanīyāni snapanadravyāṇi>
ghṛtamuṣṇodakaṃ raṃtnaṃ phalaṃ lohaṃ yathākramam|
snānagandhākṣatayavaṃ brāhme'ṣṭau madhyataḥ kramāt || 20.9 ||
<daivapade sthāpyāni snapanadravyāṇi>
divye padāṣṭake sthāpyaṃ pādyaṃ dadhi tataḥ param|
arghyaṃ pādyaṃ ca pānīyaṃ tato madhughaṭaṃ nyaset || 20.10 ||
<mānuṣe pade sthāpyāni snapanadravyāṇi>
mānuṣe vinyaset prācyāṃ gandhodakaghaṭaṃ guruḥ || 20.11 ||
dakṣiṇasyāmikṣurasaṃ nārikelāmbu vāruṇe|
śāntitoyamudīcīne jvalane maṅgalodakam || 20.12 ||
sarvauṣadhirasaṃ kuṃbhaṃ yātudhāne niveśayet|
sarvagandhodakaṃ vāyau raudre mūlauṣadhiṃ nyaset || 20.13 ||
<ratnodakadravyaparigaṇanam>
māṇikyaṃ padmarāgaṃ ca nīlaṃ vajraṃ ca puṣyakam|
pravālaṃ mauktikaṃ caiva tathā marakataṃ kṣipet || 20.14 ||
vaiḍūryaṃ nava ratnāni nyasedratnodake ghaṭe|
<phalodakadravyaparigaṇanam>
kadalībījapūrāmradhātrībilvaphalaissaha || 20.15 ||
nālikeraṃ ca panasaṃ mātuluṅgaphalodake|
<lohodakadravyāṇi>
suvarṇaṃ rajataṃ tāmraṃ sīsakaṃ trapukaṃ tathā || 20.16 ||
kāṃsyamāyasasāraṃ ca sapta lohaghaṭodake|
pratyekaṃ niṣkamānāni vinyasetkalpatantravit || 20.17 ||
<mārjanadrayāṇi>
snānakuṃbhe nyaseddhīmān rajanīsūryavartinīm|
sahadevīṃ śirīṣaṃ ca sadābhadraṃ kuśāñcalam || 20.18 ||
mārjanāni ṣaḍetāni muṣṭimātrāṇi vai pṛthak|
<gandhodakadravyāṇi>
candanāgarukuṣṭhāni murāmāṃsī ca kuṅkumam || 20.19 ||
hraberamapyuśīraṃ ca girijaṃ ca tathā nava|
gandhavastūni tatkuṃbhe vinyasetpalamātrataḥ || 20.20 ||
<ṣaḍakṣatadravyāṇi>
nīvārāḥ vaiṇavāśśālyaḥ priyaṅguṣṣāṣṭikāstathā|
godhūmāḥ taṇḍulāścaite muṣṭimātrāḥ ṣaḍakṣatāḥ || 20.21 ||
<yavāmbudravyāṇi>
yāvaśca vrīhayaścāpi veṇavaśca ime trayaḥ|
pṛthak kuḍavamātreṇa yavāmbu kalaśe nyaset || 20.22 ||
<pādyadravyāṇi>
tulasīpadmadūrvāñca śyāmākaṃ cākṣataṃ tathā|
viṣṇukrāntaṃ bilvapatraṃ muṣṭimātraṃ pṛthak pṛthak|| 20.23 ||
palamekaṃ candanaṃ ca pādye'ṣṭau viniveśayet|
<ardhyadravyāṇi>
kuśāgrākṣatasiddhārthatilapuṣpaphalāni ca || 20.24 ||
yavāḥ pṛthaṅmuṣṭimātrāḥ palamātraṃ ca candanam|
aṣṭau dravyāṇyardhyakuṃbhe vinyaset phalitaṃ phalam || 20.25 ||
<ācāmabhājane nikṣepyadravyāṇi>
elālavaṅgatakkolajātikarpūracampakāḥ|
candanaṃ kusumānyevamaṣṭau ācāmabhājane || 20.26 ||
palārdhaparimāṇāni pratyekaṃ tatra nikṣipet|
<pañcagavyadravyāṇi>
gomūtraṃ gomayaṃ kṣīraṃ dadhisarpirimāni vai || 20.27 ||
catuṣpalāni pratyekaṃ pañcagavyaghaṭe kṣipet|
<kaṣāyodakadravyāṇi>
kaṣāyāmbughaṭe dravyāṇyetāni viniveśayet || 20.28 ||
śamīpalāśakakhadirabilvāśvatthavikaṅkatāḥ|
audumbaraśca nyagrodhasteṣāmaṣṭau tvacaḥ smṛtāḥ || 20.29 ||
<śāntikuṃbhadravyāṇi maṅgalodakadravyāṇi>
tulasīveṇunīvārayavāśca śvetasarṣapāḥ|
tilāśca ṣaṭ śāntikuṃbhe dravyāṇi parikalpayet || 20.30 ||
pratyekaṃ muṣṭimātrāṇi cūrṇitāśca tvacastathā|
indravalyaṅkurāśvatthapallavaṃ ca kuśeśayam || 20.31 ||
ekapatrāmbujaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ kuṃkumaṃ rohiṇīdrumam|
puṇyāssumanasaścāṣṭau yūthikā mallikā api || 20.32 ||
trīṇyutpalāni jātiśca campakaṃ ketakī tathā|
dravyāṇi pañcadaśa ca nyaset maṅgalapāthasi || 20.33 ||
<sarvauṣadhijaladravyāṇi>
māṃsī kuṣṭhaṃ haridre dve murā śaileyacampake|
mustā vacā ca karpūraṃ pratyekaṃ palamātrakam || 20.34 ||
viṣṭvā sarvauṣadhijale daśaitāni vinikṣipet|
<sarvagandhodakadravyāṇi>
karpūraṃ kuṃkumaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ māṃsī malayajaṃ murā || 20.35 ||
priyaṅgukesaraṃ mustā tamālaṃ nāgakesaram|
mūladvayaṃ ca kaccoraṃ surasarpikakesaram || 20.36 ||
uśīraṃ tagaraṃ lodhraṃ tathaiva haricandanam|
agarūdvāsitaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ kāleyaṃ granthipallavam || 20.37 ||
mukulaṃ campakasyeti pratyekaṃ palasammitam|
cūrṇīkṛtya nyasettatra sarvagandhodake ghaṭe || 20.38 ||
<mūlauṣadhidravyāṇi>
vyāghrī siṃhī balākāgnī śarapuṃkhā śatāvarī|
bilvamūlaṃ vacā śuṇṭhī koraṇḍaṃ śatamūlikā || 20.39 ||
pratyekaṃ palamānāḥ syuḥ piṣṭvā kuṃbhe vinikṣipet|
mūlauṣadhyaḥ imāḥ proktāḥ devasnapanakarmaṇi || 20.40 ||
<makarandādisnapanadravyaparimāṇam>
makarandadadhikṣīrapañcagavyaghṛtānyapi|
pratyekaṃ droṇamānīya yadvā śivamathāḍhakam || 20.41 ||
dravyakuṃbhāni sarvāṇi tatpramāṇāni kalpayet|
<cūrṇakuṃbhadravyāṇi>
cūrṇakuṃbhaṃ niśācūrṇaiḥ kaṇṭhadadhnaiḥ prapūrayet || 20.42 ||
<mṛttikodakadravyāṇi>
puṇyakṣetre nadītīre parvate puline hrade|
nirjhare saṅgame'śoṣye vedikorvarayostathā || 20.43 ||
śālikṣetre devakhāte vṛṣaśṛṅge ca hastinaḥ|
varāhadhṛṣṭe valmīke kulīrāvasathe tathā || 20.44 ||
nalinyāṃ dīrghikāyāṃ ca sthitāścaikonaviṃśatiḥ|
pṛthak ca muṣṭimātrābhiḥ pūrayenmṛttikodakam || 20.45 ||
<pratisarabandhaḥ śayanakramaśca>
aṅkurāṇyarpayetpūrvaṃ kuryātpratisaraṃ kare|
ekabere mūlabere bāhulye snānakautuke || 20.46 ||
śayyāyāṃ śāyayedbimbaṃ snānīyamitaradyadi|
kūrcadvāreṇa śayanaṃ prātarutthāpya deśikaḥ || 20.47 ||
<snānamaṇḍape snapanabimbasthāpanaṃ kalaśasthāpanādikaṃ ca>
dvāratoraṇakuṃbhādīn samabhyarcya yathāpuram|
dvāreṣvadhyayanaṃ nṛttaṃ gītaṃ vādyaṃ pravartayet || 20.48 ||
snapanapratimāṃ paścāt sthāpayetsnānamaṇḍape|
puṇyāhaṃ vācayetpaścāt prokṣayetkalaśānapi || 20.49 ||
snānīyānyapi vastūni kalaśasthāpanaṃ caret|
`indraṃ natve'ti mantreṇa tantubhirveṣṭanaṃ bhavet || 20.50 ||
tattatsthāneṣu kalaśān sthāpaye'nmūlavidyayā'|
`sarvamantreṇa' kuṃbheṣu dravyairambuprapūraṇam || 20.51 ||
kūrcena pallavanyāso yathānetramanoharaḥ|
`astramantreṇa' tān kuṃbhān śarāvairapidhāpayet || 20.52 ||
nārikelaphalaiḥ teṣāmalaṅkāraḥ śubhodayaḥ|
vāsobhirveṣṭayetkumbhān maṇḍayeccandanākṣataiḥ || 20.53 ||
<snapanakalaśeṣu tattaddevānāmāvahanādikramaḥ>
vedaturyādighoṣaiśca ghoṣite ca digantare|
devatāvāhanaṃ kuryāt kalaśeṣu gurūttamaḥ || 20.54 ||
ghṛtakuṃbhe vāsudevaḥ puruṣaścoṣṇavāriṇi|
phalatoye tu satyaḥ syāt snāne'cyuta udāhṛtaḥ || 20.55 ||
ananta akṣate kuṃbhe keśavo maṇivāriṇi|
lohe nārāyaṇo devo mādhavo gandhavāriṇi || 20.56 ||
yavodake tu govindaḥ pādye viṣṇuḥ prakīrtitaḥ|
madhuhantā'rdhyakalaśe ācāme tu trivikramaḥ || 20.57 ||
vāmanaḥ pañcagavye ca śrīdharo dadhibhājane|
dugdhakuṃbhe hṛṣīkeśaḥ padmanābho madhunyapi || 20.58 ||
dāmodaraḥ kaṣāyāmbhasyevaṃ kṛtvātha mānuṣe|
guḍakumbhe varāhassyānnṛsiṃhaścaikṣavodake || 20.59 ||
śrīdharo nālikerode hayāsyaḥ śāntivāriṇi|
vāsudevo maṅgalode sarvauṣadhyāṃ saṅkarṣaṇaḥ || 20.60 ||
pradyumnaḥ sarvagandhāpsu mūlauṣadhyāmaniruddhaḥ|
evaṃ brāhme tu kuṃbhānāṃ devatāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 20.61 ||
cūrṇakubhe bhavellakṣmīḥ mṛttikākalaśe mahī|
evamāvāhayeddhīmān yogapīṭhapurassaram || 20.62 ||
tattannāmacaturthyaiva namaḥ praṇavayuktayā|
aṣṭāvaraṃ samuccārya tata āvāhanaṃ caret|| 20.63 ||
arghyādi praṇavāntaṃ taṃ samabhyarcya yathāvidhi|
<agnihomamantrāṇāṃ nirdeśaḥ>
agniṃ saṃskṛtya vidhivat tatrārādhya śriyaḥ patim || 20.64 ||
`madhuvātā' `madhunaktaṃ' `madhumānno' `vanaspati'|
`vedāhaṃ' `viṣṇornukaṃ' ca `oṣadhayaḥ saṃvadante' || 20.65 ||
nārāyaṇānuvākaśca tathā `yā oṣadhīriti'|
viṣṇugāyatriyā cāpi `tadviṣṇori'tyanantaram || 20.66 ||
`na te viṣṇuri'ti śrutyā `viṣṇoḥ karmeti' mantrataḥ|
`dadhikrāvṇeti'yajuṣā `āpyāyasveti' vai ṛcā || 20.67 ||
`madhuvātā' `oṣadhayastvaṃ' `viṣṇuryā phalinī'riti|
`śannodevīśca' sāvitrī trātāramiti deśikaḥ || 20.68 ||
mahāvyāhṛtibhiḥ paścāt `gandhadvārāma'nantaram|
śatadhāraṃ vasūnāṃ ca tato ghṛtaṃ mimikṣire || 20.69 ||
sarpiṣā juhuyādetairmantraiḥ sampātamāharet|
secayeccāpi kuṃbheṣu devamagnervisarjayet || 20.70 ||
<brāhmasnapanavidhinirdeśaḥ>
ṛtvigbhiḥ sahitaḥ kuṃbhān śāntimantraiśca pañcabhiḥ|
abhimantryāstramantreṇa vastreṇācchādya deśikaḥ || 20.71 ||
dantakāṣṭhaṃ gandhatailaṃ dadyādāmalakaṃ tataḥ|
gaṇḍūṣācamane ca dve devāyārdhyādi dāpayet || 20.72 ||
pauruṣeṇaiva sūktena kalaśaiḥ snāpayeddharim|
vastrayajñopavītādi naivedyāntaṃ samarcayet || 20.73 ||
pūrvaṃ kūrcena samprokṣya paścātkuṃbhābhiṣecanam|
etadbrāhmaṃ bhavet snānam
<vaiṣṇavasnapanavidhinirdeśaḥ>
vaiṣṇavaṃ śṛṇu vakṣyate || 20.74 ||
brāhme daive ca tānkuṃbhān saṃsthāpyāvāhya pūrvavat|
vaiṣṇavenaiva sūktena juhuyāt ṣoḍaśāhutīḥ || 20.75 ||
ante ca viṣṇugāyatryā hutvā sampātasecanam|
anyat sarvaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ na viśeṣo'sti kaścana || 20.76 ||
<puṇyasnapanavidhinirdeśaḥ>
atha puṇyaṃ pravakṣyāmi snapanaṃ śārṅgadhanvanaḥ|
divyaṃ ca mānuṣaṃ tyaktvā brāhme navaghaṭāspadam || 20.77 ||
ghṛtaṃ brāhme nyaset kuṃbhaṃ prācyāṃ pādyaghaṭaṃ nyaset|
arghyakuṃbhaṃ pade yāmye ācāmāmbu ca vāruṇe || 20.78 ||
pañcagavyamudīcīne āgneye dadhibhājanam|
nairṛte tu payaḥ kuṃbhaṃ mārute madhubhājanam || 20.79 ||
kaṣāyamīśakoṇe ca dravyadevāśca pūrvavat|
adbhayaḥ sambhūta iti ca vedāhaṃ ca prajāpiḥ || 20.80 ||
yo devebhyaḥ ṛcaṃ brāhmaṃ yadvāgdevīṃ tataḥ param|
....................catvāri śraddhayeti ca || 20.81 ||
navabhirjuhuyādagnau saṃpātājyena secayet|
anyatsavaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ kathitaṃ kalaśodbhava || 20.82 ||
<haridrāmṛttikāsnapanamantranirdeśaḥ>
`śrī sūktena' bhaveddhomo haridrāsnānakarmaṇi|
havanaṃ `bhūmisūktena' mṛttikāsnapane sati || 20.83 ||
<mahāsnapanabhedāḥ>
vāsudevaṃ pārameṣṭhyaṃ prājāpatyamiti tridhā|
mahāsnapanametattu prāyaścitteṣu karmasu || 20.84 ||
<vāsudevasnapanavidhiḥ>
brāhmāṇāṃ dravyakuṃbhānāmaṣṭāvaṣṭau samantataḥ|
śuddhodakuṃbhāḥ saṃsthāpyāḥ teṣāṃ saṃkhyā śatadvayam || 20.85 ||
dravyakuṃbhārcanaṃ pūrvaṃ kathitaṃ kalaśodbhava|
sarvaśuddhodakuṃbhānāṃ devo nārāyaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ || 20.86 ||
śuddhodakeṣu sarveṣu mauktikaṃ rājataṃ tu vā|
vinyaseddhomamantrastu pūrvamevoditaṃ mune || 20.87 ||
tairhutvā sarpiṣā paścāt dvādaśākṣaravidyayā|
sahasramaṣṭasahitaṃ hutvā sampāta homayet || 20.88 ||
anyat sarvaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ kārayetkarmakovidaḥ|
etattu vāsudevākhyaṃ snapanaṃ sarvakāmadam || 20.89 ||
<pārameṣṭhyasnapanavidhiḥ>
pārameṣṭhyamatho vakṣye yathāvadavadhāraya|
vaiṣṇavānāṃ tu kuṃbhānāṃ parito'ṣṭau pṛthak pṛthak || 20.90 ||
eteṣāmudakuṃbhānāṃ ṣaṭtriṃśat sahitāḥ śatam|
pūrvavat sakalaṃ kṛtvā vaiṣṇavoktavidhānataḥ || 20.91 ||
nārāyaṇārcanaṃ kuryāt mūlenaiva ghaṭāmbuṣu|
vaiṣṇavenaiva sūktena gāyatryantaṃ yathāpuram || 20.92 ||
hutvā mūlena manunā homaścāṣṭasahasrakam|
anyat sarvaṃ yathāpūrvam prājāpatyamatha śṛṇu || 20.93 ||
<prājāpatyasnapanavidhiḥ>
puṇyasnapanavat kāryam aṣṭāvaṣṭau samantataḥ|
teṣāṃ tathākṛte'mbūnāṃ dvisaptati ghaṭāstathā || 20.94 ||
vaiṣṇavena ṣaḍarṇena viṣṇorāvāhanaṃ bhavet|
viśeṣayajanaṃ tena aṣṭottarasahasrakam || 20.95 ||
abhiṣicya dravyakuṃbhaiḥ taistaisteṣāmupodakaiḥ|
snāpayitvā maṇḍayitvā samabhyarcya yathāvidhi || 20.96 ||
praṇamya ca parikramya stutvā stotrairanekadhā|
mandirābhyantaraṃ paścāt gamayetsnānakautukam || 20.97 ||
ekaberavidhānaṃ cet snapanaṃ mūlakautuke|
<kauberasnapanavidhiḥ>
kauberaṃ snapanaṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣva kalaśodbhava || 20.98 ||
ghṛtaṃ madhye dadhyanale pīyūṣaṃ nairṛte pade|
makarandaṃ ganadhavade śarkarā śāṅkare pade || 20.99 ||
nṛsiṃhaḥ śarkarādevaḥ itareṣāṃ yathāpuram|
`brahmajajñānami'ti ca `ayāścāgneti' vidyayā || 20.100 ||
`asunvante'ti ca ṛcā `cānoniyuni' mantrataḥ|
`tamīśānami'tītyevaṃ sarvamanyat yathāpuram || 20.101 ||
<kalyāṇasnapanaprakāraḥ>
anenaiva prakāreṇa catvāriṃśacchubhodakaiḥ|
snānaṃ kalyāṇamuditaṃ vyāhṛtyāvāhanaṃ hareḥ || 20.102 ||
tayaiva juhuyādagnau aṣṭottarasahasrakam|
<aparājitasnapanasya aindrasnapanasya ca svarūpam>
ekena ghṛtakuṃbhena snānaṃ syādaparājitam || 20.103 ||
upakuṃbhāṣṭakayutamaindrametanmunīśvara|
`aparājitamantreṇa' homaḥ syādaparājite || 20.104 ||
aindre śuddhodakuṃbhānāṃ devastrailokyamohanaḥ|
homastrailokyamantreṇa aṣṭottarasahasrakam || 20.105 ||
anyatsarvaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ kartavyaṃ karmakovidaiḥ|
<snapanajalasnānādiphalaśrutiḥ>
evaṃ te snapanaṃ proktaṃ saprakāraṃ mahāmune || 20.106 ||
devasnānāmbubhiḥ snātaḥ narake na nimajjati|
tattīrthaṃ pāti yo bhaktyā punarjanma na vidyate || 20.107 ||
pādukāṃ śirasā vibhran vaikuṇṭai kiṅkaro bhavet|
cūrṇaṃ dhṛtvā tu śirasi modate divi devavat || 20.108 ||
<snapane pādukātīrthādipradānanirdeśaḥ>
pādukātīrthacūrṇāni gṛhītvā deśikottamaḥ
siṣeviṣubhyaḥ sarvebhyo bhaktebhyo'pi pradāpayet || 20.109 ||
|| iti bhārgavatantre viṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<21>
|| ekaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<pavitrāropaṇavidhinirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<karmasu jāyamānadoṣaparihāraviṣayakaḥ praśnaḥ>
agastyaḥ
bhagavan devadeveśa jāmadagnya kṛpānidhe|
nityanaimittikādīnāṃ nyūnādhikyādisaṃbhave || 21.1 ||
sarveṣāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāṃ cāturāśramyameyuṣām|
kiṃ nu tatra samādhānaṃ brūhi me bhaktavatsala || 21.2 ||
<pavitrāropaṇasya doṣaparihāropāyatvam>
śrīrāmaḥ
pavitrayajanaṃ nāma samārādhanamuttamam|
samāpya pūtāḥ te sarve galitāḥ karmaṇo narāḥ || 21.3 ||
<pavitrāropaṇārthaṃ kālanirdeśaḥ>
śrāvaṇe bhādrapadyāṃ vā māsi vāśvayuje bhavet|
śobhane tithinakṣatre vāsare śubhanandite || 21.4 ||
pavitrabhūṣaṇaṃ kuryāt kalyāṇaṃ kamalāpateḥ|
aśvinyoḥ śravaṇe puṣye rohiṇyāṃ vā punarvasau || 21.5 ||
uttaratritaye svātau mṛge maitre'ntyabhe'pi vā|
kuryāt pavitraṃ kalyāṇaṃ yathāvidhi munīśvara || 21.6 ||
<pavitropādānadravyasannāhaḥ>
tantūn brāhmaṇakanyābhirnirmitānathavā'paṇāt|
krītān vā doṣarahitān kauśeyān vā yathāvasu || 21.7 ||
ādāya kṣālayitvādbhiḥ mudgacūrṇairvimiśrayet|
śoṣayedātape samyak triguṇīkṛtya tān punaḥ || 21.8 ||
punaśca triguṇīkṛtya śaṅkoḥ sūtraṃ niveśayet|
aṅgasthalānuguṇyena pavitraṃ kārayet sudhīḥ || 21.9 ||
sūtrāṇyaṣṭottaraśataṃ sarvatra parikīrtitam|
<pavitramānaṃ granthisaṃkhyā ca>
pavitramadhivāsāya nābhyantaṃ parikalpayet || 21.10 ||
ācāryadevapadmānāṃ śiromānaṃ gurerbhavet|
dvātriṃśadgranthayasteṣām anale mekhalāsamam || 21.11 ||
kuṃbhasya karakasyāpi kaṇṭhamānamudīritam|
viṃśatissapta ca tayorgranthayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 21.12 ||
<daśapavitrāṇāṃ nāmāni lakṣaṇañca>
pavitrasaṃkhyā berāṇāṃ daśa tāni vadāmyaham|
prāmāṇyaṃ granthisaṃjñaṃ ca alaṅkārāhvayaṃ tathā || 21.13 ||
uttamādhamamadhyāni kirīṭākhyamanantaram|
śrīvatsakaustubhāhvānaṃ vanamālāhvayaṃ daśa || 21.14 ||
prāmāṇyaṃ nābhimaryādaṃ dvātriṃśadgranthisaṃyutam|
hṛdayāntaṃ granthisaṃjñaṃ granthayaḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ || 21.15 ||
alaṅkāraṃ tu jaṅghāntaṃ dvātriṃśadgranthinirmitam|
uttamaṃ jānusīmāntaṃ granthayo'ṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 21.16 ||
aśīti granthisahitamūrvantaṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet|
nābhyantaṃ granthayaḥ ṣaṣṭiḥ jaghanyasya prakīrtitam || 21.17 ||
kirīṭamānaṃ tannāmno dvātriṃśat granthayaḥ smṛtāḥ|
śrīvatsaṃ snamaryādaṃ granthayaḥ saptaviṃśatiḥ || 21.18 ||
kaustubhaṃ hṛdayāntaṃ syāt dvātriṃśadgranthisaṃyutam|
vanamālāpavitraṃ tu caraṇāntaṃ prakalpayet || 21.19 ||
anekagranthisahitaṃ svatantrāṇāmidaṃ bhavet|
<paratantrabimbānāṃ pavitramānanirdeśaḥ>
bimbānāṃ paratantrāṇāṃ devīnāṃ cottamādikam || 21.20 ||
hastamānaṃ bhavedagnergranthayo dvādaśa smṛtāḥ|
sarveṣāṃ parivārāṇāṃ dvātriṃśadgranthibhiryutam || 21.21 ||
nābhyantaṃ dvārapālānāmapi tādṛśakalpanam|
karṇikāyāmasadṛśaṃ pīṭhāntaṃ parikalpayet || 21.22 ||
anekagranthisahitaṃ gurūṇāmṛtvijāmapi|
anyeṣāmapi sarveṣāṃ nābhyantaṃ parikalpayet || 21.23 ||
<pavitranirmāṇavidhiḥ>
gandhaiḥ parimalīkuryāt dravyaiḥ karpūrikādibhiḥ|
maṇimuktāpravālādyaiḥ svarṇairvā kusumairapi || 21.24 ||
granthīnāṃ pūraṇaṃ kuryāt kuṅkumādyaiśca śobhayet|
yathāvibhavavistāraṃ pavitrāṇi prakalpayet || 21.25 ||
<pavitrotsavasampādanārthamanujñāyācanam>
aṅkurāṇyarpayodrātrau daśamyāṃ prārthayeddharim|
praṇamya ca parikramya ṛtvigbhiḥ sahito guruḥ || 21.26 ||
cāturvarṇasyāśramiṇāṃ karmavaikalyaśāntaye|
sarvadoṣavināśārthaṃ sevatāṃ ca mahotsavam || 21.27 ||
sampade sarvalokānāṃ durbhikṣotsādanāya ca|
ārogyasidhyai rugṇānāṃ prabhoriṣṭārthasiddhaye || 21.28 ||
pavitrārohaṇaṃ nāma kriyate'dya mahotsavaḥ|
anujānīhi deveśa karmasvasmānniyojaya || 21.29 ||
<pavitrādhivāsanavidhinirdeśaḥ>
prārthyaivaṃ mandirāttasmāt ācāryo maṇḍapāntare|
ṛtvigbhiḥ saha darbheṣu prāgānana upoṣitaḥ || 21.30 ||
japairdhyānena ca hareḥ rātriṃ sarvāṃ samāpayet|
ārabheta prabhātāyāmadhivāsanakarmaṇi || 21.31 ||
nyastacitto havirbhuktvā niśāyāmadhivāsayet|
pavitrabhūṣaṇasnānakautukāni dhruve bhavet || 21.32 ||
ekabere taditare sava kalyāṇakautuke|
tataḥ pratisaraṃ badhvā pūjayet dvāratoraṇān || 21.33 ||
caturvedavido viprān sthāpayecca caturdiśam|
vādyaghoṣe ca saṃghuṣṭe dhānyarāśau ca maṇḍape || 21.34 ||
prāgagreṣu ca darbheṣu pavitrāṇi niveśayet|
puṇyāhavāribhiḥ prokṣya śoṣayecchoṣaṇādibhiḥ || 21.35 ||
gandhavastubhirālipya sugandhairapi dhūpayet|
kusumāni vikīryātha veṣṭayennavavāsasā || 21.36 ||
pradarśya cakramudrāṃ ca pūjayedastravidyayā|
sūtrairīśānamārabhya maṇḍapaṃ pariveṣṭayet || 21.37 ||
ūrdhvaṃ cakramadhaḥ padmaṃ gadāṃ dvāreṣu cāṣṭasu|
dikṣu śaṃkhaṃ maṇḍapasya dhvātvā bhūtabaliṃ kṣipet || 21.38 ||
chidrayanti ca karmāṇi vighnayanti ca yāni vai|
baliṃ gṛhītvā niryāntu tāni bhūtāni vai bahiḥ || 21.39 ||
iti mantreṇa bhūtebhyo māṣānnena balirbhavet|
<pavitrotsavakrame bhagavataḥ catuḥsthānārcanam>
tataḥ saṃsnāpya deveśamupavītāntamarcayet || 21.40 ||
nirmāya maṇḍalaṃ tasmin devamāropya pūjayet|
maṇḍalaṃ ca tataḥ kuṃbhe vāsudevaṃ samarcayet|| 21.41 ||
sudarśanaṃ ca karake nivedyānnaṃ yathāvidhi|
tataḥ kuṇḍegnimādhāya mūlena samidaṣṭakam || 21.42 ||
caruṇātha `nṛsūktena' juhuyāt ṣoḍaśāhutīḥ|
sruveṇājyena cāṣṭau ca mūlamantreṇa deśikaḥ || 21.43 ||
sampātamājyaṃ saṅgṛhya pavitreṣu visarjayet|
<pavitrārohaṇaprakāraḥ>
`idaṃ viṣṇu'riti śrutyā pavitrārohaṇaṃ bhavet || 21.44 ||
kalyāṇakautuke kumbhe karake'gnau ca maṇḍale|
āropya maṅgalaiḥ sārdhaṃ mūlabimbe tathā caret || 21.45 ||
sarvārcāsu tathā katvā prāsādaṃ veṣṭayettataḥ|
pañcavarṇaistathā sūtrairnayedrātriṃ japādibhiḥ || 21.46 ||
<pavitrotsavadvitīyadinakṛtyam>
jāgareṇa tataḥ prātarmaṇḍalasthaṃ jagadgurum|
snāpayitvā samabhyarcya cakrābjaṃ vartayet panaḥ || 21.47 ||
dvārādiyajanaṃ kṛttvā pūjayeccakrapaṅkajam|
tasmin devaṃ samāropya kuṃbhaṃ ca karakaṃ tathā || 21.48 ||
dīpāntamarcayetprājñaḥ nivedya ca caturvidham|
<pavitrotsavakrame homavidhiḥ>
haviragnau ca juhuyāt `nṛsūktena' caturvidham || 21.49 ||
yavaṃ brīhiṃ ca veṇuṃ ca tilaṃ lājaṃ tathā sumam|
agaruṃ ca ghṛtaṃ paścāt samidho navamaṃ pṛthak || 21.50 ||
aṣṭottaraśataṃ homo muhūrte śobhane guruḥ|
<maṇḍalādīnāṃ pavitraiḥ bhūṣaṇaprakāraḥ>
pavitrairmaṇḍalaṃ kuṃbhaṃ karakaṃ makhakautukam || 21.51 ||
kuṇḍaṃ ca havyavāhaṃ ca bhūṣayitvābjamaṇḍalam|
pradakṣiṇīkṛtya tato mandire dhruvakautukam || 21.52 ||
pavitraiḥ samalaṅkṛtya havirantaṃ samarcayet|
berāṇyanyāni sarvāṇi paratantrāṃstathetarān || 21.53 ||
devīśca parivārāṃśca pīṭhānyapi yathākramam|
vādyavedādighoṣeśca pavitrārohaṇaṃ caret || 21.54 ||
`mūlamantre'ṇa sarveṣāṃ pavitrārohaṇaṃ matam|
ācāryo mūrtipaiḥ sārdhaṃ praviśya harimandiram || 21.55 ||
praṇamya svāñjaliṃ puṣpairāpūrya stunuyāttataḥ|
vikirettāni puṣpāṇi devapādāravindayoḥ || 21.56 ||
manuṣyārthāni sarvāṇi pavitrāṇi tathā hareḥ|
vinyasya pādayoḥ stutvā natvā svārtha samāharet || 21.57 ||
tataḥ pūjakamukhyānām ṛtvijāṃ sahakāriṇām|
<aparāhṇe bhagavantaṃ prati kṣamāprārthanam>
anyeṣāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāmitareṣāṃ ca dāpayet || 21.58 ||
devasyānanamālokya pādau spṛṣṭvā jagadguroḥ|
vijñāpayedimāṃ gāthāṃ prāñcalirvinayānvitaḥ || 21.59 ||
bhagavan devadeveśa sarvalokanamaskṛtaḥ|
saṃvatsaropacāreṣu nyūnādhikyopaśāntaye || 21.60 ||
ārādhanamidaṃ bhaktyā sahāsmābhiranuṣṭhitam|
gṛhāṇa neha kiñcicca svātantryaṃ mama mādhava || 21.61 ||
tvadājñayā kṛtaṃ sarvaṃ tvameva paramā gatiḥ|
atra nyūnātirekāṇi kṣamasva puruṣottama || 21.62 ||
iti taṃ prārthayeddevamaparāhṇe jagatpateḥ|
<utsavakrame dainandinakṛtyam mahotsavadivasaśca>
utsavaṃ kārayedbhaktyā pratyahaṃ snapanaṃ tathā || 21.63 ||
kāladvaye'pi homaśca nava vā sapta pañca vā|
trirātramekarātraṃ vā dvārādiyajanānvitam || 21.64 ||
mahotsavaṃ prakurvīta carame divase guruḥ|
<pavitrārohaṇe vaikalpikamantraḥ>
pavitrārohaṇaṃ kuryāt `puṃsūkte'nāpi vā hareḥ || 21.65 ||
<pavitrārohaṇaphalaśrutiḥ>
ityeṣa kathito brahman pavitrārohaṇo vidhiḥ|
evaṃ yaḥ kurute bhaktyā sa yāti paramāṃ gatim || 21.66 ||
|| iti bhārgavatantre ekaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<22>
|| dvāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<vividhaprāyaścittanirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<pūjāvaikalyādau niṣkṛtiviṣayakaḥ sāmānyaḥ praśnaḥ>
agastyaḥ
nityanaimittikādyānāṃ pūjānāṃ bhagavan prabho|
vaikalye vāthavā lope niṣkṛtistatra kā bhavet || 22.1 ||
<aśuddhadehena saṃpādite arcane prāyaścittam>
śrīrāmaḥ
śṛṇuṣva maitrāvaruṇe sarvaṃ vakṣye yathākramam|
karmāṇi yena pūryante yena tuṣṭo bhaveddhariḥ || 22.2 ||
asnāto'nupanīto vā tilakena vivarjitaḥ|
vasāno malinaṃ vastraṃ kliṣṭavāso'pavitrakaḥ || 22.3 ||
pralayādikramāddehamasaṃśodhya samādhinā|
mantranyāsamakṛtvā ca pūjite ca jagatprabhau || 22.4 ||
japedaṣṭākṣaraṃ mantram aṣṭottaraśataṃ budhaḥ|
<aśuddhapādādisamarcane prāyaścittam>
pādau hastāvasaṃśodhya anācamya samarcane || 22.5 ||
japaḥ pañcopaniṣadāṃ nagno muktaśiroruhaḥ|
<śvasanādipūrvakasampādite arcane prāyaścittam>
śvasan hasan vā kupyan vā svinnāṅgaḥ pralapannapi || 22.6 ||
arcayeddevadeveśaṃ śatamastramanuṃ japet|
<lomādisparśanādipūrvakabhagavadarcane prāyaścittam>
lomaloṣṭhāsthikeśādisparśanenaiva niṣkriyā|| 22.7 ||
anāplutya striyaṃ gatvā pūjayetpuraṣottamam|
snāpayetpañcagavyena pañcopaniṣadaṃ japet || 22.8 ||
<rajasvalāvastrādyasparśānantaraṃ sampādite arcane prāyaścittam>
evaṃ rajasvalāvastraṃ spṛṣṭvā devasamarcane|
vedavikrayacaṇḍālayūpaśvānarajasvalāḥ || 22.9 ||
kākapāṣaṇḍapatitapratilomakharādikān|
caityavṛkṣaṃ citiṃ sparśāyogyaṃ spṛṣṭvā samarcane || 22.10 ||
pañcavāruṇikairmantraiḥ snapanaṃ kuśavāriṇā|
kṣīreṇa snāpayeddevaṃ sakeśakusumārcane || 22.11 ||
<malamūtrādirūṣite bimbe bhagavadarcane niṣkṛtiḥ>
rūṣitairmalamūtrādyaiḥ pūjite parameśvare|
strībhirvānunītairvā bimbe spṛṣṭe'pi vācaret || 22.12 ||
kuśodakena snapanaṃ mantreṇa parameṣṭhinā|
<duṣṭānnanivedite prāyaścittam>
mlecchapulkasacaṇḍālapratilomānulomajaiḥ || 22.13 ||
aspṛśyairapi śūdrādyairācārarahitai dvijaiḥ|
śvabhiḥ sṛgālaiśca kharaiḥ spṛṣṭamannaṃ niveditam || 22.14 ||
ayājñiyaiḥ kodravādyaiḥ haviḥ kṛtvā niveditam|
pramādena ghṛtasnānaṃ snānamuṣṇāmbubhistataḥ || 22.15 ||
<atyuṣṇādihavirnivedane prāyaścittam>
atyuṣṇamatiśītaṃ vā vinā śākādibhistu vā|
havirnivedane kuryāt `pañcopaniṣadāṃ' śatam || 22.16 ||
<śavādidūṣito grāme prāyaścittam>
śavādidūṣite grāme pakvānnaṃ vinivedayet|
payasā snapanaṃ kṛtvā `pañcopaniṣadāṃ' japaḥ || 22.17 ||
<pūjāraṃbhānantaraṃ maraṇāśauce vidhiḥ>
ārabdhāyāṃ tu pūjāyāmantarā mriyate yadi|
tantreṇa sakalaṃ kṛtvā devadeve samāpayet || 22.18 ||
na snānādikriyā tatra na homo na balirmahaḥ|
naivedyamekaṃ kurvīta tattantramiti kathyate || 22.19 ||
<niveditasya anyasmai nivedane prāyaścittam>
bhūyo nivedayetpūrvam anyasmai vā niveditam|
kuśodakaiḥ snāpayitvā `mūlamantrā'yutaṃ japaḥ || 22.20 ||
<anyadevārcanaprayuktasādhanopayoge prāyaścittam>
anyadevārcitaṃ puṣpam anyadīyaṃ rathādikam|
pūjādiṣūpayuktaṃ cet gandhavastrādikaṃ tathā || 22.21 ||
kalyāṇasnapanaṃ kṛtvā `mūlamantraṃ' śataṃ japet|
<kṛmyādidūṣitapuṣpādyupayoge prāyaścittam>
kṛmikīṭādibhirduṣṭamanyadevāya nirmitam || 22.22 ||
pūjādiṣūpayuktaṃ cet dadhnā snānaṃ samācaret|
<samarpitāvaśeṣopayoga prāyaścittam>
bhuktaśeṣeṇa vā viṣṇordattaśeṣeṇa vā punaḥ || 22.23 ||
`mūlamantra'sahasra tu japedaṣṭottaraṃ budhaḥ|
<pūjāyāṃ paryuṣitodakopayoge prāyaścittam>
pūjāyāṃ snapane vāpi yadi paryuṣitodakam || 22.24||
yojayecchāntaye tasya kuśodakaghaṭāplavaḥ|
<malādiduṣṭavastuprayoge prāyaścittam>
malamūtrādiduṣṭena vastunā pūjite yadi || 22.25 ||
snāpayedvaiṣṇavaiḥ kumbhaiḥ punaḥ pūjādi kārayet|
<kūpadoṣe prāyaścittam>
śavādidūṣitāt kūpāt jalaṃ sarvaṃ samuddharet || 22.26 ||
puṇyāhaṃ vācayitvānte prokṣayejjalaśuddhaye|
<vihitopacārabhaṅge prāyaścittam>
klṛptopacārabhaṅge tu dviguṇaṃ kārayettataḥ || 22.27 ||
śāntaye'ṣṭottaraśataṃ `mūlamantraṃ'japetsudhīḥ|
<pūjāvaikalyādau prāyaścittam>
vaikalye devadevasya pūjāyāḥ dviguṇārcanam || 22.28 ||
pūjāvaikalyakālānāṃ dviguṇairvastubhiḥ punaḥ|
prāyaścittāvasāne tu pūjayetparameśvaram || 22.29 ||
<pūjākālavaikalye prāyaścittavidhiḥ>
ekāhapūjāvaikalye snapanaṃ śāntivāriṇā|
