dinamekamatikramya vaikalyañceddinatraye || 22.30 ||
maṅgalodakakuṃbhena snapanaṃ kārayetsudhīḥ|
ata ūrdhvaṃ dvādaśāhaṃ pūjāvaikalyasaṃbhave || 22.31 ||
snāpayetsarvagandhena pakṣāntaṃ vikale sati|
mūlauṣadhībhiḥ snapanaṃ māsaṃ pūjāviparyaye || 22.32 ||
brāhmeṇa kārayetsnānaṃ vatsarāntaṃ yadā bhavet|
snāpayetpārameṣṭhena vatsaradvayalaṅghane || 22.33 ||
vāsudevaṃ bhavetsnānaṃ tataḥ saṃvatsarāvadhi|
saṃprokṣaṇaṃ prakurvīta pratiṣṭhāvatsaratrayāt || 22.34 ||
prāyaścitteṣu sarveṣu kuryādbrāhmaṇabhojanam|
<aśāstrīyapūjādau prāyaścittam>
aśāstrīyeṇa pūjāṃ vā baliṃ vā homakarma vā || 22.35 ||
kurute'kṣatatoyena snapanaṃ tasya niṣkṛtiḥ|
evaṃ bahudine jāte vidhivarjitapūjane || 22.36 ||
snāpayedvāsudevena tena śāntirbhaviṣyati|
<agnidoṣe prāyaścittam>
aspṛśyasparśane vahneḥ kṛmikeśādidūṣaṇe || 22.37 ||
aṣṭottaraśataṃ homo vyāhṛtyā tena śāmyati|
caṇḍālasūtakodakyāṃśavapulkasadūṣite || 22.38 ||
ayutaṃ homa udito vyāhṛtyā havyavāhane|
<parivārārcananāśādau prāyaścittam>
parivārārcane naṣṭe bhaṅge vā balikarmaṇaḥ || 22.39 ||
tattanmantreryathākālaṃ dviguṇīkṛtya pāvake|
juhuyācchāntaye teṣāṃ samidhā vā ghṛtena vā || 22.40 ||
<balidravyabalibimbapatane prāyaścittam>
patite tu balidravye punaranyena kārayet|
śāntaye snapanaṃ kuryāt nārikelāmbhasā hareḥ || 22.41 ||
balibimbe nipatite yānādeḥ sthāpayetpunaḥ|
baliśeṣaṃ samāpyātha vaiṣṇavaṃ snapanaṃ caret || 22.42 ||
<ajñātajāticauraiḥ spṛṣṭe bimbe prāyaścittam>
ajñātajātibhiścoraiḥ spṛṣṭe bimbe jagatpateḥ|
prājāpatyaṃ prakurvīta snapanaṃ tasya śāntaye || 22.43 ||
<mṛṇmayādibimbadoṣe prāyaśttim>
mṛṇmayaṃ citralikhitaṃ kautukaṃ ca trivastukam|
caṇḍālādyairduṣitaṃ cet tyaktvā sarvaṃ punaḥ sṛjet || 22.44 ||
<aspṛśyaiḥ spṛṣṭe bimbe prāyaścittam>
bimbe pātakibhiḥ sapṛṣṭe brāhmaṃ snapanamācaret|
śūdreṇa kautuke spṛṣṭe puṇyena snāpayeddharim || 22.45 ||
sāmānyairbrāhmaṇaiḥ spṛṣṭe kautuke parameṣṭhinaḥ|
snāpanaṃ kuśatoyena tasya niṣkṛtaye bhavet || 22.46 ||
duṣṭastrībhistathā'spṛśyaiḥ kuṇḍervā golakaistu vā|
spṛṣṭe tacchāntaye kuryāt puṇyena snapanaṃ hareḥ || 22.47 ||
<vaiśyādibhiḥ garbhagehapraveśe prāyaścittam>
vaiśyaśūdrānulomādyairgarbhagehapraveśane|
strībhirvā pañcagavyena prokṣaṇaṃ tatra kalpayet || 22.48 ||
garbhagehapraveśaścet caṇḍālaiḥ pulkasādibhiḥ|
mṛdādikautukaṃ tyājyaṃ kṣālayet gomayāmbubhiḥ || 22.49 ||
prokṣayetpañcagavyena puṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ|
punaḥ sṛṣṭvā yathāpūrvaṃ sarvamanyadyathā puram || 22.50 ||
śailādīnāṃ tu bimbānāṃ sparśe brāhmaṃ samācaret|
aśaucavatpraveśe'pi snānaṃ caṇḍālavat smṛtam || 22.51 ||
<śūdrādibhiḥ sampādite karmaṇi prāyaścittam>
śūdrapulkasacaṇḍālayavanāśaucipāpinaḥ|
karmasvadhikṛtā jñānādajñānātkāmato'pi vā || 22.52 ||
kurvīran yadi karmāṇi śodhayenmandiraṃ tataḥ|
puṇyāhaṃ vācayet paścāt vedān vipraiśca ghoṣayet || 22.53 ||
snāpayedvāsudevena kuryādbrāhmaṇabhojanam|
<mandire cāṇḍālādipraveśe prāyaścittam>
caṇḍālapulkasasvānasṛgālāśaucigardabhaiḥ || 22.54 ||
dhāmāvaraṇabhūbhāge praviṣṭe niṣkṛtiṃ śṛṇu|
prokṣaṇaṃ pañcagavyena puṇyāhaṃ vācayettataḥ || 22.55 ||
sparśe tatratya bimbānāṃ parivāragaṇasya vā|
pīṭhānāṃ vā yathāpūrvaṃ kṛtvā kuryācca niṣkṛtim || 22.56 ||
<khadyotasparśādau prāyaścittam>
khadyotavarṣasaṃsparśe yātrāyāmathavāgninā|
kurvīta puṇyasnapanaṃ dagdhaścet sarvathā tataḥ || 22.57 ||
punarnirmāya vidhivat yathāpūrvaṃ samācaret|
snāpayedvāsudevena pratiṣṭhānte jagatpatim || 22.58 ||
sthāpite yāgaśālāyāṃ kalaśe'gnau pratiṣṭhite|
caṇḍālapratilomādyairaspṛśyairitarairapi || 22.59 ||
spaṣṭe tyaktvāparaṃ sthāpya mūlenāṣṭau śatāhutīḥ|
tattaddevatamantrāṇi japedaṣṭottaraṃ śatam || 22.60 ||
snapane vāpi yātrāyāṃ patite kautuke bhuvi|
punaḥ saṃsthāpya tadvimbaṃ puṇyasnapanamācaret || 22.61 ||
maṇḍūkamūṣikādyaistu sarvairmārjārakādibhiḥ|
<cāṇḍālādibhiḥkalaśādisparśe prāyaścittam>
sthāpite kalaśe spṛṣṭe naṣṭe vā taṃ parityajet || 22.62 ||
kuṃbhāntaraṃ tu saṃsthāpya tattadāvāhanaṃ caret|
taddaivatyān japenmantrān dhvajapūrve mahotsave || 22.63 ||
skhālityaṃ yadi jāyeta puṣpayāgena śāmyati|
hīnanityotsave dhāmni kāryaṃ na dhvajapūrvakam || 22.64 ||
utsavaṃ kārayeddhīmān kṛte tanniṣkṛtaye punaḥ|
snānena pārameṣṭhyena prīṇayetparameśvaram || 22.65 ||
<snapanādau bimbapatane prāyaścittam>
mahotsave vartamāne dhvaje kenāpi hetunā|
naṣṭe dhvajāntaraṃ kṛtvā bhojayedbrāhmaṇān bahūn || 22.66 ||
dhvaje tu patite bhūmau hetunā yena kena vā|
punarāropya taṃ paścāt viprebhyo bhūribhojanam || 22.67 ||
bhaṅgacchidrādiviṣaye varṇalopādisaṃbhave|
saṃdhāya ca yathāyogaṃ saṃprokṣya vidhipūrvakam || 22.68 ||
punarāropya tacchāntyai brāhmaṇebhyo'nnatarpaṇam|
caṇḍālādipraveśena yathākuṃbhāntaraṃ bhavet || 22.69 ||
<cāṇḍālādibhiḥ pālikādisparśe prāyaścittam>
tathaiva pālikādīnāṃ mṛṇmayānāṃ bhaveddhruvam|
aṅkurāṇyarpayedbhūyaḥ tacchāntyai viprabhojanam || 22.70 ||
<aṅkure aprāśastyādau prāyaścittam>
aṅkureṣvapraśasteṣu mūṣikā paśuvājibhiḥ|
bhakṣiteṣvapi tacchātyai valmīkādisamudbhave || 22.71 ||
śāntihomo vyāhṛtibhiḥ brāhmaṇānāṃ ca bhojanam|
<grāmādirahite mandire parikramaṇavidhiḥ>
grāmādirahite dhāmni dhāmanyena parikramaḥ || 22.72 ||
<mahotsave varṣādibhiḥ śaithilye prāyaścittam>
mahotsave vartamāne varṣavātānalādibhiḥ|
grāmādau śithile paścāt prāyaścittaṃ punarmahaḥ || 22.73 ||
prājāpatyavidhānena snapanaṃ niṣkṛtirbhavet|
<utsave vighne saṃjāte sati prāyaścittam>
utsave daivayogena vighnite mānuṣeṇa vā || 22.74 ||
balimeva kṣipeddhāmni grāme vīthyāṃ caturdiśam|
naṣṭakarmāṇyaśeṣāṇi punaḥ kṛtvā yathāpuram || 22.75 ||
kartavyamakhilaṃ paścāt kuryādvidhyuktavartmanā|
balipradāne'pyakṛte atikrāntaṃ purācaret || 22.76 ||
anyadyathāvata kartavyaṃ mahāvātādyupadrave|
caṇḍapracaṇḍayoreva bhūtebhyaśca tadantare || 22.77 ||
<tīrthayātrāhīne utsave prāyaścittam>
hīnāyāṃ tīrthayātrāyāṃ snapanaṃ vāsudevajam|
<utsavabimbacauryādau vidhiḥ prāyaścittañca>
naṣṭe corādibhirbimbe kuryātkarmārcayā mahaḥ || 22.78 ||
bimbe tu patite vīthyāṃ yānamāropya tatpunaḥ|
vīthīparikramaṃ kṛtvā prājāpatyamathācaret || 22.79 ||
patanenāṅgahīnaṃ cet kuryāt karmārcayā mahaḥ|
sandhāya vidhivat sarvamanyat kāryaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ || 22.80 ||
yānabhaṅge nayeddevaṃ yānenānyena kenacit|
<karmādhikṛtajane vighnite vidhiḥ>
karmaṇyadhikṛtā ye ca pratiṣṭhāyāḥ savasya vā || 22.81 ||
daivādvā mānuṣādyadvā vighnitā yadi kāraṇāt|
tāṃ kriyāṃ pūrayedanyaiḥ sadṛśairadhikāribhiḥ || 22.82 ||
<pavitrādinyūnādhikye pāyaścittam>
pavitrabhūṣaṇānāṃ vā kuṇḍānāṃ maṇḍalasya vā|
nyūnātirekaśāntyarthamastramantraṃ śataṃ japet || 22.83 ||
<jalādhivāsahīnapratiṣṭhāyāṃ prāyaścittam>
jalādhivāsarahitaṃ pratiṣṭhā kāritā yadi|
snapanaṃ pārameṣṭhyena `mūlamantraṃ' śataṃ japet || 22.84 ||
<kleśavāsāvadhyatikrānte prāyaścittam>
māsādūrdhvaṃ dvādaśābdādarvākkleśaṃ saheddhariḥ|
ata ūrdhvaṃ kleśavāsaṃ vāsudevo na manyate || 22.85 ||
kleśāvadhāvatikrānte kuryāt bālālayāntaram|
prāyaścittaṃ vāsudevaṃ snapanaṃ parikalpayet || 22.86 ||
<siddhāntānāṃ parasparasāṃkarye prāyaścittam>
siddhāntānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ saṃkaro jāyate yadi|
parasparaṃ vā tantrāṇāṃ pārameṣṭhyaṃ tu niṣkṛtiḥ || 22.87 ||
<mantrasiddhāntasvarūpam>
parivārayutā vāpi vinā vā taiḥ samarcyate|
mantrasiddhāntamityuktaṃ mūrtirekaiva nānyathā || 22.88 ||
<āgamasiddhāntasvarūpam>
vāsudevādayo vyūhāḥ parivāravivarjitāḥ|
catvāro yatra pūjyante siddhāntaṃ syāttadāgamam || 22.89 ||
<tantrasiddhāntasvarūpam>
vāsudevādibhiḥ sārdhaṃ nārāyaṇahayānanau|
viṣṇuśca narasiṃhaśca mūrtayo nava sūkaraḥ || 22.90 ||
pūjyante parivāraiśca siddhāntaṃ tantrasaṃjñitam|
<tantrāntarasiddhāntasvarūpam>
nṛsiṃhakapilakroḍahaṃsavāgīśvarādayaḥ || 22.91 ||
vaktṛbhedena pūjyante dvitryādivadanāstathā|
ekaiva mūrtistatrāpi parivārairvināpi vā || 22.92 ||
tattantrāntaramuddiṣṭaṃ tantrametaccaturvidham|
<siddhāntādīnāṃ parasparasāṃkaryaniṣedhaḥ>
siddhāntānāṃ ca tantrāṇāṃ sāṅkaryaṃ vyasanāya tat || 22.93 ||
tasmādasaṃkareṇaiva pūjayetparameśvaram|
<tantracatuṣṭayaṃ sāṃkaryadoṣaḥ ekatraikasyaiva grāhyatvañca>
vaikhānasaṃ pāñcarātraṃ bhārgavaṃ śaivamityapi || 22.94 ||
tantrāṇyamūni catvāri praśastānyarcanāvidhau|
asaṃkareṇa deveśamarcayetpuruṣottamam || 22.95 ||
sāṅkaryaṃ yadi jāyeta pratiṣṭhotsavakarmasu|
pūjāyāṃ snapanādau vā rājarāṣṭrakṣayo mahān || 22.96 ||
tasmādasaṃkareṇaiva tathaivānyādhikāriṇām|
asparśanena kurvīta karmajālaṃ yathāvidhi|| 22.97 ||
karṣaṇādipratiṣṭhāntā kriyā yena purātanī|
tenaiva punaruddhāre nānyathā parikalpayet || 22.98 ||
sā mūrtistacca tantraṃ ca sa eva sthāpakaḥ punaḥ|
punaḥ prakalpanenaiva kriyājālaviparyayaḥ || 22.99 ||
<ajñātādhikārijīrṇamandirādau vidhiḥ>
lupte purātane dhāmni pratimā vā purātanī|
na jñāyate cādhikārī punaḥ sṛṣṭiryathecchayā || 22.100 ||
<jñātādhikārimandirādau pūjālopādau vidhiḥ>
adhikārī parijñātaḥ ajīrṇaṃ dhruvakautukam|
mandiraṃ ca manohāri pūjālopaścirādabhūt || 22.101 ||
pūrvatantrānurodhena bhūyastenādhikāriṇā|
kurvīta sakalaṃ karma rāṣṭrakṣobho viparyaye || 22.102 ||
<tantrāntarānusāraṃ bhagavataḥ ārādhanāvasaraḥ>
kleśavāsaṃ gate deve grāme vā pattane'pi vā|
āmūlakarmanirvṛttirna kuryāttatra maṅgalam || 22.103 ||
kiṃ pūnardevadevasya pūjāvaikalyasaṃbhave|
tasmāt sarvātmanā tīvraṃ saṃprokṣyātha samarcayet || 22.104 ||
hetunā yena kenāpi pūjālope tu śārṅgiṇaḥ|
tantrādhikāryalābhaścet yajamānecchayārcanam || 22.105 ||
<bhagavadārādhanādau siddhāntādīnāṃ vyatikramaniṣedhaḥ>
vaikalyaśāntiṃ kṛtvātha saṃprokṣya vidhivatprabhuḥ|
tantrāntareṇa yajanaṃ tataḥ prīto bhaveddhariḥ || 22.106 ||
siddhānte vā'thavā tantre śāstre vā na vyatikramaḥ|
kāryo'nāpadi śāstrajñaiḥ bhūtikāmairviśeṣataḥ || 22.107 ||
<gṛhārcākrame pūjālope prāyaścittam>
pūjālope gṛhārcāyāḥ pañcagavyābhiṣecanam|
māsādupari ṣaṇmāsaṃ puṇyasnapanamācaret || 22.108 ||
ṣaṇmāsādvatsarādarvāk saṃprokṣaṇavidhirbhavet|
ata ūrdhvaṃ pratiṣṭhā syādgṛhārcārādhanakṣaye || 22.109 ||
tridaśādipratiṣṭhāyāṃ pūjāvaikalyasaṃbhave|
kevalaṃ pauruṣaiḥ sūktaiḥ snapanaṃ nānyadiṣyate || 22.110 ||
<sālagrāmaśilāpūjāvaikalyādau prāyaścittam>
sālagrāmaśilādīnāṃ pūjāvaikalyasaṃbhave|
aspṛśya sparśane vāpi kṣālayitvārccayejjalaiḥ || 22.111 ||
|| iti bhārgavatantre dvāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<23>
|| trayoviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<samprokṣaṇanirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<sarvatra prāyaścitteṣu sāmānyakṛtyāni>
śrīrāmaḥ
prāyaścittāni sarvāṇi kathitāni mahāmune|
teṣu sarveṣu kurvīta sāṅkuraṃ kautukaṃ purā || 23.1 ||
adhivāsaśca kartavyaḥ pratiṣṭhāyāṃ yatheritaḥ|
aparedyurbhavet prātaḥ kalaśasthāpanakramaḥ || 23.2 ||
brāhmavaiṣṇavapuṇyānāṃ vinānyatrāparājitam|
prāyaścitte dravyakuṃbhasthāpane sodakaṃ bhavet || 23.3 ||
madhye nyaseddravyakuṃbhaṃ paritaḥ śuddhavāri ca|
mauktikaṃ rajataṃ vāpi teṣu nyasyāmbupūraṇam || 23.4 ||
āvāhya devamabhyarcya pratiṣṭhāpya hutāśanam|
hutvā yathāvidhi guruḥ pūrṇāhutyavasānikam || 23.5 ||
snāpayitvātha japtvā ca bhojayitvā dvijottamān|
gurumabhyarcya natvā ca dattvā tasmai ca dakṣiṇām || 23.6 ||
ṛtvigbhyaḥ sahakāribhyo brāhmaṇebhyaśca dakṣiṇām|
vitīrya devaṃ ninayet mandirāntarbhuvaṃ guruḥ || 23.7 ||
<samprākṣaṇāvasaraḥ>
śṛṇu vakṣye'dhunā brahman saṃprokṣaṇavidhiṃ kramāt|
aṅgabhaṅgādisandhāne dhāmno vā kautukasya vā || 23.8 ||
cirāya pūjālope vā bhūgupte maraṇe'pi yā|
janane maithune dhāmni śilpispṛṣṭe'pi kautuke || 23.9 ||
evamādiṣu cānyeṣu samprokṣaṇamudāhṛtam|
<samprokṣaṇasannāhaḥ>
puṇyāhaṃ vācayetpūrvaṃ `viṣṇornukami'ti bruvam || 23.10 ||
prokṣayeddhāmabimbādīn abliṅgaiḥ kṣālayedapi|
aṅkurāṇyarpayetpaścāt badhvā pratisaraṃ tataḥ || 23.11 ||
karmāṅgasnapanaṃ kuryāt vaiṣṇavenaiva vartmanā|
śāyayecchayane devaṃ pratiṣṭhāyāmivātmavān || 23.12 ||
dvāratoraṇakuṃbhādīn yathāpūrvaṃ samarcayet|
vedikāyāṃ dhānyapīṭhe mahākuṃbhaṃ niveśayet || 23.13 ||
sūtraveṣṭitasarvāṅgaṃ saratnaṃ pratimānvitam|
kūrcāpidhānapatrāḍhyaṃ vastrayugmasamanvitam || 23.14 ||
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe pārśve tādṛśaṃ karakaṃ nyaset|
vāsudevaṃ mahākuṃbhe karake ca sudarśanam || 23.15 ||
āvāhyātha yathānyāyaṃ naivedyāntaṃ samarcayet|
<samprokṣaṇāṅgahomavidhiḥ prokṣaṇaprakāraśca>
agniṃ saṃsādhya vidhivat tatrāvāhya jagatprabhum || 23.16 ||
samidbhirājyaiścarubhistalaiścāpi pṛthak pṛthak|
aṣṭottaraśataṃ hutvā śāntihomaṃ yathāpuram || 23.17 ||
pūrṇāhutiṃ tato hutvā samuddhāsyānalasthitam|
anyāṃśca devānudvāsya devamantarniveśayet || 23.18 ||
mahākuṃbhajalenaiva prokṣayetpuruṣottamam|
kūrcena pañcopaniṣadairabliṅgairitarairapi || 23.19 ||
śāntimantrāṇi samaye paṭheyurbrāhmaṇottamāḥ|
evaṃ samprokṣya deveśaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ samarcayet || 23.20 ||
<brāhmaṇādisammānanaṃ dakṣiṇāpradānaṃ ca>
brāhmaṇān bhojayedbhaktyā deyā tāmbūladakṣiṇā|
ācāryadakṣiṇāṃ dadyāt śataniṣkaṃ tato'dhikam || 23.21 ||
|| iti bhārgavatantre trayoviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<24>
|| caturviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<aṣṭākṣaramantranirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<rāmasya māhātmyakīrtanaṃ kārtajñyajñāpanañca>
agastyaḥ
bhagavan sarvalokeśa sarvarṣigaṇasevita|
tvameva bhagavān viṣṇustvaṃ brahmā tvaṃ maheśvaraḥ || 24.1 ||
tvamādityastvamagniśca cdramāstvaṃ purandaraḥ|
vāyustvaṃ sarvabhūtātmā nāgarājo'si vāsukiḥ || 24.2 ||
anantaḥ phaṇināmindraḥ sraṣṭā tvaṃ sarvadehinām|
antaścarasi bhūtānāmādimadhyāntavarjitaḥ || 24.3 ||
puruṣastvaṃ paraṃ brahma jagadbījaṃ sanātanam|
tvāṃ vinā naiva paśyāmi parātparataraṃ prabhum || 24.4 ||
kalpānte sāgare kṣubdhe saṃhṛtya nikhilaṃ jagat|
kukṣau vahasi deveśa śeṣatvaṃ śeṣamaṇḍale || 24.5 ||
tvaṃ yāsi pakṣirājena śriyaṃ vahasi vakṣasi|
śeṣaṃ sarvaṃ jagadidaṃ śeṣī tvaṃ parameśvara || 24.6 ||
tvayādyānugṛhīto'smi kaṭākṣābhyāṃ vilokitaḥ|
pūto'smi puruṣeśāna puṇyaloke'smi kaścana || 24.7 ||
kṛtsnasya pañcarātrasya sāramagrāhiṣaṃ prabho|
<aṣṭākṣaramantrādhikāritvaviṣaye jijñāsā>
kṛpayā te dayāsindho śuśrūṣā kācidasti me || 24.8 ||
tadapyadyopadeṣṭavyaṃ rahasyamapi durlabham|
aṣṭākṣarākhyamantrasya adhikārī ca tajjape || 24.9 ||
<aṣṭākṣaramantrādhikārilakṣaṇam>
śrīrāmaḥ
samyak pṛṣṭaṃ muniśreṣṭha sarvasvaṃ saṅgraheṇa te|
upadekṣyāmi tanmantramadhikārī prapañcyate || 24.10 ||
ātmānaṃ manyate śeṣaṃ śeṣiṇaṃ parameśvaram|
anukampāsya bhūteṣu tairapyeṣo'nukampyate || 24.11 ||
nirīhaḥ sarvakāryeṣu so'hamasmiñjagatrayam|
mṛtyumapyātmano mitraṃ manyate'smādvibheti saḥ || 24.12 ||
kṣudhito vā na tṛṣitaḥ cintayānaḥ sada harim|
varṣaṃ vātaṃ ca bhānuṃ ca sahajaṃ manyate samam || 24.13 ||
kṛcchreṣvapyatisaṃtuṣṭo vinītaḥ kṣemavṛddhiṣu|
vanitāsu bhavet ṣaṇḍaḥ janmāndho doṣadarśane || 24.14 ||
paradoṣapravacane mūko nindāpraśaṃsane|
badhiro vedavidgoṣṭhyām ānandāśrupariplutaḥ || 24.15 ||
kiṅkaro haribhaktānāṃ harisevaikamānasaḥ|
adhikārī bhavedeṣaḥ grahītuṃ manumīdṛśam || 24.16 ||
<śuddhamiśrayoḥ sāmānyabhedaḥ>
śuddho miśra iti dvaidhīṃ bhajeta sa munīśvaraḥ|
ādyamekāyanaṃ vedaṃ bahmaikapratipādakam || 24.17 ||
tenaiva saṃskṛtaḥ śuddhaḥ miśrastraiyyā prakīrtitaḥ|
aṣṭākṣarastraividyānāṃ śuddhānāṃ dvādaśākṣarī || 24.18 ||
balādimantrairacchidraṃ pañcakālapravartanam|
ekāghnā niṣekādisaṃskāraśca balādibhiḥ || 24.19 ||
dvādaśākṣaravidyaiva teṣāṃ vidyā na ca trayī|
na sāvitrī na mantro'nyaḥ svādhyāyajapakarmaṇoḥ || 24.20 ||
mūleṣu nispṛhāḥ sarve `dvādaśākṣara'cintakāḥ|
śāvasūtakahīnāste kevalaṃ mokṣakāminaḥ || 24.21 ||
karṣaṇādikriyāsvete kalpyante nādhikāriṇaḥ|
ajñānādyadi tāḥ kuryuścayavante nātra śaṃsayaḥ || 24.22 ||
mantrasiddhāntaniṣṭhena dīkṣitena pratiṣṭhitam|
pūjārthamātmano bimbaṃ pratigṛhya samacayet|| 24.23 ||
bījaśaktyaṅgarahitaṃ `dvādaśākṣara'vidyayā|
kṛtvā pratiṣṭhāṃ tadbimbaṃ dāpayanmūlaśākhine || 24.24 ||
na dīkṣā naiva dehasya dahanādiviśodhanam|
nāṅganyāsādisakalaṃ neṣṭamekāyane'rdhvani || 24.25 ||
ekāyanapraviṣṭānāṃ traividyānāmapīdṛśaḥ|
bījaśaktyaṅgarahitam ṛṣicchando vivarjitam || 24.26 ||
svarūpameva mantrasya japaḥ svādhyāyakarmaṇoḥ|
<dīkṣayā yogyatotpādanaṃ mukhyā dīkṣā ca>
dīkṣayā jāyate yogyaḥ traividyaḥ devapūjane || 24.27 ||
cakrābjamaṇḍale dīkṣā tasya mukhyā prakīrtitā|
anukalpā bhaveddīkṣā kevalaṃ vahnisannidhau || 24.28 ||
dīkṣito devadevasya karṣaṇādikriyāṃ caret|
nādhikārī bhavedyastu dīkṣāvirahito dvijaḥ || 24.29 ||
tasmāddharyarcanākāṅkṣī dīkṣeta harisannidhau|
kuṃbhe ca maṇḍale cāgnau harimāvāhya pūjayet || 24.30 ||
māyāsūtraṃ ca nirbhidya pūto bhavati mānavaḥ|
netrabandhādvinirmukto dṛṣṭvā cakrābjamaṇḍalam || 24.31 ||
vikīrṇasvarṇakusumo labdhapuṇḍrāhvayo guroḥ|
adhītya sakalān mantrān aṣṭākṣarapurassarān || 24.32 ||
vidyāṃ haryarcanamayīm abhiṣikto ghaṭodakaiḥ|
arharyarcayituṃ viṣṇuṃ pratiṣṭhātuṃ yathāvidhi|| 24.33 ||
mahotsavādikaṃ kartuṃ nityanaimittikādikam|
eṣā mukhyā bhaveddīkṣā jaghanyā vakṣyate śṛṇu || 24.34 ||
<jaghanyāyāḥ dīkṣāyāḥ svarūpam>
sthaṇḍilaṃ pratimāṃ viṣṇoḥ kuṃbhamarciśca pūjayet|
athavā tu yathālābhaṃ kevalaṃ vā hutāśanam || 24.35 ||
juhuyāddhetirājena bāhumūle samaṅkitaḥ|
pāñcajanyena vā sākaṃ tato nāmādipūrvavat || 24.36 ||
labdhvā guroryathāśāstraṃ bhūyādeṣo'rcanāvidhau|
nitye naimittike kāmaye karṣaṇādiṣu karmasu || 24.37 ||
eṣā dīkṣā dvitīyā syāt pañcasaṃskāralakṣaṇā|
ime'dhikāriṇaḥ proktāḥ vāsudevārcanāvidhau || 24.38 ||
ete hyaṣṭākṣarān japtum itarān api vā manūn|
nānye'dhikāriṇastaiṣāṃ kīrtane smaraṇe'pi vā || 24.39 ||
<aṣṭākṣaramantroddhāraprakāraḥ>
aṣṭākṣaramanuṃ vakṣye sāvadhānamanāḥ śṛṇu|
ādau tu chandasāmādirvighneśastadanantaram || 24.40 ||
otadehena jīvātmā tṛtīyo varṇa īritaḥ|
viṣṇunā saha vighneśaḥ caturthaḥ kalaśodbha || 24.41 ||
viṣṇuṃ vibhran mahājvālaḥ pañcamo mārutastataḥ|
sa viṣṇuḥ sumukhaḥ ṣaṣṭhādanantara udīritaḥ || 24.42 ||
avasāne marudbhāti prāṇo bhūtabhṛtāṃ sadā|
eṣa te'ṣṭakṣaro mantro yathāvadudito mayā || 24.43 ||
<aṣṭākṣaramantrasya ṛṣyādinirdeśaḥ>
ṛṣirnārāyaṇo devo devatā cāsya kīrtitau|
chandastu devī gāyatrī bījaṃ viṣṇuḥ sabindukaḥ || 24.44 ||
tribhiḥ padaiśca bhūyo'pi ṣaḍaṅgāni mahāmune|
samaste kathito bhāvaḥ vyaste vakṣyāmi tacchṛṇu || 24.45 ||
bharadvājaḥ kauśikaśca jamadagnirudīritaḥ|
vasiṣṭhaḥ kasyapaścātriragastyaḥ ṛṣayaḥ kramāt || 24.46 ||
dvitīyādyāstārakasya parameṣṭhī ṛṣirbhavet|
chandāṃsi praṇavādīnāṃ gāyatryādīni cāñcale || 24.47 ||
virāṭ ca devatā vacmi kramādeṣāṃ dharo dhruvaḥ|
soma āpastathā'gniśca ṣaṣṭho varuṇa īritaḥ || 24.48 ||
pratyūhaśca prabhātaśca devā aṣṭau yathākramam|
nābhyādihṛdayānteṣu nyasanīyāśca bhūmiṣu || 24.49 ||
<aṣṭākṣaramantrajapāt pūrvaṃ dhyeyaṃ bhagavatsvarūpam>
caturbhujamudārāṅgaṃ śaṃkhacakradharaṃ vibhum|
kālameghapratīkāśaṃ padmapatranibhekṣaṇam || 24.50 ||
kirīṭahārakeyūrakāñcīkaṭakamaṇḍitam|
śrīvatsakaustubhoraskaṃ kuṇḍalāṅgulibhūṣaṇam || 24.51 ||
pītāmbareṇa saṃvītaṃ yajñasūtropaśobhitam|
lalāṭe cordhvatilakaṃ śvetayā mṛdumṛtsnayā || 24.52 ||
śriyā bhūmyā ca satataṃ vālavyajanavījitam|
praphullavārijamukha meghagambhīraniḥsvanam || 24.53 ||
evaṃ dhyātvā japenmantraṃ caturvargaphalapradam|
<aṣṭākṣamantropayogaḥ tasya phalaśrutiśca>
anena sakalāḥ devāḥ sādhyāḥ santi na saṃśayaḥ || 24.54 ||
kuryādanena mantreṇa prāṇāyāmaṃ dvijottamaḥ|
sarvapāpavinirmukto mahāyogī bhaveddhruvam || 24.55 ||
aṅgānivedāścatvāraḥ śāstrāṇi vividhānyapi|
sarvamaṣṭākṣare mantre svayameva pratiṣṭhitam || 24.56 ||
etajjapan sadā dhyāyan sarvapāpāt pramucyate|
kimanyairbahubhirmantraiḥ sarvametena sidhyati || 24.57 ||
|| iti bhārgavatantre caturviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<25>
|| pañcaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
<pañcakālaprakriyānirūpaṇādhyāyaḥ>
<vaiṣṇavāṇāṃ kṛte pañcakālaparatvanirdeśaḥ>
śrīrāmaḥ
traividyo dīkṣitastvevaṃ japadhyānaparāyaṇaḥ|
harinyastamanā bhūtvā pañcakālaparo bhavet || 25.1 ||
<pañcakālavibhāgakramaḥ>
prātaḥ kuryādadhigamamupādadyāttaḥ param|
ijyāṃ kurvīta tadanu svādhyāyaniratastataḥ || 25.2 ||
yogena gamayedante evaṃ kālaṃ nayeddvijaḥ|
<adhigamanādividhīnāṃ saṃkṣiptavarṇanam>
prātarutthāya śayanāddharernāmāni kīrtayet || 25.3 ||
śodhayitvā tato dehaṃ dantadhāvanapūrvakam|
avagāhyāpsu nadyāde`raghamarṣaṇamu'ccaran || 25.4 ||
vāsobhyāmatha saṃvītaścandanena mṛdāpi vā|
ūrdhvapuṇḍraṃ vahet phāle dadyādardhyaṃ vivasvate || 25.5 ||
sāvitryā triḥparikramya tarpayitvātha devatāḥ|
prāṇānāyamya saṅkalpya japedāsūryamaṇḍalam || 25.6 ||
aṣṭottarasahasraṃ vā śatamaṣṭottaraṃ tu vā|
upasthāya raviṃ dṛṣṭvā karmaśeṣaṃ samāpya ca || 25.7 ||
agnau ca hutvā deveśaṃ praṇamyāpīṭhamastakam|
ālokya triḥparikramya putradārādabhiḥ saha || 25.8 ||
sambhāryānapi sambhārān sambhāryārādhanātmakān|
snātvārādhya jagannāthaṃ yatīnapyatithīnapi || 25.9 ||
toṣayitvātha bhuñjīta guruśeṣaṃ haviḥ svayam|
tato ṛgādīn śāstrān vā paṭhedvā pāṭhayedapi || 25.10 ||
<yogaprakriyānirdeśaḥ>
snātvā sādhyaṃ samāpyārghyaṃ japtvāgniṃ paricarya ca|
praṇamya devadevaśaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣavidyayā || 25.11 ||
stutvā bhuktvā ca yuñjīta svātmānaṃ dhyānakarmaṇi|
prāṇāyāmaparo bhūyāt `mūmantre'ṇa tattvavit || 25.12 ||
nāḍīmiḍāṃ piṅgalāṃ ca śodhayecca gatāgataiḥ|
tatra tatra ca yogena kārayedvāyudhāraṇam || 25.13 ||
pṛthivīmapsu tāṃ vahnau taṃ vāyāvambare ca tam|
taṃ jīve taṃ pare brahmaṇyamale viniveśayet || 25.14 ||
rātrau niśīthe vimale snātvā tīrthe'nvahaṃ sudhīḥ|
āsane samyagāsīnaḥ vivikte'malamānasaḥ || 25.15 ||
evaṃ dhyāyet paraṃ brahma yogakarmaṇi dīkṣitaḥ|
hṛtpuṇḍarīkamadhyasthaṃ caitanyaṃ jyotiravyayam || 25.16 ||
kadambamukulākāraṃ nityatṛptaṃ nirāmayam|
anantamānandamayaṃ cinmayaṃ bhāsvaraṃ vibhum || 25.17 ||
nivātadīpasadṛśamakṛtrimamaṇiprabham|
evaṃ dhyāyannayetkālaṃ yogavidyāviśāradaḥ || 25.18 ||
<nidrāsvīkāraprakāraḥ>
yogāvasāne bhūyo'pi layakramaviparyayāt|
jīvādivasumatyantaṃ sṛṣṭvā devāya tarpayet || 25.19 ||
tataḥ śayīta khaṭvāyāṃ suvāsinyā vināpi vā|
evaṃ dināni gamayannante devaṃ hariṃ vrajet || 25.20 ||
<nidrāsvīkārābhāve vikalpaḥ aṣṭākṣaramaṃtrajapaphalañca>
athavā`ṣṭākṣaraṃ mantraṃ' yathāśakti japecchuciḥ|
mumukṣurlabhate muktiṃ muktikāmo'tulāṃ śriyam || 25.21 ||
ārogyaṃ putrasaṃpattiṃ dharaṇīṃ sasyamālinīm|
rājaśrīvasyamatulaṃ rājyalakṣmīṃ ca śāśvatīm || 25.22 ||
āyuśca jñānamatulaṃ prāvaṇyaṃ sarvakarmasu|
yadyadicchanti japtāraḥ sarvaṃ karatale bhavet || 25.23 ||
<asya śāstrasya grahaṇe adhikāriṇaḥ>
śāstrametanmuniśreṣṭha sarvaguhyaṃ sanātanam|
viṣṇubhaktāya dātavyaṃ jagatāmupakāriṇe || 25.24 ||
puṇyakṛdbhyaḥ pradātavyaṃ bhīrubhyaḥ pāpakarmaṇaḥ|
na nāstikāya dātavyaṃ nābhaktāya kadācana || 25.25 ||
gopanīyamidaṃ śāstraṃ yadetadbrahmasampuṭam|
<asya śāstrasyādhyayanaphalaśrutiḥ>
etadyaḥ paṭhate bhaktyā tasmin devaḥ prasīdati || 25.26 ||
tasya muktiḥ karatale sa pāpebhyaḥ pramucyate|
sutārthī sutamāpnoti strīkāmaḥ sudatīṃ drutam || 25.27 ||
āyuṣkāmo dīrghamāyuḥ rugṇo rogādvimucyate|
atulāṃ śriyamāpnoti lakṣmīkāmo na saṃśayaḥ || 25.28 ||
bahunātra kimuktena labhate yadyadīpsitam|
<pāñcarātropadeśānantaraṃ rāmasyāntardhyānatvam>
etadrahasyamuditaṃ prītyā tvayi munīśvara || 25.29 ||
muktastvaṃ kilviṣātsarvāt vraja sthānamiti buvan|
ālokitaśca sa mayā parameṣṭhī jagatprabhuḥ || 25.30 ||
paśyato'ntardadhe devo rāmo raktāntalocanaḥ|
sarahasyamidaṃ śāstramagrahīṣaṃ jagatprabhoḥ || 25.31 ||
oṃ namo vāsudevāya nirmalānantaśaktaye|
puṇyāya puruhūtāya puruṣāya parātmane || 25.32 ||
<viṣṇavārādhanaphalakīrtanaṃ granthopasaṃhāraśca>
agastyaḥ
anena vidhinā yūyamarcayadhvamadhokṣajam|
sarvapāpaharaṃ devaṃ jagadbījaṃ parāt param || 25.33 ||
tatparaṃ tatparaṃ tīrthaṃ tattapo maṅgalaṃ bahu|
tena sarvaṃ karatale bhavatyatra na saṃśayaḥ || 25.34 ||
yaḥ pūjayati deveśaṃ vāsudevaṃ sanātanam|
kiṃ tasya bahubhistīrthaiḥ kiṃ yajñaiḥ kiṃ vratairapi || 25.35 ||
tatsarvaṃ viṣṇuyajanāt hastaprāptaṃ bhaviṣyati|
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena vāsudevaṃ samarcayet || 25.36 ||
iti śāstraṃ mahaddivyaṃ gṛhītaṃ bhārgavānmuneḥ|
upādiśattapasvibhyaḥ agastyo bhagavān ṛṣiḥ || 25.37 ||
|| iti bhārgavatantre pañcaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ||
